### [1] [AmbiSteer: Selectively Eliciting Ambiguity-Aware Responses in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17900)
*Monica Riedler,Samuele Antonelli,Barbara Plank,Raffaella Bernardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AmbiSteer 框架，用激活引导（activation steering）结合提示与线性歧义分类器，使 VLM 在指代歧义的 VQA 输入上更恰当地选择澄清/枚举或直接回答，将适切处理比例从 59.0% 提升至 77.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 存在过度自信问题，面对视觉证据不足仍给出确定答案；VQA 中的指代歧义即典型场景——图像中多个实体均可作为问题的合理指代对象。模型需先识别歧义：歧义问题应请求澄清或枚举候选指代，无歧义问题应直接作答。现有 VLM 难以区分二者，往往默认单一解释。

Method: 构建轻量框架 AmbiSteer，作为推理期机制有选择地激发歧义感知行为。在三个 VLM 上对比三种策略：单独激活引导、提示（prompting）、以及二者结合；并进一步引入线性分类器预测输入是否存在歧义，据此对引导方向做条件化控制。

Result: 单独引导效果有限且依赖具体模型，提示总体更有效；提示与引导结合显著提升澄清与枚举行为，但也会在无歧义输入上误触发。经线性分类器条件化后，三个模型上被恰当处理的输入比例平均由 59.0% 升至 77.0%。

Conclusion: 激活引导可作为轻量的推理期手段缓解多模态不确定场景下的模型过度自信，但需与提示及输入歧义判别相结合方能奏效；该工作为低成本降低 VLM 过度自信奠定基础。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often exhibit overconfidence, providing firm answers even when visual evidence is insufficient. A prominent case is referential ambiguity in Visual Question Answering (VQA), where multiple entities in an image are plausible referents of a question. Addressing such cases requires recognizing when ambiguity is present: ambiguous questions can be handled by asking for clarification or enumerating plausible referents, while unambiguous questions should receive a direct answer. Current VLMs struggle with this distinction, often defaulting to a single interpretation. To investigate whether activation steering can serve as an inference-time mechanism to elicit such behaviors, we introduce AmbiSteer, a lightweight framework to selectively elicit ambiguity-aware behavior. Across three VLMs, we find that steering alone has limited and model-dependent effects, while prompting is generally more effective. Combining prompting and steering substantially increases clarification and enumeration behaviors, but also elicits them on unambiguous cases. AmbiSteer addresses this using a linear classifier to predict input ambiguity and condition the steering direction accordingly, increasing the proportion of inputs handled appropriately from 59.0% to 77.0% on average across models. Our work sets the ground for lightweight approaches to reduce model overconfidence in uncertain multimodal settings.

</details>


### [2] [ParseBench-2: Severity-Aware Evaluation of Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17400)
*Pierre-Loïc Doulcet,Boyang Zhang,Sebastián G. Acosta,Sacha Bron,Daniel B. Ospina,Antonio Jimeno Yepes,Nutchapol Dendumrongsup,Simon Suo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出基于"严重性策略"（severity policy）的文档解析指标构建方法：用测试场景显式表达人类对各类错误严重程度的偏好，再用编码智能体在人工审核下优化指标实现（比较规则、错误检测器、权重），并将其落地为覆盖4,317页、六大解析维度的基准 ParseBench-2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析指标存在两类失配：会对改变重要事实的输出给出高分，也会惩罚语义等价的无害变动；而人类判断这些后果比设计度量指标本身容易得多，因此需要把人类优先级直接作为指标设计的显式目标。

Method: 1) 用严重性策略规定每类错误对分数的影响，并写成带目标的测试场景；2) 以这些固定期望为优化目标，借助编码智能体迭代改进比较规则、错误检测器与权重，并保留人工审核；3) 构建 ParseBench-2：4,317页、六维度（文本、格式、表格、图表、视觉定位、表单），通过细粒度标注和针对性难例检测事实篡改、结构关系破坏与内容缺失；4) 评分取"内容恢复分"与"严重性上限"的较小值，防止大段正确掩盖严重错误。

Result: 在六个严重性测试套件上，新指标与专家判断（由场景目标表达）的一致性优于现有指标，且对良性变异的误罚率不超过3.4%；并据此对前沿视觉语言模型、专用文档解析器与开放权重系统进行了综合评测，比较总体排名、各解析能力表现及精度—成本权衡。

Conclusion: 将人类严重性偏好显式化并作为指标优化的目标，可得到更贴合专家判断、对无害差异更鲁棒的解析度量；该范式配合 ParseBench-2 为文档解析提供了更可信的评测基础。

Abstract: Document-parsing metrics can give high scores to outputs that change important facts and penalize changes that preserve meaning. People can often judge these consequences more easily than they can design a metric to measure them. We introduce a method for developing parsing metrics from severity policies that specify how each error type should affect the score. We express these policies through test scenarios that specify how a metric should behave. We then optimize the metric implementations against these fixed expectations, using coding agents to refine comparison rules, error detectors and weights under human review. This makes stated human priorities an explicit objective of metric design. We apply this method in ParseBench-2, a comprehensive document-parsing benchmark that combines broad capability coverage with metrics calibrated to error severity. It covers 4,317 pages across six dimensions of parsing quality: text, formatting, tables, charts, visual grounding, and forms. We use detailed annotations and targeted difficult cases to check for changed facts, broken structural relationships, and missing content. We take the lower of a parsing score that measures content recovery and a severity cap that penalizes detected errors, so recovering most of a page cannot hide a serious error. Across six severity test suites, our metrics more closely match the expert judgments expressed by the scenario targets than existing metrics and penalize at most 3.4% of the tested benign variations. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation spanning frontier vision-language models, specialized document parsers and open-weight systems, comparing their overall rankings, performance across parsing capabilities and tradeoffs between accuracy and cost.

</details>


### [3] [Training-Free Acceleration of Multi-View 3D Transformers via Regime-Aware Attention Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17629)
*Muhammed Izzet Sezer,Sevval Belkis Dikkaya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析多视角3D Transformer的全局注意力，发现每层承担"全局平均、跨图匹配、首图锚定"三种角色之一，据此提出免训练近似方法RAWATTA：主要用8-bit注意力，仅在少数层保留16-bit。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT等前馈多视角Transformer的全局注意力代价随图像数二次增长；借用LLM的稀疏注意力加速器在此类模型上精度下降或几乎没有加速。需要针对该模型注意力结构特点设计加速方案。

Method: 在7个数据集上分析三种多视角Transformer的全局注意力，归纳出三类层角色（全场景平均、跨多图点匹配、以第一张图为锚）。基于此提出免训练的RAWATTA：注意力以8-bit运行，仅当最坏情况校准检验要求时该层用16-bit；RAWATTA-Fast进一步在平均层和锚定层近似注意力，而匹配层与首图保留全部key；并实现自定义CUDA引擎。

Result: 12GB GPU、50张图下，RAWATTA的VGGT运行速度快于官方代码（主要来自CUDA引擎），任意数据集上位姿精度AUC@3损失不超过约1个点；RAWATTA-Fast稍快但精度更低。两者均可处理200张图，而官方代码在100张图时即显存不足。

Conclusion: 多视角Transformer的注意力可按层角色差异化近似，免训练即可在低显存下大幅提升可扩展图像数并保持位姿精度，说明角色感知的低精度与稀疏化是实用且有效的加速路径。

Abstract: Multi-view 3D transformers such as VGGT reconstruct a scene from a set of unposed images in a single forward pass, predicting camera poses, depth and 3D points, but the cost of their global attention grows quadratically with the num- ber of images. Accelerators borrowed from large language models assume that each token attends to only a few oth- ers; on these models they lose accuracy or gain little speed. We study the global attention of three multi-view transform- ers on seven datasets and find that every layer plays one of three roles: it averages over the whole scene, matches points across many images, or anchors on the first image, whose coordinates the outputs use. Building on this anal- ysis, we propose Regime-AWare ATTention Approximation (RAWATTA), a training-free method that runs attention in 8- bit precision and keeps 16-bit precision only in the layers where a worst-case calibration check requires it. Its fast set- ting, RAWATTA-Fast, also approximates attention in a few averaging and anchoring layers, while matching layers and the first image keep all their keys. On a 12 GB GPU at 50 im- ages, RAWATTA runs VGGT faster than the released code, mostly through our CUDA engine, and loses at most about one point of pose accuracy (AUC@3) on any dataset; RAWATTA-Fast is slightly faster and less accurate. Both fit 200 images, where the released code runs out of memory at 100.

</details>


### [4] [Rethinking Visual Reasoning in Text-to-Image Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17585)
*Shiye Su,Xiaohan Wang,Ludwig Schmidt,Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 奖励模型在视觉推理上表现差，偏好微调会侵蚀推理能力；作者通过"翻转配对轴"将视觉推理数据转为偏好对（同图不同prompt），混合训练实现视觉理解与偏好预测的帕累托改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型是文生图系统的核心组件（用于推理期选择与RL后训练），其前提是具备视觉理解能力（属性绑定、关系解析、计数）。然而作者发现：广泛使用的奖励模型在针对性视觉推理评测上表现差；即便以强VLM为骨干，偏好微调仍会侵蚀这些能力；持续学习基线也无法缓解视觉推理与偏好准确率之间的陡峭权衡。

Method: 数据中心的干预方法：翻转配对轴。传统人类偏好数据是"同一prompt下的成对图像"；作者将现有视觉推理数据集转换为"同一图像、不同prompt"的偏好对，并将两类数据混合训练，从而在偏好学习中注入视觉推理监督。

Result: 在视觉理解与偏好预测上均取得帕累托改进；在推理期best-of-n选择与RL后训练两种设定下，所得奖励模型在组合性强的GenEval prompt上将文生图生成分别平均提升4.0%与5.5%，并优于使用了6倍偏好对训练的HPSv3。

Conclusion: 视觉推理与偏好判断并非必然冲突，可通过重构偏好数据配对范式（同图异prompt）实现兼顾；该简单数据层面的干预即可显著提升下游生成质量并超越更大规模偏好数据训练的强基线。

Abstract: Reward models are a core component of modern text-to-image systems, serving as the objective for inference-time selection and RL-based post-training. A key prerequisite is visual understanding: to judge prompt adherence and ground aesthetic preferences, a reward model must bind attributes, resolve relations, and count reliably. We find that widely-used reward models perform poorly on targeted visual reasoning evaluations. Even when starting from strong vision-language backbones, preference fine-tuning erodes these capabilities, and continual-learning baselines fail to mitigate a steep tradeoff between visual reasoning and preference accuracy. We propose a data-centric intervention that injects visual reasoning supervision into preference learning by flipping the pairing axis. To complement conventional human preference data (paired images under a shared prompt), we convert existing visual reasoning datasets into preference pairs that share the same image but differ in prompt, and train on the mixture. This yields a Pareto improvement in both visual understanding and preference prediction. Across inference-time best-of-n selection and RL post-training, the resulting reward model improves text-to-image generation on challenging compositional prompts (GenEval) by an average of 4.0% and 5.5% respectively, outperforming HPSv3 which was trained on 6x more preference pairs.

</details>


### [5] [CoPre-MoE: Consolidating and Preserving MoE for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18417)
*Tao Hu,Ling-Xiang Zhang,Da-Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoPre-MoE：通过“共享+私有”专家架构（重叠专家整合为通用专家池、其余专家分解为共享投影基与紧凑任务核心）与冻结历史检索键+正交正则的路由设计，在多模态持续指令微调中兼顾抗遗忘与参数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态持续指令微调需顺序学习新能力且不遗忘旧任务。现有MoE方法为每任务分配独立专家以避免覆写共享参数，但导致跨任务冗余（不同任务专家学到重叠功能，如ScienceQA与TextVQA均需定位图像区域）与参数线性增长。

Method: 1）对新任务先学习任务专属MoE获得专有能力；2）评估功能重叠度，将跨任务重叠专家选择性整合进容量固定的通用专家池；3）将其余任务专属专家分解为共享投影基与紧凑任务核心，置于私有专家池；4）路由为每个专家配对可学习检索键，持续更新中历史键冻结、新增键施加正交惩罚；5）推理时依输入动态组合两池，无需测试期任务标签。

Result: 大量实验表明CoPre-MoE取得更优性能，同时显著降低参数开销。

Conclusion: 以“整合冗余+保留特异”的思路替代逐任务扩张，可在不引入遗忘的前提下抑制MoE在持续指令微调中的参数冗余。

Abstract: Multimodal continual instruction tuning requires multimodal large language models to sequentially acquire new capabilities without catastrophic forgetting. Mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures offer a promising way to mitigate task interference through expert specialization and combination. However, while existing MoE-based methods avoid overwriting shared parameters by allocating separate experts for each task, this progressive expansion incurs severe cross-task redundancy and linear parameter growth. Specifically, experts across tasks often learn overlapping functions alongside task-specific capabilities. For instance, ScienceQA and TextVQA both locate relevant image regions. To curb parameter redundancy without inducing forgetting, we propose Consolidating and Preserving MoE (CoPre-MoE). For each incoming task, CoPre-MoE first learns task-specific MoE to capture specialized capabilities. To eliminate redundancy and reduce parameter growth across tasks, we design a shared-and-private expert architecture. Specifically, we first evaluate functional overlap to selectively consolidate overlapping experts across tasks into a fixed-capacity generic expert pool. We then factorize the remaining task-specific experts into shared projection bases and compact task-specific cores within a private expert pool. To mitigate forgetting, we construct the router by pairing each expert with learnable retrieval keys, where historical keys remain frozen during continual updates while newly added keys are regularized with an orthogonality penalty. At inference, input-dependent routing dynamically combines both pools without test-time task labels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoPre-MoE achieves superior performance while substantially reducing parameter overhead.

</details>


### [6] [Photogrammetric 3D Model-Based Animal Behavior Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17881)
*Olaf Hellwich,Moritz Heine,Patrik Reiske,Sole Traverso,Katharina Hohlbaum,Henning Sprekeler,Christa Thöne-Reineke,Lars Lewejohann,Roman Bhuiyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视频行为估计可采用"先重建三维几何、后分类行为"的两阶段范式替代端到端方法，以显式暴露并纠正二维到三维逆问题的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的行为估计多采用图像序列到行为标签的端到端学习，将病态的二维到三维逆问题的不确定性隐式吸收进网络，难以诊断与修正。

Method: 提出两阶段策略：第一阶段从视频中重建身体与物体的三维几何结构，第二阶段基于该几何表示进行行为分类；以模拟的交互小鼠数据验证该策略，并扩展至真实视频。

Result: 在模拟交互小鼠场景中验证了该策略的有效性，并成功迁移至真实视频数据。

Conclusion: 将三维重建与行为分类解耦可使二维到三维逆问题的歧义显式化、具备可纠正性，是端到端方法之外有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Video-based behavior estimation is often implemented end-to-end from image sequences. We advocate a two-stage alternative: reconstruct 3D body and object geometry before classifying behavior. This makes ambiguities of the ill-posed 2D-to-3D inverse problem explicit and potentially correctable. We demonstrate the strategy with simulated interacting mice and extend it to real video.

</details>


### [7] [SceneAgent: A Multi-Agent Framework for Portable Game Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17821)
*Xingyi Li,Jiahao Yang,Yiqin Zhu,Jingwen Ye,Yang Sun,Weidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多智能体框架 SceneAgent，通过规范化 SceneContext、后端适配器与可复用技能库，把场景生成器的构建过程与经验跨游戏引擎复用，并在新基准 GameSceneBench 上取得 78.2–89.4% 的人类偏好胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 把场景生成器迁移到新游戏引擎时，仅导出最终产物不够：规划、构建工具与已积累经验都依赖原后端。因此需要一种可跨后端复用构建流程与经验的方法。

Method: 1) 规范化 SceneContext 维护演化中的世界状态；2) 后端适配器隔离坐标约定、资产绑定与导出；3) 结构化交接使新的 Builder 会话可查询持久状态而不继承完整构建记录；4) 将人类认可的轨迹蒸馏为不含资产ID的 Layout 技能与语义 Content 技能，支持检索与重组。

Result: 构建 GameSceneBench（69 条室内外提示）；在统一资产与评测协议下，相对五个基线获得 78.2–89.4% 的平局校正人类偏好得分；消融显示可复用技能与多智能体协议主要提升提示对齐与场景完整性，代价是额外运行时间；在第二引擎部署时可沿用构建协议与技能库，仅替换后端集成组件。

Conclusion: 结果支持将持久场景状态、任务局部推理与可复用经验三者分离，以实现可移植的场景构建。

Abstract: Moving a scene generator to a new game engine requires more than exporting its final output: planning, construction tools, and stored experience may also depend on the original backend. We present SceneAgent, a multi-agent framework for reusing the construction process and its experience across backends. A canonical SceneContext maintains the evolving world, while backend adapters isolate coordinate conventions, asset bindings, and export. Structured handoffs let fresh Builder sessions query this persistent state without inheriting the full construction transcript. Across generations, human-approved traces are distilled into asset-ID-free Layout skills and semantic Content skills that can be retrieved and recombined. We introduce GameSceneBench, comprising 69 indoor and outdoor prompts. Under a common asset and evaluation protocol, SceneAgent obtains human tie-adjusted preference scores of 78.2-89.4% against five baselines. Ablations show that reusable skills and the multi-agent protocol primarily improve prompt alignment and scene completeness, with additional runtime. Deployment in a second engine preserves the shared construction protocol and skill library while replacing backend integration components. Together, these results support separating persistent scene state, task-local reasoning, and reusable experience for portable scene construction.

</details>


### [8] [Can Large Language Models Optimize Pretrained Neural Network Weights? A Multi-Phase Empirical Evaluation of Gradient-Free Editing in YOLO26 Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17789)
*Mohamed Abdallah Salem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文系统评估了免梯度权重编辑（含LLM引导）对YOLO26检测器的效果，发现表观增益无法迁移到独立验证子集，且LLM引导相比随机策略无统计显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接权重编辑被视为微调预训练检测器的免梯度替代方案，但其泛化能力以及大语言模型（LLM）引导是否真正带来价值仍不明确，需要严格对照实验加以检验。

Method: 基于YOLO26与MS COCO设计五阶段实验：确定性缩放、随机扰动、差分进化、CMA-ES、结构化干预、真实本地LLM提案、失败感知引导及LLM-CMA-ES混合方法，并与匹配的随机策略作对照。开发阶段使用Ultralytics原生mAP50-95（1,000张COCO Val2017图像）优化，候选在不相交的1,500张已检验子集上评估，另单独报告COCO API AP50-95；含CMA-ES预算8→250的对比及YOLO26m五随机种子复现。

Result: CMA-ES预算从8增至250使开发集增益由+0.11升至+0.28个百分点，但选择集变化仍为负（-0.03至-0.12个百分点），且250次预算仅单种子。LLM-CMA-ES混合方法在各预算（8/25/50）下相较随机策略均无统计显著优势（p=0.612、0.369、0.694）。五种子YOLO26m复现的COCO API AP差异为+0.004（95% CI: -0.026至+0.034）。225条存档LLM提案中仅53条满足最终严格模式，早期张量级执行无法完全核验。

Conclusion: LLM介导的权重编辑在技术上可行，但所测试方法未能在开发子集之外实现一致的性能迁移，也未展现可测量的LLM引导优势；现有权重编辑范式缺乏可靠泛化证据。

Abstract: Direct weight editing offers a gradient-free alternative to fine-tuning pretrained detectors, but its generalization and the value of large language model (LLM) guidance remain uncertain. We evaluate five experimental phases using YOLO26 models and MS COCO, comparing deterministic scaling, random perturbations, differential evolution, CMA-ES, structured interventions, real local-LLM proposals, failure-aware guidance, and hybrid LLM-CMA-ES against a matched random-policy control. Optimization uses native Ultralytics mAP50-95 on 1,000 COCO Val2017 images; selected candidates are assessed on a disjoint, previously examined 1,500-image subset, with COCO API AP50-95 reported separately where available. Increasing the CMA-ES budget from 8 to 250 evaluations raised development gains from +0.11 to +0.28 percentage points, while selection changes remained negative (-0.03 to -0.12 points); the 250-evaluation result uses one seed. The hybrid LLM approach showed no statistically established advantage over random-policy guidance (p = 0.612, 0.369, and 0.694 at budgets 8, 25, and 50). Five-seed YOLO26m replication yielded a COCO API AP difference of +0.004 points (95% CI: -0.026 to +0.034). Of 225 archived LLM proposals, 53 satisfy the final strict schema; early tensor-level execution cannot be fully verified. Genuine LLM-mediated weight editing is feasible, but the tested approaches did not demonstrate consistent transfer beyond the development subset or a measurable LLM advantage.

</details>


### [9] [AI-Based Multi-View Echocardiographic Image Synthesis using Dynamic Shape Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18375)
*João Freitas,Anca Balinisteanu,Ahmed Youssef,Vitor Hugo Pereira,Jan D'hooge,Sandro Queirós*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种形状可控的多视角超声心动图序列合成框架：从动态全心网格提取多视角切面，经"GAN-扩散"无配对转换到逼真超声帧，再用掩码视频重建提升时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化超声心动图方法受限于大规模、高质量标注数据；临床数据库建设受伦理与后勤制约，合成数据成为替代方案。但现有合成方法多依赖左心室形状监督或仅针对特定标准切面，难以生成其他标准及非常规成像平面，也难以保持多心腔、多视角间的一致性。

Method: 1) 从动态全心网格中提取多视角横截面，获得形状与运动可控的几何先验；2) 两阶段AI流水线引入类超声外观：Stage 1 用无配对GAN-扩散模型将切面逐帧转换为逼真超声图像；3) 由于逐帧转换独立进行、缺乏时序约束，Stage 2 采用掩码视频重建以增强时间连贯性与心脏运动合理性。

Result: 两阶段在帧级别均达到较高的真实性；引入Stage 2后，视频级运动质量与时序一致性显著提升。

Conclusion: 该框架可从多样心脏模型生成逼真的多视角超声心动图序列，为数据增强和多视角心脏评估方法开发提供可扩展的带标注数据来源。

Abstract: Although automated methods have improved echocardiographic workflows, progress remains constrained by the need for large, well-annotated datasets. As the development of clinical databases is often limited by ethical and logistical challenges, synthetic data generation has emerged as a promising alternative. However, existing approaches commonly rely on left ventricle shape-based supervision or are tailored to specific cardiac views, limiting the generation of additional standard and unconventional imaging planes and the preservation of multi-view consistency across cardiac chambers. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework for shape-controlled synthesis of multi-view echocardiographic sequences. First, multi-view cross-sections are extracted from dynamic whole-heart meshes. Ultrasound-like appearance is then introduced through a two-stage AI-based pipeline. In Stage 1, an unpaired GAN-diffusion model translates these cross-sections into realistic echocardiographic frames. As this translation is performed independently at the frame level, Stage 2 employs masked video reconstruction to improve temporal coherence and cardiac motion. Evaluation demonstrated high frame-level realism for both stages, while the integration of Stage 2 substantially improved video-level motion quality and temporal consistency. Overall, the proposed framework enables the generation of realistic multi-view echocardiographic sequences from diverse cardiac models, providing a scalable source of annotated data for dataset augmentation and the development of multi-view cardiac assessment methods.

</details>


### [10] [EcoVGGT: Pinned-Key Inference for Efficient and Streamable Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction from Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18099)
*Hongsen Cao,Shanxin Yuan,Mona Jaber,Ahmed Sayed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EcoVGGT是一个免训练推理框架，通过固定的31帧"钉扎关键帧集"替代全帧交叉注意力，使前馈多视图Transformer在长视频上实现常数代价、高精度与实时流式重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT等前馈多视图Transformer虽能一次前向恢复相机与几何，但跨帧注意力代价随帧数二次增长，且长视频精度下降。现有解法或预算式近似全帧注意力、或按内容选键，均未抓住关键。作者假设：长视频的键可不看内容而选取，实验在VGGT与π³上验证了该假设。

Method: 提出EcoVGGT：(1) Pinned Key Set——均匀分布的31帧作为所有帧共享的键；(2) Scene Cache——pin pass将钉扎帧token缓存，其余帧以独立批次读取，精确复现以这些键的联合推理，每帧代价恒定；(3) Online Stream——录制过程中在线更新钉扎帧；(4) 利用中间6个全局块作为可复用对齐模块，提出Alignment Score（读取注意力以检测失败）与Alignment Refocusing（仅微调这6个块，3.6 GPU小时）。

Result: EcoVGGT(π³)在1,000帧ScanNet视频上以13.8倍速度匹配π³成功率；加refocusing后EcoVGGT(VGGT)在500帧上将成功率从70%提升到82%，速度8.5倍；单GPU可重建4,000帧；EcoVGGT(π³)以18.6 FPS流式处理1,000帧，在线ATE最低。

Conclusion: 内容无关的稀疏键选择足以支撑长视频几何重建，配合轻量对齐微调可同时获得高效率、高成功率与低漂移的在线流式重建能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward multi-view transformers such as VGGT recover the cameras and geometry of a video in one pass, but the cost of their cross-frame attention grows quadratically with the frame count, and their accuracy drops on long videos. Most remedies approximate attention over all frames within a budget or select a few keys by their content. We hypothesize that the keys of a long video can be chosen without looking at its content, and our experiments on VGGT and $π^3$ support it: a few dozen evenly spread frames can serve as the keys of every frame. We present EcoVGGT, a training-free inference framework whose keys form a Pinned Key Set of 31 such frames. A pin pass stores their tokens in a Scene Cache, and the other frames read it in independent batches that reproduce joint inference with these keys exactly, at a constant cost per frame. An Online Stream updates the pinned frames during recording. Six middle global blocks align the frames and form a reusable VGGT module: the Alignment Score reads their attention to flag failures, and Alignment Refocusing reduces them by fine-tuning only these blocks (3.6 GPU hours). EcoVGGT ($π^3$) matches the success rate of $π^3$ on 1,000-frame ScanNet videos at 13.8 times its speed. With refocusing, EcoVGGT (VGGT) raises the success rate of VGGT from 70% to 82% at 8.5 times its speed on 500 frames. EcoVGGT reconstructs 4,000 frames on one GPU, and EcoVGGT ($π^3$) streams 1,000 frames at 18.6 frames per second with the lowest online absolute trajectory error.

</details>


### [11] [From Images to Grounded Edits: A Real-World Benchmark for Chinese Grammatical Error Correction in Handwritten Essays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17966)
*Weiru Zhang,Yuzhong Hong,Junwei Bao,Lemao Liu,Dejing Dou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出图像到定位编辑的新任务G-CGEC，要求系统在手写作文图像上同时预测修改内容与位置，并构建真实K-12作文评测基准G-CGEC-Eval。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中文语法纠错（CGEC）多建模为文本到文本问题，适用于数字文本，但难以满足K-12教育中直接在手写作答图像上给出批改的实际需求；图像存在书写不规范等问题，亟需视觉感知与语言推理结合的方案。

Method: 定义G-CGEC任务（图像到定位编辑）；构建含细粒度标注的真实K-12作文图像基准G-CGEC-Eval；评测采用目标检测式IoU指标，并提出更宽容的基于中心距离匹配的替代指标；在单次直推、OCR与字符定位辅助组件的流水线及agentic harness设置下评测开源与闭源视觉语言模型，并进行消融与细粒度分析。

Result: 单次直推设置下即使顶级闭源模型表现也显著不佳；流水线与agentic harness带来明显提升；基于错误类型的多智能体工作流取得最佳性能，成为最强基线；任务仍远未解决，语言错误发现与字符级定位是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: G-CGEC揭示了图像场景下语法纠错的真实挑战，未来需发展更好融合视觉感知、语言推理与视觉定位的模型。

Abstract: Chinese Grammatical Error Correction (CGEC) is typically formulated as a text-to-text problem. While suitable for digital texts, this formulation falls short in real-world K-12 education, where AI-assisted corrections are expected directly on essay images, which are primarily handwritten and exhibit many irregularities. To bridge this gap, we introduce Grounded Chinese Grammatical Error Correction (G-CGEC), an image-to-grounded-edit task requiring systems to predict both corrections and their positions on essay images. We propose G-CGEC-Eval, a benchmark comprising real-world images of K-12 Chinese essays with fine-grained annotations. For evaluation, we adapt IoU-based metrics from object detection and propose a more tolerant alternative based on center-distance matching. Using this benchmark, we evaluate open-source and proprietary vision-language models under a direct single-pass setting, as well as settings with auxiliary components (OCR and character grounding) coordinated via pipelining or agentic harnessing. Results show that even top-tier proprietary models perform surprisingly poorly in the single-pass setting, while pipelining and agentic harnessing yield meaningful improvements. Notably, an error-type-based multi-agent workflow achieves the best performance, establishing it as the strongest baseline for future G-CGEC research. Through ablations and fine-grained analysis, we find the task remains far from solved, with linguistic error discovery and character-level grounding as the primary bottlenecks. This underscores the need for models that better integrate visual perception with linguistic reasoning and visual grounding.

</details>


### [12] [Creative Product Design under Brand Image Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17762)
*Takeshi Onishi,Hiroya Makino,Takahiro Yamaguchi,Hiroyuki Sakai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"品牌感知再设计生成"框架：先构建品牌形象调研报告，提炼创意方向，再嵌入"生成—批评—改写提示"的自我增强循环，在创造力与品牌可识别性之间取得更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业设计需在满足产品特定且常为隐性的约束下进行创造性视觉探索。现有文生图方法（概念混合、多智能体自我增强）能产生创意设计，但缺乏保证设计符合此类约束的机制。论文以品牌形象作为代表性设计约束来切入。

Method: 输入产品名称后，(1) 构建品牌形象调研报告，汇总产品与市场信息；(2) 从报告中推导创意方向；(3) 将该方向整合进自我增强循环，对生成图像进行批评并修订提示词。实验在5个产品类别共50个再设计任务上，与Naive T2I、IP-Adapter/IT-Blender及无约束自我增强在相同T2I主干下对比，并采用VLM-as-Judge协议评估创造力与品牌可识别性。

Result: 该框架在创造力与品牌可识别性两目标间取得更有利的权衡；在汽车、摩托车等可行设计空间相对受限的高约束类别上增益尤为明显。

Conclusion: 动态地引出并文本化品牌形象，可有效引导受品牌形象约束的创意产品设计生成。

Abstract: Industrial design requires creative visual exploration while satisfying constraints that are often product-specific and implicit. Existing text-to-image (T2I) approaches can generate creative designs through concept blending or multi-agent self-enhancement, but they provide limited mechanisms for ensuring that generated designs remain consistent with such constraints. We focus on brand image as a representative design constraint and propose a framework for brand-aware redesign generation. Given a product name, the framework constructs a brand image survey report that summarizes product and market information, derives a creative direction from the report, and integrates this direction into a self-enhancement loop in which generated images are critiqued and prompts are revised. We evaluate the framework on 50 redesign tasks across five product categories, comparing it with Naive T2I, IP-Adapter/IT-Blender, and unconstrained self-enhancement under the same T2I backbone. By a VLM-as-Judge protocol to measure creativity and brand identifiability, the proposed framework achieves a more favorable trade-off between the two objectives, with particularly clear gains in highly constrained categories such as automotive and motorcycle, where the plausible design space is relatively limited compared with that of other product categories. These results suggest that dynamically eliciting and textualizing brand image can guide creative product design generation under brand image constraints.

</details>


### [13] [V-JEPA-LM: Video-Language Models That Read Dynamics Beyond Static, Text-Aligned Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17831)
*Keuntae Kim,Dongjun Kim,Yong Suk Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 冻结的V-JEPA等世界模型视频编码器特征中蕴含视频动态信息（速度线性探针87%），但现有VLM仅学会检索字幕/问答中提及的少量属性，未真正"读取"动态。本文定位动态信息位于预测式编码器的跨视频共享子空间，并提出反事实监督使语言模型读取该子空间，1B-8B模型在11个视频基准上领先同类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型视频编码器（如V-JEPA）能从视频中学到场景变化（方向、速度、顺序、状态），但将其与基于字幕和问答对训练的语言模型拼接后，字幕/问题只命名少量属性，模型倾向于检索这些属性而非阅读视频，导致编码器承载的动态信息被浪费：同一批样本上线性探针可从冻结V-JEPA特征读出速度达87%，而VLM仅随机水平。

Method: 1）分析动态信息在冻结编码器中的位置：发现其存在于预测式编码器（V-JEPA）跨视频共享的子空间中，而非帧级、文本对齐编码器（SigLIP）的共享分量。2）构建反事实监督：通过像素变换将公开视频转为答案均衡的变体，答案由变换决定且仅在"变化"上不同，从而迫使语言模型读取动态而非检索属性。3）单阶段训练：4.53M公开样本，每帧32个视觉token，训练V-JEPA-LM（1B-8B，Qwen3与Llama主干）。并做受控的"编码器×监督"研究。

Result: V-JEPA-LM各规模在11个视频基准平均分上领先对应规模级别：4B模型得72.1，对比最佳开源4B的61.1与最佳开源8B的64.8。受控研究表明编码器决定"能读什么"，监督决定"实际读到什么"：在同等预算下，反事实监督平均提升4.5分，在自然基准的速度问题上提升38分。且仅需单阶段、32 token/帧，远少于开源视频LLM的多阶段、9M-172M样本、64-256 token/帧。

Conclusion: 世界模型编码器中的动态信息可通过反事实监督被语言模型有效利用；编码器与监督分别决定可读性与读取内容，该范式以更低数据与token成本在视频基准上取得领先。

Abstract: World-model video encoders such as V-JEPA learn how scenes change (direction, speed, order, state) from video alone. To answer questions, they are joined to a language model trained on captions and question-answer pairs. But each caption or question names only a few attributes of a clip, so the model learns to retrieve those attributes rather than to read the clip, and the dynamics the encoder carries go unused: on the same items, a linear probe reads speed from frozen V-JEPA features at 87% while the VLM answers at chance. We show where dynamics lives in a frozen encoder: in a subspace shared across videos for predictive encoders such as V-JEPA, but not as a shared component of frame-wise, text-aligned encoders such as SigLIP. We then build counterfactual supervision that makes the language model read it: pixel transformations turn public videos into answer-balanced variants whose answers are set by the transformation and differ only in the change. Trained in a single stage on 4.53M public samples with 32 visual tokens per frame, where open video LLMs use several stages, 9M to 172M samples and 64 to 256 tokens per frame on average, the resulting V-JEPA-LM models (1B to 8B, Qwen3 and Llama) lead every size class in average score across eleven video benchmarks; our 4B model scores 72.1, against 61.1 for the best open 4B and 64.8 for the best open 8B model. A controlled encoder x supervision study shows that the encoder decides what can be read and the supervision what is read: at a matched budget, counterfactual supervision adds 4.5 points on average and 38 on the speed questions of natural benchmarks. Code and models: https://github.com/kkt94/Llama-V-JEPA-LM.

</details>


### [14] [AG-JEPA: Anatomically Guided Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture for Neurodegenerative Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18097)
*Azad Singh,Ayantika Das,Amog Rao,Aiman Farooq,Anirban Porya,Ananya Shukla,Sowbhagya Suresh,Srinivasan Iyengar,Mohit Jain,Mercy Prasanna Ranjit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出解剖引导的JEPA架构AG-JEPA及其进展建模版本Prog-AG-JEPA，用于脑MRI上阿尔茨海默病早期诊断与进展预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑MRI可在认知下降明显之前揭示AD早期征象，但CN向MCI过渡阶段的病变信号微弱且局限于少数脑区；现有JEPA模型仅学习通用结构表征，未显式刻画AD受累的解剖区域。

Method: AG-JEPA在预训练中引入解剖token，与patch token交互以从与神经退行相关的脑区学习区域特异表征；在此基础上，Prog-AG-JEPA将疾病进展轨迹建模为该表征空间内以条件为约束的潜在位移（conditioned latent displacement）。

Result: 在覆盖AD诊断与进展预测的五个下游任务上评估：CN/MCI分类AUROC较最强基线提升8.4个百分点，MR-RATE数据集上分布外CN/AD分类提升7.1个百分点；进展建模方面，Prog-AG-JEPA在未来诊断与脑体积变化预测上一致优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 解剖学锚定的神经退行表征对诊断与预后均有价值，可同时提升早期检测能力与跨队列泛化性。

Abstract: Brain MRI can reveal early signs of Alzheimers disease (AD) before measurable cognitive decline becomes apparent. However, during the transition from cognitively normal (CN) to mild cognitive impairment (MCI), the disease-related signals are often subtle and confined to only a few brain regions. Existing Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) models learn general structural representations but do not explicitly capture the anatomical regions affected in AD. We propose Anatomically Guided JEPA (AG-JEPA), which introduces anatomical tokens that interact with patch tokens during pretraining to learn region-specific representations from brain regions relevant to neurodegeneration. Building on this representation space, we further propose Prog-AG-JEPA, which models disease progression trajectory as a conditioned latent displacement within this space. We evaluate AG-JEPA across five downstream tasks spanning Alzheimer's disease diagnosis and progression prediction. AG-JEPA improves diagnosis and MCI prognosis, achieving an 8.4 percentage point AUROC gain over the strongest baseline on CN/MCI classification and a 7.1 percentage point gain on out-of-distribution CN/AD classification on the MR-RATE dataset, highlighting its effectiveness across both early-stage detection and cross-cohort generalization. For disease progression modeling, Prog-AG-JEPA consistently outperforms all baselines on future diagnosis and brain volume change prediction, demonstrating the value of anatomically grounded neurodegeneration representations for diagnosis and prognosis.

</details>


### [15] [More Genes, Faster Inference: Efficient Large-Panel Spatial Transcriptomics Prediction from Histology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17899)
*Songming Zhang,Yuxiao Luo,Shifu Luo,Siran Chen,Shuquan Su,Jinyan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeneFlow是一个简洁高效的空间转录组预测框架，将流匹配生成建模与结构化多模态融合统一，可直接从组织学图像预测大规模（千基因级）空间基因表达，取得SOTA性能并大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从组织学图像预测空间转录组（ST）的方法普遍依赖复杂模型架构、额外输入与繁琐训练流程，且受限于固定的小基因面板，缺乏面向大规模基因面板的高效预测方法。

Method: 提出GeneFlow框架：先通过组织学tokenizer与基因tokenizer分别编码空间位点的组织形态学特征与跨基因交互；模型直接预测空间基因表达，而非中间速度场，同时以基于速度的流匹配目标进行训练，实现流匹配生成建模与结构化多模态融合的统一，无需沉重的生成式流水线。

Result: 在多个ST基准上达到SOTA，1000基因预测提升最高达56.8%；生成的位点表达谱具有生物学意义，同时计算成本降低数个数量级，显著提升效率与可扩展性，尤适用于临床场景。

Conclusion: GeneFlow为大规模空间基因表达预测提供了简单而有效的方案，有助于利用广泛可得的组织学图像推动下游生物学发现，代码已开源。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) prediction from histology aims to infer spatially resolved gene expression profiles directly from tissue images. This is a challenging problem that is currently often addressed with complicated model architectures, additional inputs, and complicated training procedures, while remaining limited to fixed, small gene panels. This raises a natural question: Is there an efficient method for large-panel ST prediction? Here, we present GeneFlow, a simpler yet effective framework for large-scale spatial gene expression prediction that unifies flow-matching generative modeling with structured multimodal fusion. Specifically, GeneFlow first encodes both histological morphology and cross-gene interactions in spatial spots through histology and gene tokenizers. The model directly predicts spatial gene expression, rather than an intermediate velocity field, while being trained with a velocity-based flow-matching objective. We show that, even without relying on heavy generative pipelines, GeneFlow achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple ST benchmarks, with up to 56.8% gains in 1,000-gene prediction. Extensive experiments further show that GeneFlow produces biologically meaningful spot profiles while reducing computational cost by orders of magnitude, thereby improving efficiency and scalability, particularly in clinical settings. We believe GeneFlow provides an opportunity for leveraging widely available histology images for downstream biological discovery. Code is available at: https://github.com/ZZhangsm/GeneFlow

</details>


### [16] [DySENSE: Dynamic Spatiotemporal ENergy Synthesis and Emulation with Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17878)
*Kailai Sun,Zijie Gao,Mingyi He,Can Rong,Alok Prakash,Baoshen Guo,Shenhao Wang,Jinhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DySENSE——一个多模态联合生成式AI框架，能将静态城市卫星影像与空间对齐的动态逐月建筑能耗图联合合成，解耦长期静态城市形态与短期动态能耗，用少于25%标注数据生成高保真带标注合成数据，提升下游预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市占全球能耗逾2/3、CO2排放超70%，城市能耗对2050净零目标及SDG 7/11至关重要。现有研究存在三类缺口：(1)多为对既有城市的预测性建模而非面向未来城市的生成式建模；(2)缺乏细粒度、空间对齐的逐月动态能耗数据；(3)现有生成式AI模型难以生成动态的城市功能级月度能耗。

Method: 构建多模态联合生成框架 DySENSE，将长期静态城市形态与短期动态能耗解耦。一方面以静态土地利用为条件，生成真实、多样且全新的静态城市影像及其对齐能耗图；另一方面以逐月动态与气候提示为条件，在保持城市形态不变的前提下联合生成动态月度能耗图。在纽约、华盛顿、诺福克、釜山四城开展实验。

Result: DySENSE 仅用不足25%的标注数据即可生成带标注的高保真合成能耗数据，使下游预测性能提升8% mIoU，并具备较强的能耗动态一致性。相较现有方法，预测性能提升8.7%（accuracy），预测误差降低9%–18%（CVRMSE，ASHRAE标准）。

Conclusion: DySENSE 为节能城市、峰值负荷分析、建筑性能模拟及城市/能源/建筑科学的动态需求响应提供了有效基础，并开源了数据与代码（MUSE2 数据集与 GitHub 仓库）。

Abstract: Cities account for over two-thirds of the world's energy and more than 70% of global carbon dioxide emissions. City energy is essential for achieving the 2050 net-zero target and the UN's Sustainable Development Goals 7 and 11. Although existing studies have made progress, they are limited by data and methodological gaps. Existing studies are largely predictive for existing cities rather than generative for future cities; fine-grained, aligned monthly dynamic energy data are scarce; and existing GenAI models struggle to generate dynamic urban functional monthly energy. We present DySENSE, a joint multimodal GenAI framework that synthesizes static urban satellite imagery together with spatially aligned, dynamic monthly building energy consumption maps. We decouple long-term static urban morphology from short-term dynamic energy consumption. By conditioning on static urban land use, DySENSE can generate realistic, diverse and new static urban imagery with aligned energy maps. By conditioning on dynamic monthly and climate prompts, DySENSE can jointly generate dynamic, monthly energy consumption maps while keeping the urban morphology static. Experiments across four cities (New York, Washington, Norfolk, Busan) demonstrated that DySENSE can generate annotated high-fidelity synthetic energy data using less than 25% labelled data, boosting downstream prediction performance by 8% mIoU, and achieving strong energy dynamic consistency. Compared to existing methods, DySENSE significantly improved prediction performance (8.7% accuracy) and reduced prediction error (9%-18% CVRMSE, ASHRAE). DySENSE offers an effective foundation for energy-efficient cities, peak-load analysis, building performance simulation and dynamic demand response for city, energy, and building science. The data and code: https://huggingface.co/datasets/skl24/MUSE2 and https://github.com/kailaisun/DySENSE

</details>


### [17] [Towards Seconds-level Video Representation: Quadratically Gated Feature Grid with Adaptive, Prediction-free Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17786)
*Tie-Yun Li,Hao-Ran Wang,Chao Wang,Zhiyong Yin,Yiming Xu,Zhenwei Cai,Li Li,Rongzhi Lin,Feng-Lei Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Q-GAS 通过多分辨率显式3D特征网格、SwiGLU式二次门控解码器与免预测的误差感知采样器，大幅提升隐式神经视频表征的拟合与解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式神经视频表征构建速度过慢，难以满足多数工业应用的基本实用门槛，亟需在保持表达力与质量的同时显著提速训练与编解码。

Method: 1）用多分辨率显式3D特征网格替代哈希编码，避免哈希碰撞导致的系数共享，虽存储略高但显著增强表达容量；2）采用紧凑的SwiGLU风格二次门控（QG）解码器，引入低秩局部二阶交互以改善非线性特征响应；3）训练时用全局误差图周期性刷新高误差样本池，每批次将池采样与均匀采样混合，无需辅助模型预测难样本；并将拟合与量化压缩至50秒内。

Result: 在UVG Beauty/Bosphorus/Jockey上，单张RTX PRO 6000下50秒内完成拟合与量化；混合精度解码超370 FPS，FP32约146 FPS。PyTorch实现中训练+量化比Instant-NGP基线快5.5–7.2倍，比RNeRV快19–28倍；90秒预算下在可比BPP的重叠区间内，PSNR/MS-SSIM/LPIPS优于所评估的NeRV族流程。

Conclusion: Q-GAS 以显式网格+轻量二阶门控解码+误差感知采样，在速度与率失真质量上同时超越Instant-NGP与NeRV族方法，达到实用级视频表征阈值，代码已开源。

Abstract: Implicit neural video representations are notoriously slow to build, which does not cross the basic practical threshold for most industrial applications. Here, we present Q-GAS, a coordinate-queryable video representation that couples multi-resolution explicit 3D feature grids with a compact SwiGLU-style quadratic-gating (QG) decoder and an error-aware, prediction-free sampler. The explicit grids avoid hash-collision coefficient sharing at the cost of modestly higher storage yet greatly boost the expressive capacity; the QG decoder introduces low-rank local second-order interactions to improve the nonlinear feature response. During fitting, a global error map periodically refreshes a high-error pool, and each batch mixes pool samples with uniform samples, which is free from the auxiliary model to predict difficult samples, thereby further escalating the efficiency. On UVG Beauty/Bosphorus/Jockey, Q-GAS fits and quantizes under 50 s on one RTX PRO 6000, decodes at over 370 FPS with mixed precision and about 146 FPS in FP32. In our PyTorch implementation, Q-GAS trains and quantizes 5.5-7.2 times faster than the evaluated Instant-NGP baseline and 19-28 times faster than RNeRV. Under a 90 s budget, it improves PSNR/MS-SSIM/LPIPS over the evaluated NeRV-family pipelines at comparable BPP over the measured overlap. We have shared our code at https://github.com/MillenRosen/Q-GAS for readers' free use.

</details>


### [18] [Adversarial Tracking: Attacks on Image Sensors for Object Tracking in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17505)
*Han Wu,Alex Chan,Ekin Can Erkus,Tousif Rahman,Rishad Shafik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文评估对抗攻击对TBD跟踪系统的影响，在CARLA中对YOLOv3/v4与SORT、Deep SORT、Strong SORT、OC-SORT组合测试图像特定与图像无关攻击，用MOTA、HOTA、IDF1衡量，发现YOLOv3系跟踪器HOTA从约53-56%骤降至15-17%，MOTA最差-772.60%，YOLOv4更鲁棒但HOTA仍降至25-27%，单张UAP即可显著降低跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TBD系统依赖神经网络检测器，而检测器易受对抗扰动攻击；仅用检测精度无法充分刻画攻击对下游轨迹估计与跟踪质量的影响，因此需要系统评估对抗攻击对完整跟踪流水线的影响。

Method: 在CARLA仿真环境中，对YOLOv3和YOLOv4检测器与SORT、Deep SORT、Strong SORT、OC-SORT四种跟踪器组合，分别施加图像特定（image-specific）和图像无关（image-agnostic，即通用对抗扰动UAP）攻击，并用MOTA、HOTA、IDF1三项指标评估跟踪质量。

Result: 图像特定攻击下，YOLOv3系跟踪器HOTA从53.45-56.19%降至14.85-17.03%，最差MOTA达-772.60%；YOLOv4系更鲁棒，但HOTA仍降至25.06-26.86%；单张UAP的图像无关攻击无需逐帧生成扰动即可降低跟踪精度。

Conclusion: 对抗攻击会严重破坏TBD跟踪系统的身份保持与轨迹估计，且影响远超检测精度下降所反映的程度；图像无关的通用扰动同样构成实际威胁，凸显跟踪系统需针对下游任务评估鲁棒性。

Abstract: Object tracking systems estimate the motion of surrounding vehicles by linking detected objects across video frames. In particular, Tracking-By-Detection (TBD) systems combine object detectors with motion prediction and data association to maintain object identities over time. However, neural network-based detectors are vulnerable to adversarial attacks that perturb input images, and their impact on downstream trajectory estimation is not fully characterized by detection accuracy alone. Therefore, this paper evaluates image-specific and image-agnostic attacks on YOLOv3 and YOLOv4 detectors paired with SORT, Deep SORT, Strong SORT, and OC-SORT tracking systems. The evaluation uses CARLA simulator and measures tracking quality through Multiple Object Tracking Accuracy (MOTA), Higher Order Tracking Accuracy (HOTA), and Identification F1 (IDF1). Under image-specific attacks, HOTA for YOLOv3-based trackers declines from 53.45-56.19% to 14.85-17.03%, while MOTA reaches -772.60% in the worst case. YOLOv4-based trackers exhibit greater robustness, while HOTA still declines to 25.06-26.86%. In addition, image-agnostic attacks can reduce the tracking accuracy using a single Universal Adversarial Perturbation (UAP), without generating a separate perturbation for individual frames.

</details>


### [19] [TelePhysica: Agentic Reconstruction of Physically Interactive Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18078)
*Ruiqi Shu,Xufeng Zhan,Yuanzhi Liang,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TelePhysica提出闭环框架，从单张照片重建可执行的多机制物理世界，通过视觉语言编码智能体验证网格与物理模型，再编译为统一求解器驱动有状态3D交互并对轨迹条件化的视频扩散模型进行合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接图像到视频扩散模型将像素合成与隐式运动纠缠，无法保持持久3D力学平衡、进行中途干预或反事实力控制；而单次程序化3D重建受单目深度与支撑间隙约束不足，易导致互穿或重力下崩塌。

Method: 视觉语言编码智能体在两阶段验证循环（像素对齐轮廓评判+零力重力平衡预检）中生成可分离视觉网格与声明式物理模型；验证后的世界编译为耦合刚体、关节链、共维布料壳与3D四面体弹性动力学的统一求解器，其模拟rollout通过像素对齐结构流条件化预训练视频扩散模型。

Result: 100场景单图基准上，平衡验证世界恢复率从单次合成的0%提升至81%；有限元布料相较匹配刚板代理的局部角点位移与面外褶皱大一个数量级以上；轨迹条件化使动态运动掩码重叠提升2–5倍，并在反事实力反转下保持运动方向，而直接图像到视频模型失败。

Conclusion: 闭环可执行物理重建与轨迹条件化生成显著优于单次重建和直接视频扩散，实现可控、可干预且力学一致的单图驱动3D交互与视频合成。

Abstract: Direct image-to-video diffusion models entangle pixel synthesis with implicit latent motion, precluding persistent 3D mechanical equilibrium, mid-rollout intervention, and counterfactual force control. Conversely, single-pass programmatic 3D reconstruction from an uncalibrated photograph leaves monocular depth and support clearance underconstrained, causing reconstructed scenes to interpenetrate or collapse under gravity. We present TelePhysica, a closed-loop framework that reconstructs an executable, multi-regime physical world from a single photograph to drive stateful 3D interaction and trajectory-grounded video synthesis. A vision-language coding agent synthesizes separable visual meshes and declarative physical models within a two-stage verification loop that pairs pixel-aligned silhouette critique with a zero-force gravitational equilibrium preflight. The verified world compiles into a unified solver coupling rigid bodies, articulated chains, co-dimensional cloth shells, and 3D tetrahedral elastodynamics, whose simulated rollouts condition pretrained video diffusion models through pixel-aligned structural streams. On a 100-scene single-image benchmark, closed-loop execution raises equilibrium-verified world recovery from $0\%$ in single-pass synthesis to $81\%$. Controlled constitutive ablations show over an order-of-magnitude larger localized corner displacement and out-of-plane pleating in finite-element cloth than in matched rigid-plate proxies. Conditioning video diffusion on simulated trajectories increases dynamic motion-mask overlap by $2{\times}\text{--}5{\times}$ and preserves motion direction under counterfactual force reversals where direct image-to-video models fail.

</details>


### [20] [Real-Time Diffusion Relighting as a Neural Sensor: Closing the Low-Light Domain Gap for Robotic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17438)
*Shengjie Xu,Alexander Gao,Anshul Rai,Dae Yeol Lee,Guan-Ming Su,Ming C. Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在机器人控制回路中嵌入"生成式神经传感器"，将光照视为生成先验而非信号处理问题以实现任务相关的语义恢复；并提出实时感知架构，将高延迟语义恢复与低延迟控制解耦，仅在检测置信度下降时于后台线程激活扩散模型。方法在ExDark上取得87.1% mAP的新SOTA，在CARLA闭环导航中实现100%完圈率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光与恶劣天气使基于日间图像训练的检测器失效（分布外问题）；同时现有LLIE方法面向离线处理，延迟高、难以嵌入实时控制回路，且往往以像素级保真而非任务相关语义为目标。

Method: 1）将生成式神经传感器嵌入控制回路，把光照作为生成先验进行图像到图像的扩散式语义恢复；2）提出实时感知架构，将高延迟语义恢复与低延迟机器人控制解耦：大模型在后台线程运行，仅在检测置信度下降触发"感知不确定性事件"时激活，降低计算开销。

Result: 在ExDark低光基准上达到87.1% mAP，刷新SOTA；在恶劣天气条件下检测率显著优于对比方法；在CARLA闭环自动驾驶导航任务中达到100%完圈率。

Conclusion: 验证了将高延迟生成式模型与标准机器人自主控制栈集成的可行性，提供了仿真层面的概念验证；将感知增强重定义为按需触发的生成式语义恢复，可兼顾鲁棒性与实时性。

Abstract: Low-light conditions severely degrade robotic perception: object detectors trained on daylight imagery are prone to failure under dark and adverse weather conditions that fall outside of their training distribution. Furthermore, from an efficiency standpoint, existing Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE) methods are designed for offline processing and are therefore impractical to integrate into live control loops. To address these issues, we propose to embed a Generative Neural Sensor within the control loop that treats illumination as a generative prior rather than a signal processing problem, enabling task-relevant semantic recovery. We further propose an Real-Time Perceptual Architecture that decouples high-latency semantic recovery from low-latency robotic control. Rather than blocking the control loop, our system runs a large-scale image-to-image diffusion model in a background thread, and is activated only when a drop in detection confidence signals a perceptual uncertainty event, significantly reducing computational overhead. Our method sets a new state-of-the-art performance on the ExDark low-light benchmark with 87.1% mAP, and under adverse weather conditions, significantly boosts detection rates over competing methods. On a navigation task in a closed-loop autonomous driving simulator (CARLA), we achieve a 100% lap completion rate, establishing a simulation-based proof-of-concept for integrating high-latency generative models with standard robotic control autonomy stacks.

</details>


### [21] [Zoom In, Scale Out: Metric Depth Models Mistake Digital Zoom for Moving Closer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17903)
*Rabeya Noor,Khandoker Nosiba Arifin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"数字变焦"免标注精确测试，检验单目深度估计模型的相机感知，发现主流模型把变焦误判为相机靠近（α≈0.95），根源在焦距估计的回归训练偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张照片的度量深度估计必须以正确推断拍摄相机（焦距/内参）为前提，但此前缺乏检验模型"是否真的做到了"的严格手段，需要一种无需标签、无成本的诊断方法。

Method: 数字变焦测试：对图像裁剪并按缩放因子s放大回原尺寸，场景几何与真实距离不变，正确模型应给出与s无关的深度。用α（深度随s^{-α}变化，理想0）与β（焦距随s^β变化，理想1）量化反应；在KITTI、NYUv2、DIODE共800张图像上测试Depth Anything V2、带相机预测的模型、Depth Pro、DA3-Nested，并以真实内参、真实光学变焦镜头作对照，排除模糊与重采样伪影；此外尝试无标签自一致性微调并剔除"模糊即变焦"捷径。

Result: Depth Anything V2 得α=0.95，几乎将变焦视作相机靠近；联合预测相机的模型仅部分修正（α≈0.31–0.36），因其焦距估计增长不足（β偏低）。给定真实相机时 Depth Pro 与 DA3-Nested 深度基本不变（α≈0），误差完全来自相机估计。根因：变焦图逐像素等同"深度被压缩"的普通图像，尺度线索不可区分，且回归式焦距估计器偏向训练分布。无标签、免训练地反向修正可使 Depth Pro 通过测试（α=0.04）且对普通图像几乎无损。无标签自一致性微调在去除"模糊即变焦"捷径后，将α降至KITTI 0.05、NYUv2 0.07，但需评估其在普通与未见图像上的代价。

Conclusion: 数字变焦提供了一个免费、精确、免标注的相机感知诊断基准，揭示度量深度模型的焦距估计是主要失效点；该误差可通过无需训练的修正显著缓解，或通过去捷径的无标签微调进一步改善，但后者存在泛化代价。

Abstract: Many new AI models estimate how far away things are, in metres, from a single photo. To do this they must also work out what camera took the photo. We check whether they really do with a simple test: digital zoom, that is, cropping a photo and enlarging the crop back to full size. Zooming moves nothing in the scene, so a correct model should give the same distance for every point still in the picture. The test is free, exact and needs no labels. Two numbers describe a model's reaction: its depth changes like $s^{-α}$ (ideal $α=0$) and its focal-length estimate like $s^β$ (ideal $β=1$), for zoom factor $s$. On 800 images from KITTI, NYUv2 and DIODE, the widely used Depth Anything V2 has $α=0.95$: it treats a zoomed photo almost as if the camera had moved closer, because it judges distance mainly from how large things look. Models that also predict the camera remove only part of this error ($α\approx0.31$-$0.36$), because their focal estimate grows too little. Given the true camera, Depth Pro and DA3-Nested keep their depth ($α\approx0$), so the error lies in their camera estimate. The reason: a zoomed photo is, pixel for pixel, a normal photo of a scene squashed in depth, so size alone cannot tell the two apart, and a focal estimator trained by regression stays close to the cameras it saw in training. Undoing this needs no labels or training and makes Depth Pro pass the test ($α=0.04$) at almost no cost on normal photos. Controls, including a real optical zoom lens, rule out blur and resizing artefacts. Finally, label-free fine-tuning for self-consistency lowers $α$ to 0.05 on KITTI and 0.07 on NYUv2 once a "blurry means zoomed" shortcut is removed; we report what this costs on normal and unseen photos.

</details>


### [22] [Efficient Genotype Classification from 3D MRI in ADPKD via Density-Aware Point Sampling and E(3)-invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17758)
*Kwanghee Lee,Hyeongsub Lee,Youngwoo Kim,Hyunji Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出E3PN，基于点采样与E(3)等变性的高效3D MRI多囊肾病(ADPKD)基因型五分类网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADPKD中如截短型PKD1突变等基因型进展快，早期识别关键；MRI基因型推断可作为基因检测的低成本补充。但腹部MRI体数据过大、ADPKD标注数据不足，使面向小图像（如脑MRI）的深度学习方法难以适用。

Method: 采用3D MRI点表示替代体素表示以降低显存；在每个图像的两个肾脏上按混合分布采样3D点以进一步节省内存；通过坐标规范化(coordinate canonicalization)引入E(3)不变性以提升数据效率。

Result: 相比3D ResNet，训练快163倍、显存少103倍；F1由基线0.165提升至0.226；建立首个基于3D MRI的五分类ADPKD基因型基准。

Conclusion: 点采样与坐标规范化带来两个数量级的效率提升，并伴随适度的F1稳定改进。

Abstract: Early detection of genetic kidney diseases, especially autosomal dominant polycystic kidney disease (ADPKD), is crucial because certain genotypes such as truncating PKD1 mutations cause rapid kidney growth and early progression to end-stage kidney disease. MRI-based genotype inference is a potential low-cost complement to genetic testing. However, although many deep learning methods have been developed for classifying various MR images, applying them to ADPKD remains challenging: (1) abdominal MR images are generally too large to be handled by deep learning models designed for smaller images like brain MRI, and (2) the amount of labeled data for ADPKD genetic mutations is insufficient to train deep learning models. In order to address these challenges, we propose E3PN, an efficient 3D MRI-based ADPKD genotype classification network that relies on point sampling and E(3)-invariance. We utilize a point representation of 3D MRI, which consumes less GPU memory than a voxel representation. For further memory efficiency, 3D points are sampled from a mixture distribution over the two kidneys in each image. Since E(3)-invariance has been shown to improve data efficiency, we also incorporate it into our model through coordinate canonicalization. On our ADPKD dataset, E3PN trains 163 times faster and uses 103x less GPU memory than a 3D ResNet, while also improving F1 from 0.165 to 0.226 over both baselines. These results establish a first benchmark for five-way ADPKD genotype classification from 3D MRI and show that point sampling and coordinate canonicalization yield two-orders-of-magnitude efficiency gains with a modest but consistent improvement in F1.

</details>


### [23] [Open-World Scene Deocclusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17695)
*K. M. S. J. Kumarasinghe,Neil A. Dodgson,Fang-Lue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的开放世界场景去遮挡框架，将单张RGB图像分解为按深度排序的RGBA图层，恢复被遮挡物体的完整外观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张图像恢复被遮挡物体的完整外观是场景理解与图像编辑的基础问题。真实场景存在杂物、复杂空间遮挡和类别多样性；现有方法在遮挡涉及背景表面或未被显式检测的物体时失效，且难以处理自然场景中常见的循环遮挡依赖关系。

Method: 免训练（training-free）、开放世界（open-world）框架：对检测到或用户指定的物体，从单张RGB图像恢复其完整的amodal外观，并将场景分解为按深度排序的RGBA图层，要求图层几何合理、语义忠实且与可见场景视觉一致。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法提升了场景级去遮挡效果，在真实场景中产生连贯且逼真的重建结果。

Conclusion: 通过深度排序RGBA分层分解，可有效解决循环遮挡依赖与未显式检测遮挡物的问题，实现开放世界场景级去遮挡。代码将于项目主页发布。

Abstract: Recovering the complete appearance of occluded objects from a single image is a fundamental problem for scene understanding and image editing. Real-world scenes are challenging due to clutter, complex spatial occlusions, and diverse object categories. Existing methods often struggle when occlusion involves background surfaces or objects that are not explicitly detected, and fail to resolve the cyclic occlusion dependencies that frequently occur in natural scenes. We introduce a training-free, open-world scene deocclusion framework that recovers the full amodal appearance of detected or user-selected objects from a single RGB image, decomposing the scene into depth-ordered RGBA layers that are geometrically plausible, semantically faithful, and visually consistent with the visible scene. Extensive experiments show that our approach improves scene-level deocclusion, producing coherent and realistic reconstructions in real-world settings. Code will be available on the project page: https://sashjk.github.io/owsd/

</details>


### [24] [MMFlash: Faster Speculative Decoding for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18482)
*Sicheng Feng,Zigeng Chen,Song Wang,Gongfan Fang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MMFlash：面向多模态推测解码的扩散式草稿框架，针对视觉依赖强的token接受率低的问题自适应加权，并结合稀疏注意力处理长视频，实现最高3.58倍无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散式草稿器（如DFlash）单次前向生成整块token，但主要面向纯文本任务，多模态草稿尚属空白；且作者观察到视觉依赖程度越高，草稿token的接受率越低（从最低四分位的46%降至最高四分位的22%），即使经过多模态训练该差距依然存在。

Method: 首先提出基于“有/无视觉输入时下一token分布KL散度”的token级视觉依赖度量指标；据此设计MMFlash框架，对草稿器预测较差且视觉依赖强的token进行自适应加权（upweight）。针对长视频输入，进一步引入稀疏注意力以减少冗余注意力计算并提升草稿质量。

Result: 在多项多模态基准上实现无损解码加速最高达3.58倍，相比EAGLE-3与DFlash分别最高提升195.4%和17.1%。

Conclusion: 视觉依赖性是制约多模态推测解码接受率的关键因素，针对性地强化视觉依赖token的草稿训练并结合稀疏注意力，可在保持输出无损的前提下显著提升多模态生成效率。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates generation by letting a lightweight drafter propose tokens for parallel verification by a target model. Diffusion drafters such as DFlash propose an entire token block in a single forward pass, but mainly focus on text-only tasks, leaving multimodal drafting underexplored. To study this setting, we first introduce a metric of token-level visual dependence based on the KL divergence between next-token distributions with and without visual input. We find that acceptance rates decrease from 46% in the lowest visual-dependence quartile to 22% in the highest, with this disparity persisting after multimodal training. Motivated by this observation, we propose MMFlash, a simple and effective framework that adaptively upweights visually dependent tokens that the drafter predicts poorly. For long video inputs, MMFlash further employs sparse attention to reduce redundant attention computation and improve drafting quality. Across various multimodal benchmarks, MMFlash achieves lossless decoding speedups of up to $3.58\times$, outperforming EAGLE-3 and DFlash by up to 195.4% and 17.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [25] [SemRAG: Semantic-Guided Sentence-Level Retrieval-Augmented Sign Language Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18189)
*Xiao Liu,Shiwei Gan,Yafeng Yin,Peishuo Liu,Bowen Guo,Kuizhuang Liu,Cong Wang,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SemRAG：句级检索增强的手语生成框架，以完整句子手语模板作先验，避免依赖 gloss/词级词典；并用 SLT 引导的模板语义定位与分层可学习门控，缓解语义失配并自适应融合模板特征，提升语义一致性与动作质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SLP 方法从训练数据学习 text-to-pose 映射，在长句/复杂句上易出现语义错误；已有检索增强方法停留在 gloss 或词级，依赖昂贵的 gloss-pose 词典或孤立手语片段，难以保持连续句级动态。故需句级检索增强，但会带来检索句与目标语义不匹配（无关动作干扰）的问题。

Method: 1) SemRAG：语义引导的句级检索增强框架，直接检索整句手语模板作为先验，无需构建 gloss/词级手语词典，保留局部运动模式与全局节奏；2) SLT 引导的模板语义定位模块：在检索模板中定位与目标语义相关的时间区域，抑制无关手语内容；3) 层特异性可学习门控：在不同生成器层自适应调节模板特征的贡献。

Result: 大量实验表明，在回译（back-translation）指标与动作精度指标上，SemRAG 相较基线在语义一致性和动作质量上均有提升。

Conclusion: 句级检索增强配合语义定位与分层门控可有效引入真实手语先验，缓解语义失配，从而改善长/复杂句手语生成。

Abstract: Sign language production (SLP) aims to generate sign pose sequences that faithfully express spoken-language text while preserving the rhythm and coordination patterns of signing. Common SLP methods learn text-to-pose mappings from training data but often suffer from semantic inaccuracies in long or complex sentences. Retrieval augmentation offers a way to introduce realistic signing priors to provide more explicit guidance for text-to-pose generation. However, existing retrieval-based SLP methods typically operate at the gloss or word level, relying on gloss-pose dictionaries or isolated sign segments that require costly annotations and fail to preserve continuous sentence-level dynamics. To bridge this gap, we propose SemRAG, a semantic-guided sentence-level retrieval-augmented framework that retrieves full-sentence signing templates as priors without constructing gloss/word-level sign dictionaries. While such templates preserve local motion patterns and global rhythm, they also introduce a semantic mismatch problem: irrelevant motions in the retrieved sentence may interfere with the target semantics. To address this issue, we introduce an SLT-guided template semantic localization module that identifies target-relevant temporal regions within the retrieved template while suppressing irrelevant signing content. To adaptively incorporate the template features into the generator, we further introduce layer-specific learnable gates that regulate their contribution across generator layers. Extensive experiments show that SemRAG improves semantic consistency and motion quality over baselines under both back-translation and motion accuracy metrics.

</details>


### [26] [STaG: Human-Annotation-Free Unsupervised Domain Adaptation for Text-Based Person Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18172)
*Ji Yucheng,Bai Yang,Li Yang,Zhou Xinyu,Cao Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向无人工标注的跨域文本行人检索新设定（源域有图文标注、目标域无标注图像），并设计双路径框架STaG（风格无关文本对齐SATA + 异构图记忆对齐HGMA），在三个基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署中目标域缺乏文本标注，而现有UDA-TBPR方法仍假设目标域存在人工撰写的文本（虽与图像不配对），实用性受限。因此作者研究更现实的无人工标注UDA-TBPR设定，其核心挑战为：目标域缺乏跨模态对齐、以及源到目标的视觉域偏移。

Method: 双路径框架STaG。SATA：利用源域数据将身份判别性语义与机器生成的风格伪影解耦，从而在目标域建立稳健的跨模态对齐；HGMA：借助基于记忆的图结构，挖掘跨域语义邻域关系，学习域不变的视觉特征。

Result: 在该现实设定下，STaG在三个基准数据集上均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦风格伪影的文本对齐与跨域图记忆对齐，可在无目标域人工文本标注条件下有效缓解跨模态对齐缺失与视觉域偏移，验证了该更现实设定的可行性与方法有效性。

Abstract: Real-world deployment of Text-Based Person Retrieval (TBPR) is bottlenecked by the absence of textual annotations in new domains. Existing Unsupervised Domain Adaptation (UDA-TBPR) methods still assume access to target-domain human-written annotations (albeit unpaired with images), limiting their practicality. We consider a more practical setting: human-annotation-free UDA-TBPR, with labeled image-text data in the source domain and annotation-free, unlabeled images in the target domain. This setting introduces two core challenges: the absence of cross-modal alignment in the target domain, and visual domain shifts from source to target. To tackle these, we propose STaG, a dual-path framework comprising Style-Agnostic Text Alignment (SATA) and Heterogeneous Graph Memory Alignment (HGMA). Specifically, SATA leverages source-domain data to decouple identity-discriminative semantics from machine-generated stylistic artifacts, establishing robust cross-modal alignment for the target domain. Meanwhile, HGMA exploits cross-domain semantic neighborhoods via a memory-based graph to learn domain-invariant visual features. Ultimately, STaG achieves state-of-the-art performance across three benchmarks under this realistic setting.

</details>


### [27] [KnowVis: Dual-View Evaluation and Diagnosis of World-Knowledge Grounding in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18101)
*Yonghang Yu,Ruoyu Wang,Haoyu Zhang,Lin Qi,Yawei Zhou,Changqian Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KnowVis 是一个面向文生图（T2I）模型"世界知识落地"能力的双视角评测与诊断基准，基于分层知识分类体系与结构化 MLLM 问答评判协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 T2I 模型在视觉真实感、美学与指令遵循上进步显著，但真实提示常需隐含事实、结构化知识与领域常识。现有评测多聚焦显式提示-图像语义对齐或特定知识子域，缺乏对世界知识落地的统一评测与诊断。

Method: 构建分层知识分类体系并进行案例级标注；设计两个互补基准视角——Skill-Tree Benchmark（支持细粒度原子知识评测）与 Generalized Benchmark（考察开放式知识组合与表达）；采用基于 MLLM 的结构化 VQA 评判协议：Skill-Tree 用必需 VQA 评估原子知识正确性，Generalized 结合必需、可选及动态发现的知识表达并引入人机协同评判。

Result: 在 Skill-Tree 验证集上验证了协议可靠性，支持可扩展评测与诊断性错误归因；实验显示 KnowVis 揭示了现有 T2I 模型在世界知识落地上存在明显差距，并为知识密集型图像生成提供结构化诊断信号。

Conclusion: KnowVis 提供了统一的、可诊断的世界知识落地评测框架，弥补现有评测空白，并可作为知识密集型 T2I 生成的诊断工具。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models have made substantial progress in visual realism, aesthetic quality, and instruction following. However, real-world prompts often go beyond explicit visual descriptions and require implicit facts, structured knowledge, and domain-specific commonsense. Existing evaluations mainly focus on explicit prompt-to-image semantic alignment or specific knowledge subdomains, lacking unified evaluation and diagnosis of world-knowledge grounding in T2I models. We introduce KnowVis for dual-view evaluation and diagnosis of world-knowledge grounding in T2I generation. Built on a hierarchical knowledge taxonomy with case-level annotations, KnowVis combines two complementary benchmark views with a structured MLLM-based VQA judge protocol. The Skill-Tree Benchmark supports fine-grained atomic knowledge evaluation, while the Generalized Benchmark examines open-ended knowledge composition and expression. The protocol uses Required VQAs to assess atomic knowledge correctness in the Skill-Tree Benchmark, and combines Required, Optional, and dynamically discovered knowledge expressions with human-judge collaborative evaluation in the Generalized Benchmark. We validate the protocol's reliability on the Skill-Tree validation split, enabling scalable evaluation and diagnostic error attribution. Experiments show that KnowVis reveals notable gaps in world-knowledge grounding across existing T2I models and provides structured diagnostic signals for knowledge-intensive image generation.

</details>


### [28] [SWAM: Saliency-Weighted Asymmetric Merging for Efficient Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18057)
*Utkarsh Kumar Singh,Manish Das,Shiv Ram Dubey,Satish Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SWAM（显著性加权非对称合并）token缩减框架，在BSM基础上引入显著性感知的两步匹配与非对称指数显著性加权聚合，以轻量MLP预测显著性并用注意力rollout监督，在27.5% FLOPs削减、1.35倍加速下性能持平或超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT的自注意力对token呈二次复杂度，部署成本过高；现有token缩减（如BSM）未显式考虑token显著性差异，合并时可能损害信息量大的token。

Method: 扩展Bipartite Soft Matching：1）两步匹配——先以余弦相似度确定候选对，再用乘性最大显著性惩罚约束配对选择；2）非对称指数显著性加权聚合，使高显著性token对合并表示贡献更大。显著性由轻量MLP模块预测（每阶段75k参数，约为主干1%），以attention rollout为监督信号，与DeiT-Small主干端到端联合训练，损失为交叉熵+知识蒸馏+显著性对齐的复合目标。

Result: 保守方案（196→100 token，保留CLS，FLOPs降27.5%，1.35×加速）：CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet、ImageNet-100上持平或优于基线；激进方案（196→70 token，FLOPs降33.8%，1.46×加速）：ImageNet-100提升0.23%，CIFAR-100与TinyImageNet仅有极小损失。

Conclusion: 显式建模token显著性并将其融入匹配与聚合过程，可在显著降低计算量的同时保持甚至提升识别精度，且额外参数开销极小，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong performance on image recognition tasks, yet their quadratic self-attention complexity over input tokens creates prohibitive computational costs for practical deployment. Token reduction methods offer a practical solution by progressively reducing the token sequence during the forward pass. We propose Saliency-Weighted Asymmetric Merging (SWAM), a training-based token merging framework that extends the Bipartite Soft Matching (BSM) with two saliency-aware mechanisms. We introduce a two-step matching formulation in which cosine similarity decides candidate pairs, while a multiplicative maximum saliency penalty regulates their selection for merging. We then further use asymmetric exponential saliency-weighted aggregation, which allows more salient tokens to contribute more strongly to the merged representation. A lightweight MLP-based saliency prediction module (75k parameters per stage, 1% of backbone parameters) supervised via attention rollout is trained end-to-end jointly with a DeiT-Small backbone using a composite cross-entropy, knowledge-distillation, and saliency-alignment objective. On CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and ImageNet-100, under a conservative schedule that reduces tokens from 196 to 100 patch tokens, with the CLS token retained throughout, 27.5% FLOPs reduction and 1.35 times speedup, SWAM matches or exceeds baseline across the reported results. Under an aggressive schedule (196 to 70 tokens with the CLS token kept throughout, 33.8% FLOPs reduction, and 1.46 times speedup), accuracy gains are achieved on ImageNet-100 (+0.23%) with minimal losses on CIFAR-100 and TinyImageNet.

</details>


### [29] [Do Underwater Image Quality Metrics Agree with Human Perception? A Benchmark with Human Choices, Simple Edits and Generative Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18429)
*Rishii Kumar Singh,Abhinav Rajput,Dinesh Kumar Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了73种水下图像增强质量评价指标（手工、学习型、视觉语言、通用型）与人类偏好的—致性，发现没有任何指标能稳定匹配人类判断，最差情况Cohen's kappa仅0.23，并发布基准TrustUIQA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下图像增强缺乏干净参考图，研究界依赖无参考图像质量评价（NR-IQA）指标，但这些指标是否与人类判断一致尚不清楚，需要系统检验。

Method: 以五个人类参考（四个已发表的主观评分数据集+一项新的配对比较实验）为基准，评测73种指标（手工设计的水下指标、学习型水下指标、视觉语言模型指标、通用指标）；度量其"与人在同场景版本比较中选同一张图"的一致率；再测试简单图像编辑与小型对抗扰动能否使指标分数高于人类偏好的增强结果，以及重复生成编辑加噪时分数是否下降；最终构建基准TrustUIQA。

Result: 没有任何指标能持续匹配人类偏好；五参考上最坏情况Cohen's kappa最优仅0.23（Q-Align），而本研究两名评分者间中位kappa为0.41；广泛使用的UCIQE常偏好人类拒绝的编辑；在应保持图像不变的Nano Banana Pro复现链上，73个指标中仅22个分数在每个检查点均下降，而噪声增加2.5倍。

Conclusion: 现有NR-IQA指标与人类判断存在显著差距，不能可靠替代人工评价；作者发布TrustUIQA基准（含人类参考、实验选择、编辑/攻击/生成图像及各指标全部得分），代码与数据集已开源，以推动更可信的水下图像质量评价研究。

Abstract: Underwater image enhancement is challenging to evaluate due to the lack of clean reference images, so researchers rely on no-reference image quality assessment (NR-IQA) metrics. However, it remains unclear whether these metrics agree with human judgments. We evaluate 73 handcrafted underwater, learned underwater, vision-language and general-purpose metrics against human preferences on five references: four published opinion-score sets and a new paired-comparison study. For each metric, we measure how often it selects the same image as people when comparing versions of the same scene. We further test whether simple image edits and small adversarial perturbations raise metric scores above the enhancement people preferred, and whether scores decrease as repeated generative edits add noise. Our results show that no metric consistently matches human preferences. The best worst-case Cohen's kappa over the five references is only 0.23 (Q-Align), while two raters in our study agree with a median kappa of 0.41. The widely used UCIQE often favors edits that people reject. On a Nano Banana Pro replica chain that should leave the image unchanged, only 22 of 73 scores decrease at every checkpoint, while noise grows by 2.5 times. We also introduce TrustUIQA, a benchmark that combines the human references, our study choices, the edited, attacked and generated images, and the scores of every metric on every image. The code is available at https://github.com/TrustUIQA/TrustUIQA and TrustUIQA at https://huggingface.co/datasets/TrustUIQA/TrustUIQA.

</details>


### [30] [Adaptable Watermarking: Transforming Image Watermarking Models via Lightweight Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18363)
*Mehrdad Saberi,Vishal Asnani,Shruti Agarwal,Soheil Feizi,John Collomosse*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文提出轻量适配框架，使不同神经图像水印模型的编码器-解码器可通过小型适配器互相转换，实现模块化、可升级与互操作水印，仅需每组件1-2M可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经图像水印模型各自独立训练、架构与残差风格互不兼容，难以复用与升级；作者希望探究一个水印方法能否通过轻量适配转变为另一个，从而实现模块化、可升级和互操作的水印系统而无需完整重训练。

Method: 为编码器-解码器式水印架构附加紧凑的参数高效适配器（adapter），在多域残差对齐损失（空间、梯度、频率三个域）下微调以复现目标模型的残差风格，并用secret-loss项保持比特准确率；在多解码器推理流程中实现跨模型适配。

Result: 在TrustMark、InvisMark、RoSteALS、HiDDeN、WAM等近期模型上评估：适配后的编码器能较好复现目标残差，适配后的解码器在标准增强与失真下以接近最优的准确率恢复消息；跨模型适配每组件仅需1-2M可训练参数（如多解码器流程中2M的解码器适配器）。

Conclusion: 神经图像水印模型具备可适配性，轻量适配即可实现跨模型转换，支持互操作与可升级水印，为模块化水印生态提供可行路径。

Abstract: We explore the adaptability of neural image watermarking models; the extent to which one watermarking method can be transformed into another through lightweight adaptation. We introduce a framework that enables encoder-decoder watermarking architectures to become modular and upgradable, supporting multiple watermarking styles without re-training full models. Adaptation is performed by attaching a compact parameter-efficient adapter and fine-tuning under a multi-domain residual alignment loss (spatial, gradient, and frequency) while preserving bit accuracy via a secret-loss term. We evaluate this approach across a suite of recent watermarking models, including TrustMark, InvisMark, RoSteALS, HiDDeN, and WAM, demonstrating that adapted encoders closely reproduce target residuals and that adapted decoders recover messages with near-optimal accuracy under standard augmentations and distortions. We show that cross-model adaptation, with applications in interoperable and upgradable watermarking, can be achieved with as few as 1-2M trainable parameters per component (e.g., the 2M decoder adapter used in our multi-decoder inference pipeline).

</details>


### [31] [MaskSCD: Bitemporal Mask Classification for Semantic Change Detection in Remote Sensing Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18350)
*Weikang Yu,Pedram Ghamisi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MaskSCD，将语义变化检测重构为双时相标签对图像的掩码分类，保证变化图与语义图一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度SCD网络用各自独立的逐像素解码器分别预测变化图和各地物分类图，二者不一致：像素可能被标记为变化却在两期具有同一类别，且逐像素决策导致对象破碎。

Method: 将SCD表述为对双时相标签对图像（label-pair image）的掩码分类：每个“双时相分割段”（类别对恒定的区域）被预测为一个由两期共享的掩码，并附带每期一个类别，变化图由预测的类别对导出，标签空间随类别数线性增长。实现上采用双时相掩码注意力Transformer解码器（在两幅影像中读取同一段并关联两次读取），并配以双时相融合金字塔提供各期特征与变化感知特征。

Result: 在SECOND、Landsat-SCD、Hi-UCD（0.1–30 m分辨率、4–9类）上，与统一协议重训的14个近期SCD模型比较，MaskSCD在三数据集上均取得最优的分离kappa、语义变化F1与转移F1，平均分离kappa超出最强对手约5个点，且不产生矛盾变化像素（部分多任务网络中此类像素占预测变化近30%）。

Conclusion: 以掩码分类替代逐像素多任务解码，可从根本上保证变化与语义图的一致性，并在跨分辨率、跨类别数的多数据集上取得一致领先。

Abstract: Semantic change detection (SCD) identifies where the land surface has changed and which land-cover transition has taken place. Most deep SCD networks predict the change map and the land-cover map of each date with separate per-pixel decoders. These outputs need not agree, so a pixel can be labeled as changed although it has the same class at both dates, and per-pixel decisions fragment objects. We present MaskSCD, which formulates SCD as the mask classification of a bitemporal label-pair image. Each bitemporal segment, a region of constant land-cover class pair, is predicted as one mask shared by both dates together with one class per date, and the change map is derived from the predicted class pair. This formulation keeps the change and semantic maps consistent and the label space linear in the number of classes. A bitemporal masked-attention transformer decoder implements it by reading each segment in both images and relating the two readings, and a bitemporal fusion pyramid provides per-date and change-aware features. MaskSCD is evaluated on SECOND, Landsat-SCD and Hi-UCD, which span 0.1 to 30 m resolution and four to nine land-cover classes, against fourteen recent SCD models re-trained under one protocol. MaskSCD achieves the best separated kappa, semantic change $F_1$ and transition $F_1$ on all three datasets and exceeds the strongest competitor by about five points in mean separated kappa. It produces no contradictory change pixels, which account for nearly 30% of the predicted change of some multi-task networks. The code is available at https://github.com/EricYu97/MaskSCD.

</details>


### [32] [Lens: Training-Free Semantic Search for Cross-Tile Object and Scene Retrieval in Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18251)
*Ata Akbari Asanjan,Anit Upadhyaya,Jeremy Kravitz,Subash Yeggina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lens：一种无需训练的卫星影像语义对象与场景检索系统，基于冻结DINOv3特征，去除位置偏置后做居中与量化索引，实现单次点击级交互检索，DFC2020八类平均像素F1为0.39，查询成本约为INSID3基线的1/400。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卫星镶嵌图的目标/场景检索通常依赖针对特定类别训练的检测器，泛化性差、成本高。作者希望构建免训练、不绑定特定传感或类别、可在大规模镶嵌图上实时交互检索的通用系统。

Method: (1) 用冻结的自监督ViT（DINOv3）为每个瓦片提取稠密特征；(2) 用常数图像一次性估计位置基，将稠密特征投影到其正交子空间以消除位置偏置，再用全区平均特征做中心化；(3) 查询由点击点邻域patch（或整瓦片场景）的中心化特征平均构成，以余弦相似度匹配；(4) 将中心化patch特征存入字节量化的相似度索引支持实时范围搜索，另建粗粒度整瓦片嵌入索引；(5) 每瓦片运行一次引导上采样网络AnyUp以恢复像素级边界并缓存复用。

Result: 在5,128瓦片的DFC2020测试集上，按各类最佳阈值单次点击的像素F1在0.09–0.80之间，平均F1为0.39；在八类中的六类上优于成对的INSID3基线，查询成本约低400倍。并在五个Sentinel-2区域（农业、工业、沙漠）作为交互工具运行，管线不绑定特定传感器。索引131万patch在单CPU线程耗时169 s，单次点击在6–10 ms内返回渲染叠加结果。

Conclusion: Lens证明了无需类别专用检测器、无需训练即可在大规模卫星镶嵌图上实现毫秒级语义对象与场景检索；位置偏置去除与特征中心化是关键，量化索引与缓存式上采样保证了实时性与像素级边界精度。

Abstract: We developed Lens, a training-free system for semantic object and scene search across large satellite mosaics that requires no detector trained for the class of interest. Lens represents each tile with a frozen, self-supervised vision transformer (DINOv3), removes the positional bias in its dense features by projecting them onto the subspace orthogonal to a positional basis estimated once from a constant image, and centers the debiased features on the mean feature computed across the whole area before comparison. Lens forms a query by averaging the centered features of a small neighborhood of patches around a chosen point, or of an entire tile for scene-level search, and matches it against the index by cosine similarity. It stores the centered patch features in a byte-quantized similarity index that supports real-time range search over a mosaic of thousands of patches, and a coarser whole-tile embedding index for scene-level retrieval. A guided upsampling network (AnyUp) runs once per tile to recover pixel-accurate boundaries beyond the native feature grid, and the result is cached so later queries reuse it. We evaluated Lens on the 5,128-tile DFC2020 test set and ran it as an interactive tool on five Sentinel-2 areas spanning agricultural, industrial, and desert terrain; the pipeline is not tied to a specific sensor. On DFC2020 a single click reaches 0.09 to 0.80 pixel F1 across eight land-cover classes at the per-class best threshold, with a mean F1 of 0.39, and it beats a pairwise INSID3 baseline on six of the eight classes at roughly four hundred times lower query cost. Indexing 1.31 million patches takes 169 s on one CPU thread, and a click returns a rendered overlay in 6 to 10 ms.

</details>


### [33] [StreamEmbed: Real-Time Indexing from Pixels for Screenshot Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18516)
*Dayoon Ko,Yichuan Wang,Soyeong Jeong,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Gunhee Kim,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamEmbed 利用屏幕截图的时空冗余，通过空间 token 剪枝与时间 KV 复用实现增量视觉嵌入，配合小型学习适配器保持嵌入保真度，在 M2 Pro GPU 上编码提速最高 3.7 倍且不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕历史可作为可检索的视觉记忆，但需在每张截图到达时即时编码索引。OCR 索引快却丢失视觉信息，完整视觉编码耗时超过两秒。由于屏幕变化是增量的、大多像素在截图内部与截图之间重复，作者质疑视觉编码器是否有必要对每张新截图从零重算表示。

Method: 提出 StreamEmbed 增量视觉嵌入系统，仅以像素差异为引导：空间 token 剪枝跳过与相邻块重复的块；时间 KV 复用对跨截图未变的块复用缓存的 key/value；并用一个小型学习适配器补偿剪枝与复用带来的偏差，以保持与全截图嵌入的一致性。同时构建 ScreenMemEval 基准（10,426 个问题、184 小时屏幕历史，来自两名人类与一个计算机使用智能体），并设计高内存效率的向量索引。

Result: 在 M2 Pro GPU 上建立新的精度-延迟 Pareto 前沿：编码速度最高提升 3.7 倍且精度不降；在同等延迟下，相比 OCR 索引 Recall@10 最高提升 6.3 点；内存高效向量索引可将模拟一年的截图存于 0.13 GB RAM 中。

Conclusion: 结果表明屏幕历史的原生视觉形式实时索引已具备实用性，为全本地化视觉助手奠定基础。

Abstract: Screen history can serve as searchable visual memory for people and agents, but unlike an offline corpus, it must be encoded and indexed as each screenshot arrives. OCR-based indexing is fast but loses visual information; visual encoding takes over two seconds. Yet screens change incrementally: most pixels repeat within and across screenshots. This raises a question: do visual encoders need to recompute their representations from scratch for every new screenshot? We present StreamEmbed, a system for incremental visual embedding that exploits this redundancy. Guided solely by pixel differences, it combines spatial token pruning, which skips blocks that repeat a neighbor, with temporal KV reuse, which reuses cached keys and values for blocks unchanged across screenshots. A small learned adapter compensates for pruning and reuse to preserve fidelity to full-screenshot embeddings. On ScreenMemEval, our benchmark of 10,426 questions over 184 hours of screen histories from two humans and a computer-use agent, StreamEmbed sets a new accuracy-latency Pareto frontier on an M2 Pro GPU: it encodes up to 3.7x faster than full-screenshot encoding without sacrificing accuracy and improves accuracy over OCR-based indexing by up to 6.3 Recall@10 points at matched latency. A memory-efficient vector index stores a simulated year of screenshots in 0.13 GB of RAM. Together, these results make real-time indexing of screen history in its native visual form practical, laying a foundation for fully local visual assistants.

</details>


### [34] [Learning Anisotropic Prediction Targets for Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17767)
*Rayhan Zirvi,Hansheng Chen,Gordon Wetzstein,Leonidas Guibas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AnisoFlow，将流匹配中固定的预测目标（速度/噪声/干净数据）替换为可学习目标：在网络学到的基上对各方向噪声分量施加独立的各向异性非负权重，并通过闭式映射精确恢复速度，仅学习预测目标即可把ImageNet 256×256上的FID从1.90降到1.55。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式生成模型的预测目标（速度、噪声或干净数据）通常由人工选定，该选择对训练稳定性和样本质量影响很大，但缺乏原理性依据。作者希望让预测目标本身成为可优化量，在不改动架构、损失和采样器的前提下提升生成质量。

Method: AnisoFlow学习对各向异性噪声分量加权：在联合学习的基中为每个方向分配独立非负权重，从而得到完全可学习的预测目标。通过闭式映射从网络预测精确恢复速度，保持架构、流匹配损失和采样器不变；由于恢复是精确的，训练只用于让目标更易被网络预测。作者还对由此产生的预测误差进行理论分析，预测各方向偏好噪声权重近似随数据方差线性增长。

Result: 理论上推导出噪声权重与数据方差近似线性的关系，与像素空间中经验学到的权重一致。在ImageNet 256×256上，仅学习预测目标即可将FID由JiT基线的1.90降至1.55，无需表征对齐或架构改动。在像素空间文本到图像生成中提升了图像质量，并通过更良态的恢复速度基本消除了低精度推理下的块状拼缝伪影。

Conclusion: 预测目标应被视为可学习的设计变量而非人工先验；各向异性、数据自适应的目标加权在几乎零额外代价下显著改善FID与低精度推理鲁棒性，为流式生成模型提供了简洁有效的改进途径。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models are trained on a hand-chosen prediction target, such as the velocity, the noise, or the clean data. This choice strongly affects training and sample quality. We introduce Anisotropic Flow Models (AnisoFlow), which replaces a fixed prediction target with a fully learnable one. AnisoFlow learns to scale the noise component of the target anisotropically, assigning a separate nonnegative weight to each direction in a jointly learned basis. A closed-form map recovers the velocity from the network's prediction, leaving the architecture, flow-matching loss, and sampler unchanged.
