Since recovery is exact, training optimizes the target purely to make it easier for the network to predict. We theoretically analyze the resulting prediction error and predict that the preferred noise weight in each direction scales near-linearly with data variance, in agreement with the weights learned empirically in pixel space. AnisoFlow improves FID from 1.90 to 1.55 over the JiT baseline on ImageNet 256$\times$256, by learning only the prediction target, without representation alignment or architectural changes. In pixel-space text-to-image generation, AnisoFlow improves image quality and, through better-conditioned velocity recovery, largely removes patch-seam artifacts under reduced-precision inference.

</details>


### [35] [Vessel2Brain: Edge-Aware Multi-Scale Flow Matching for 3D Brain Image Generation from Vessel Masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17919)
*Zhi Chen,Tianyang Zhang,Theodoros N. Arvanitis,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Vessel2Brain，一种边缘感知多尺度条件流匹配框架，仅以血管标签图条件合成完整3D脑MRI体数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭血管标签图生成全脑MRI极具价值（数据增强、仿真、下游分析），但问题高度病态：血管结构仅占脑容积极小部分，且不直接提供周围组织外观信息，需要有效注入血管拓扑先验。

Method: 将血管引导合成建模为条件连续流（conditional flow matching），保证训练稳定与生成灵活并维持结构对应；采用多尺度流匹配策略联合建模全局解剖一致性与局部细节，并在不同空间分辨率引入辅助速度预测作为额外监督；设计边缘感知目标，在训练中强调血管边界，促使血管周围组织合成更具解剖合理性。

Result: 在两个带配对血管标注的公开3D脑MRI数据集上，定量与定性实验显示该方法在重建精度、结构一致性和分布真实性上一致优于现有先进生成模型。

Conclusion: 条件流匹配结合多尺度建模与边缘感知监督，可有效解决稀疏解剖先验驱动的全脑MRI合成问题，为数据增强与神经影像分析提供可行方案。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D brain MRI volumes from sparse anatomical priors is a highly ill-posed yet clinically valuable problem, with important applications in data augmentation, simulation, and downstream neuroimaging analysis. In this work, we investigate the challenging task of synthesising full 3D brain MRI volumes conditioned solely on vascular label maps. While vascular structures provide strong anatomical and topological cues, they occupy only a small fraction of the brain volume and offer no direct information about surrounding tissue appearance. To address this challenge, we propose Vessel2Brain, an edge-aware multi-scale conditional flow matching framework that explicitly incorporates vascular structure information into the generative process. Our approach formulates vessel-guided brain image synthesis as a conditional continuous flow, enabling stable training and flexible generation while preserving structural correspondence. A multi-scale flow matching strategy is introduced to jointly model global anatomical coherence and fine-grained local details, with auxiliary velocity predictions providing additional supervision across spatial resolutions. Furthermore, we design an edge-aware objective that emphasises vascular boundaries during training, encouraging anatomically faithful tissue synthesis around vessel structures. We evaluate Vessel2Brain on two public 3D brain MRI datasets with paired vascular annotations. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art generative models in terms of reconstruction accuracy, structural consistency, and distributional realism.

</details>


### [36] [CardioTrace: Motion-Aware Cine Synthesis via Coupled Tracing and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17909)
*Zhi Chen,Le Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CardioTrace 是一个运动感知的心脏电影 MRI 合成框架，可从有限的稀疏输入生成完整心脏电影序列，并显式建模与追踪心脏运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床常规采集的心脏电影序列常因时间稀疏、心律失常、运动伪影与扫描时长限制而质量下降，影响心室功能的可靠量化与下游分析，因此需要从有限输入重建完整、时间一致的电影序列。

Method: 采用双路径耦合架构：像素平移分支以残差形式直接预测电影帧；流场分支估计相对参考相位的时变形变场，提供可解释的帧间运动对应。另设分割分支在关键相位监督临床有意义的心脏结构，并引入跨分支一致性目标，使合成表观动态遵循显式运动引导，抑制长序列中的时间漂移与不真实形变。框架还支持提示式条件化以融入患者或采集辅助信息，提升多中心异构数据的鲁棒性。

Result: 在公开心脏电影 MRI 数据集上，相较代表性 GAN 与扩散基线，CardioTrace 在感知质量与时间一致性上均有提升，并在整个心动周期中更好地保持心室形态。

Conclusion: 显式运动追踪与跨分支一致性约束可有效改善电影序列合成的感知质量与时间连贯性，并保持解剖合理性；代码将在评审后公开。

Abstract: Cardiac cine MRI provides time-resolved visualization of cardiac motion and is essential for evaluating ventricular function and anatomy. In routine clinical acquisition, however, cine sequences are frequently temporally sparse or degraded by arrhythmia, motion, and scan-time constraints, which can hinder reliable quantification and downstream analysis. We present CardioTrace, a motion-aware cine synthesis framework that generates a full cardiac cine sequence from limited input while explicitly modeling and tracing cardiac motion. CardioTrace couples two complementary pathways: a pixel-translation branch that directly predicts cine frames in a residual formulation, and a flow-field branch that estimates time-resolved deformation fields from a reference phase to provide interpretable motion correspondences across frames. To further preserve anatomical plausibility, a segmentation branch supervises clinically meaningful cardiac structures at key phases, and a cross-branch consistency objective encourages the synthesized appearance dynamics to follow the explicit motion guidance, mitigating temporal drift and unrealistic shape transitions over long sequences. The framework also supports prompt-style conditioning to incorporate auxiliary patient or acquisition information, improving robustness across heterogeneous multi-center data. Experiments on public cardiac cine MRI datasets demonstrate that CardioTrace improves perceptual quality and temporal coherence over representative GAN- and diffusion-based baselines, while better preserving ventricular morphology throughout the cardiac cycle. \textit{Our codes are available after review}.

</details>


### [37] [PADSNet: Physics-Aware Dual-Stream Network with Biomechanical Priors for Laryngeal Lesion Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17852)
*Zhenzhen You,Weifeng Yu,Zhenghao Shi,Shuangli Du,Minghua Zhao,Yingcheng Zhao,Xinhong Hei,Demin Kong,Xiaoyong Ren,Jin Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理感知双流网络PADSNet，将深度视觉表征与显式生物力学推理融合，实现喉部病变分类，临床数据上准确率0.8452、F1 0.8090、敏感度0.7953、特异度0.8866。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喉部病变类间视觉差异细微，分类困难；声带振动动力学异常可提供互补判别信息，但现有深度学习模型主要依赖外观表征，缺乏对临床有意义振动动力学的显式建模机制。

Method: 双流架构：1）小波注意力增强模块（WAEM）弥合频谱-语义鸿沟，实现空间纹理与动态频率表征的互补学习；2）知识计算模块（KCM）从声门距离时序波形中提取可解释生物力学指标（活动性、对称性、声门闭合、振动稳定性）；3）物理感知门控模块（PAGM）以这些指标作为生物力学先验，自适应校准视觉特征，使其偏向病理相关振动模式。

Result: 在临床数据集上取得准确率0.8452、F1 0.8090、敏感度0.7953、特异度0.8866。

Conclusion: 显式融合生物力学先验与深度视觉表征，可构建更准确、鲁棒且具临床可解释性的自动化喉部病变分类框架。

Abstract: Precise classification of laryngeal lesions is clinically crucial yet highly challenging, as inter-class visual differences are often subtle. While abnormalities in vocal fold vibratory dynamics provide complementary discriminative information, existing deep learning models predominantly rely on visual appearance and lack explicit mechanisms for modeling clinically meaningful vibratory dynamics. To address this, we propose PADSNet, a Physics-Aware Dual-Stream Network for laryngeal lesion classification that integrates visual representations with explicit biomechanical reasoning. Specifically, a Wavelet-Attention Enhancement Module (WAEM) is designed to bridge the spectral-semantic gap, enabling complementary learning of spatial texture and dynamic frequency representations. Meanwhile, a Knowledge Computing Module (KCM) extracts interpretable biomechanical indices from temporal glottal distance waveforms, including indicators of mobility, symmetry, glottal closure, and vibration stability. These descriptors are subsequently incorporated into a Physics-Aware Gating Module (PAGM) as biomechanical priors to adaptively recalibrate visual feature toward pathology-relevant vibratory patterns. Extensive experiments on a clinical dataset demonstrate that PADSNet achieves an accuracy of 0.8452, an F1-score of 0.8090, a sensitivity of 0.7953, and a specificity of 0.8866. These results indicate that explicitly integrating biomechanical priors with deep visual representations provides a more accurate, robust, and clinically interpretable framework for automated laryngeal lesion classification.

</details>


### [38] [Stage-wise Lineage-aware Dropout for Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17752)
*Yan Xing,Zhi Chen,Fanglue Zhang,Junhong Zhao,Bing Xue,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Stage-wise Lineage-aware Dropout(SLD)与Lineage-Balanced Densification(LBD)的稀疏视角3DGS正则化框架.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角下3DGS因图像约束不足易过拟合.现有Dropout正则化对单个高斯原语丢弃效果有限,原因是同一家族内相邻高斯会对被丢弃者产生补偿效应,削弱正则作用.

Method: 引入lineage family概念:将初始化生成的founder高斯经克隆/分裂派生出的原语归为同一家族.提出SLD:结构正则阶段按家族整体丢弃,后期精化阶段切换为个体丢弃;并提出LBD:在致密化时构建扩展候选池,依据各家族平均梯度得分与已有后代数量重新分配致密化预算.

Result: 在LLFF、MipNeRF360、Blender数据集上,该框架在稀疏视角重建中取得有竞争力的性能.

Conclusion: 以家族为粒度的丢弃与预算再分配可有效缓解稀疏视角3DGS过拟合,分阶段正则策略与致密化控制相结合具有实用价值.

Abstract: Sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is prone to overfitting because only a limited number of images are available to constrain scene geometry. Dropout is one regularization strategy for alleviating this problem. We introduce the concept of a lineage family to describe a set of Gaussian primitives derived from the same founder through cloning or splitting operations. The founder Gaussians refer to all Gaussian primitives generated during initialization. Descendants within the same family often jointly contribute to the refinement of adjacent local regions. However, dropping individual Gaussians can be less effective due to the compensation effect from neighboring Gaussians for the dropped ones. To address this issue, we propose Stage-wise Lineage-aware Dropout(SLD), which applies lineage-family dropout during the structural regularization stage and switches to individual dropout during the later refinement stage. We further propose Lineage-Balanced Densification(LBD), which operates during densification by constructing an expanded candidate pool and reallocating the densification budget according to the mean gradient scores and existing descendant sizes of different families. Experiments on the LLFF, MipNeRF360, and Blender datasets show that the proposed framework achieves competitive performance in sparse-view reconstruction

</details>


### [39] [Unlocking Cross-Attention in Label-Scarce Regimes: A Weakly Supervised Approach for Video Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18324)
*Andrea Marelli,Alessandro Ardenghi,Giacomo Boracchi,Luca Magri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemCAM：一种弱监督视频语义分割transformer框架，通过特征空间自监督直接学习时序cross-attention，用CAM传播算法合成监督信号，无需光流等外部先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WS-VSS仅用视频级标签学习时序一致的语义掩码。全监督模型靠cross-attention学习时序关系，但在弱监督下训练时序权重不稳定，甚至低于单帧基线。现有方法依赖光流等外部先验做时序一致，但这些线索非语义、计算昂贵且含噪，损害监督信号。

Method: MemCAM不直接监督注意力权重也不用外部先验，而是通过特征空间自监督学习时序cross-attention。引入CAM Propagation Algorithm：依据潜空间中patch级特征相似度传播历史CAM，合成监督信号；再通过预测图与传播图之间的一致性目标约束诱导CAM，稳定时序注意力学习。

Result: 在YouTube-Objects v2.2和WS²数据集上，MemCAM在时序定位与分割性能上一致优于现有WS-VSS方法。

Conclusion: cross-attention可通过内部自监督在标签稀缺的视频场景中有效训练，验证了无需外部先验的时序注意力学习路径的可行性。

Abstract: Weakly Supervised Video Semantic Segmentation (WS-VSS) aims to learn temporally consistent semantic object masks in videos using only video-level labels. While fully supervised models successfully learn temporal relationships by means of cross-attention, training these temporal weights under weak supervision is unstable and even pushes the model below its single-frame baseline. Therefore, existing WS-VSS methods exploit temporal consistency by relying on external priors, such as optical flow. Unfortunately these cues are non-semantic, computationally expensive and noisy, degrading the supervisory signal. Instead of supervising attention weights directly or using external priors, we propose MemCAM, a WS-VSS transformer framework that learns temporal cross-attention directly through feature-space self-supervision. MemCAM introduces a CAM Propagation Algorithm that synthesizes a supervisory signal by propagating past CAMs according to patch-level feature similarities in the latent space. Specifically, we constrain their induced CAMs through a consistency objective between predicted and propagated maps, stabilizing temporal attention learning. Experiments on the YouTube-Objects v2.2 and WS$^2$ datasets demonstrate that MemCAM consistently outperforms the temporal localization and segmentation performance of WS-VSS alternatives, showing that cross-attention can be effectively trained in label-scarce video settings through internal self-supervision.

</details>


### [40] [DRA-HTR: Disentangling Representation and Adaptation for Handwritten Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18143)
*Gayan H. Pathirage,Florent Imbert,Denis Coquenet,Elisa H. Barney Smith*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DRA-HTR：基于DRANet的无监督生成式域适应流水线，并引入LLM后校正DRAL-HTR，在7个数据集上刷新HTR域适应SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史手写文本识别（HTR）面临标注数据稀缺与显著域偏移（书写风格、文档退化、采集条件、语言差异），而目标域无标注数据虽多，却缺乏针对HTR特性定制的有效利用策略。

Method: 在DRANet框架上构建无监督生成式域适应流水线DRA-HTR：先源域预训练，再以生成目标风格源样本的方式实现面向目标域视觉特性的无监督适应并用于任务特定训练；进一步提出DRAL-HTR，利用大语言模型进行HTR后校正，以语言适应补充视觉适应。全流程在7个数据集、4种语言上使用同一组超参数。

Result: 识别模型在4个数据集上达SOTA、其余3个具竞争力；DRA-HTR在全部7个数据集上建立域适应新SOTA，平均CER降低13.52个百分点，平均标准差由7.23降至1.11个百分点；后校正进一步降低CER 2.16个百分点。

Conclusion: 生成式视觉域适应与LLM语言后校正互补，可显著提升HTR跨域识别的准确性与训练稳定性，且单一超参数配置具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: Handwritten text recognition (HTR), particularly when applied to historical documents, remains challenging due to the scarcity of annotated data and the substantial domain shifts caused by variations in handwriting styles, document degradation, acquisition conditions, and linguistic characteristics. While large amounts of unlabeled target-domain data are often available, effectively exploiting them requires adaptation strategies tailored to the specific requirements of HTR. We propose DRA-HTR, an unsupervised generative domain adaptation pipeline specifically designed for HTR and built upon the DRANet framework. The proposed approach combines source-domain pretraining, unsupervised adaptation to the visual characteristics of the target domain, relying on the generation of target-style source samples for task-specific training. We further investigate the integration of post-HTR correction to complement visual adaptation with language adaptation, relying on large language models, which we introduce as DRAL-HTR. The proposed framework is evaluated across seven HTR datasets spanning four languages, using a single set of hyperparameters for all source-target combinations. The recognition model achieves state-of-the-art performance on four datasets and competitive results on the remaining three. In addition, the proposed DRA-HTR establishes a new state-of-the-art for domain adaptation across all seven datasets and substantially improves overall robustness and training stability compared to the original DRANet framework: it reduces the Character Error Rate (CER) in average by 13.52 percentage points, and the average standard deviation decreases from 7.23 to 1.11 percentage points. Finally, post-HTR correction further reduces the CER by 2.16 percentage points.

</details>


### [41] [RecurIR: Learning When and How to Revise Restoration Policies via Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18006)
*Hu Gao,Haoran Zhang,Yulong Chen,Lizhuang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RecurIR 递归自改进框架，使图像复原智能体能学习"何时"以及"如何"修正决策规则，从而在退化条件变化时保持策略有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能体的图像复原通过协调工具处理异构、耦合退化已取得较好效果，但其决策改进依赖已积累的成功/失败执行经验，未显式识别所学复原策略的有效条件。因此当退化严重程度、空间覆盖范围或退化间交互发生变化时，先前决策可能失效，且缺乏关于如何修订这些决策的指导。

Method: 1）策略假设发现机制：通过匹配的工具执行，识别可检验的、依赖条件的规则；2）规则伪证驱动的任务演化机制：生成针对性的退化变体，检验已学规则的有效性并确定其适用边界；3）变化条件驱动的策略修正机制：基于所得证据学习退化条件变化应如何改变工具选择、执行顺序、操作强度与处理区域；4）引入独立验证保留有效修正，并将反馈用于指导后续假设发现与任务演化，形成递归闭环。

Result: 通过递归过程随退化条件变化逐步精化复原策略；大量实验验证了所提框架的有效性（摘要未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: RecurIR 将"条件依赖规则的发现—伪证检验—条件驱动修正—独立验证"整合为递归自改进闭环，弥补了经验式智能体缺乏策略适用边界认知的不足，为动态退化条件下的图像复原提供可解释、可修订的决策范式。

Abstract: Agent-based image restoration has achieved promising performance by coordinating restoration tools to handle heterogeneous and coupled degradations. However, existing agents often improve their decisions through accumulated successful and failed executions, without explicitly identifying the conditions under which learned restoration strategies remain effective. Consequently, changes in degradation severity, spatial coverage, or interactions may invalidate previous decisions, while providing limited guidance on how these decisions should be revised. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RecurIR}, a recursive self-improvement framework that enables restoration agents to learn when and how to revise their decision rules. Specifically, we introduce a policy-hypothesis discovery mechanism to identify testable, condition-dependent rules through matched tool executions. We then design a rule-falsification-driven task evolution mechanism to generate targeted degradation variations, examine the validity of learned rules, and identify their applicability boundaries. Based on the resulting evidence, we develop a change-conditioned policy revision mechanism that learns how variations in degradation conditions should modify tool selection, execution order, operation strength, and processing regions. We further incorporate independent verification to retain effective revisions and use the resulting feedback to guide subsequent hypothesis discovery and task evolution. Through this recursive process, RecurIR progressively refines its restoration policies as degradation conditions change. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [42] [JaRS: Contrast-Free Localization of Memorized Regions in Diffusion Models via Jacobian Row Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18024)
*Saisuchith Mahajan,Aditey Nandan,Rohit Singh,Nishanth Shetty,Chandra Sekhar Seelamantula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出JaRS（Jacobian行和）统计量及JaRS+检测评分，无需参考图即可在扩散模型中定位记忆复现区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型会记忆并复现训练图像的部分区域，带来隐私与版权风险，需定位这些区域。现有基于对比的方法使用去噪器Jacobian对角线（反映像素后验方差），但对角线小值存在歧义：既可能是记忆，也可能是平滑/可预测区域中多邻近像素的平均结果。消除该歧义需减去参考图（空提示或早期检查点的二次评估），而无条件模型无空提示、早期检查点通常不可得。

Method: 证明Jacobian行和（JaRS）区别于对角线，可在无参考图下区分记忆与局部平均。将JaRS与图像级检测评分配对，该评分在早期去噪步使用同一统计量，构成JaRS+。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion v1.4、v2.1、Realistic Vision v5.1及无条件DiT上，JaRS+在定位性能上优于基于对比的方法。

Conclusion: JaRS无需参考图即可解决对角线的歧义，JaRS+在多模型上实现更优的记忆区域定位，适用于无条件扩散模型。

Abstract: Diffusion models sometimes memorize and reproduce regions of their training images, raising privacy and copyright concerns. We aim to localize these regions. Existing contrast-based methods use the diagonal of the denoiser Jacobian, which reflects the posterior variance of each pixel. However, a small diagonal entry is ambiguous: it signals either memorization or averaging over many neighboring pixels in smooth or predictable regions. To resolve this ambiguity, these methods subtract a reference map, which requires a second evaluation with a null prompt or an earlier checkpoint. Unconditional models have no null prompt, and earlier checkpoints are unavailable in general. We show that Jacobian row sums (JaRS), unlike the diagonal, separate memorization from local averaging without a reference map. Pairing JaRS with an image-level detection score that uses the same statistic at an early denoising step yields JaRS+. Across Stable Diffusion v1.4 and v2.1, Realistic Vision v5.1, and an unconditional diffusion Transformer (DiT), JaRS+ outperforms contrast-based localization methods.

</details>


### [43] [nnAEU: a Simple and Efficient CNN-Autoencoder for Hyperspectral Unmixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17956)
*Edgard Dabier,Christophe Kervazo,Pietro Gori,Florence Tupin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级CNN自编码器nnAEU，通过对UNMamba主干剪枝完全移除Mamba模块得到，参数量比UNMamba少一个数量级以上，精度反而更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高光谱解混（HSU）研究沿"架构日益复杂化"路线发展（自编码器→Transformer→Mamba），各方法均宣称达到SOTA，但复杂度的实际收益缺乏验证。作者试图检验这些复杂组件是否必要。

Method: 以UNMamba（2025）的主干网络为基础，开展剪枝研究，将Mamba组件整体移除，得到一个仅由CNN构成的自编码器nnAEU；并提供完整、极简、可复现的代码。

Result: nnAEU的参数量相比UNMamba减少超过10倍，且远少于其他Transformer类与GAN类方法；同时在解混精度上优于上述这些方法。

Conclusion: HSU领域中Mamba、Transformer等复杂结构带来的增益可能被高估，轻量CNN自编码器即可在更低参数成本下取得更好性能，呼吁重新审视模型复杂化的必要性并重视可复现的简单基线。

Abstract: Deep learning-based hyperspectral unmixing (HSU) has followed a trajectory of increasing architectural complexity, from simple autoencoders to transformers and, most recently, Mamba-based models, each claiming to advance the state of the art. Contrary to this trend, we propose a lightweight CNN-based autoencoder, coined "Not New Autoencoder Unmixing" (nnAEU). It is derived from the backbone of UNMamba (2025) through a pruning study that removes the Mamba component entirely. nnAEU uses less than 10 times fewer parameters than UNMamba, and far fewer than other transformer- and GAN-based methods, while outperforming them in accuracy. We provide a complete, minimal, reproducible code, available at https://gitlab.telecom-paris.fr/ring/nnAEU.git.

</details>


### [44] [Token2Tile: Token-Wise Sparse Attention with Tile Execution through a Low-Rank Space for Video DiTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17930)
*Xuecheng Wu,Aichen Feng,Jinyan Chen,Qingjie Zhao,Julien Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Token2Tile：一种免训练的稀疏注意力算子，兼顾逐查询（per-query）精度与GPU tile级高效执行，用在线低秩空间实现聚类、打分与残差近似，在视频扩散Transformer上实现1.48–1.54倍端到端加速且质量损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer中注意力建模时空依赖至关重要，但二次复杂度成为主要推理瓶颈。注意力高度稀疏，可只计算重要交互来加速生成；然而精度要求按查询各自选择key token，而GPU kernel以tile为单位执行，二者粒度不一致，需要一种同时满足精度与执行效率的方案。

Method: 提出Token2Tile，免训练的稀疏注意力算子，将逐查询打分与逐查询残差同tile级执行结合。其从query和key直接计算在线低秩空间以保持注意力logits，从而高效完成聚类、打分与残差近似；随后融合逐查询选择，在查询簇间分配精确工作量，并将选中的key打包为GPU tile执行。

Result: 在MiniMax-H3与Wan2.1-14B上，相较稠密FlashAttention-3取得1.48–1.54倍端到端加速，VBench分数与稠密相差在0.5分以内。在H3上同速度下PSNR较SOL和SubBlock提升5.5–6.8 dB；在Wan上较SOL提升4.6–4.8 dB且速度更快。

Conclusion: Token2Tile有效弥合了逐查询稀疏选择的精度优势与tile级GPU执行效率之间的差距，无需训练即可在视频扩散Transformer上实现显著加速且保持生成质量，并在与已有稀疏注意力方法的对比中取得一致的质量与速度优势。

Abstract: Attention is essential for modeling spatio-temporal dependencies in video diffusion transformers, but its quadratic cost makes it a major inference bottleneck. Its high sparsity offers an opportunity to accelerate generation by focusing computation on important interactions. However, accuracy and execution favor different granularities: each query attends to its own key tokens, whereas GPU kernels work on tiles. We present Token2Tile, a training-free sparse attention operator that combines per-query scoring and a per-query residual with tile execution. An online low-rank space, computed directly from queries and keys to preserve attention logits, makes clustering, scoring, and residual approximation efficient. Token2Tile unites query-specific selections to allocate exact work across query clusters, then packs the selected keys into GPU tiles. On MiniMax-H3 and Wan2.1-14B, Token2Tile delivers 1.48-1.54x end-to-end speedup over dense FlashAttention-3 with VBench scores within 0.5 points of dense. At comparable speeds on H3, it improves PSNR over SOL and SubBlock by 5.5-6.8 dB; on Wan, it exceeds SOL by 4.6-4.8 dB while running faster.

</details>


### [45] [JVT: Just Video Transformer makes pixel-space video diffusion simple](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18549)
*Jianjun Zhou,Hua Geng,Haoyu Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 JVT：一种无需预训练自编码器、无级联、无 U-Net 跳连的简式大 patch DiT 像素空间视频扩散模型，质量可比潜空间扩散基线，并原生支持像素对齐多模态联合生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接对高维原始视频像素做扩散建模困难，现有像素空间方法依赖专门架构（自编码器、细节增强器、级联阶段、U-Net 跳连等），结构复杂。作者希望验证简洁的 DiT 式 Transformer 能否在像素空间完成高质量视频扩散。

Method: 采用大 patch 的 DiT 风格 Transformer 直接在像素空间做视频扩散，不使用预训练自编码器、辅助 detailer、级联结构或 U-Net 跳连；系统性考察视频 patch 嵌入、register token 与 adaptive layer normalization（AdaLN）等设计，以在生成质量与计算效率间取得平衡。

Result: 在类别条件与无条件设定下，生成质量与潜空间扩散基线相当；像素空间形式天然支持像素对齐多模态联合生成，在相机条件 RGB-视差生成中相较纯 RGB 基线提升相机跟随性，并在推理阶段无需额外重建模型即可获得显式场景几何。

Conclusion: 简式 DiT 风格 Transformer 在像素空间视频扩散中具有潜力；相关设计分析与实证结果为后续研究提供了可复用的见解与实践指导。

Abstract: Direct diffusion modeling of high-dimensional raw video data is challenging, and existing pixel-space methods often rely on specialized architectures. We introduce JVT (Just Video Transformer), a simple large-patch DiT-style Transformer for pixel-space video diffusion, without a pretrained autoencoder, auxiliary detailers, cascaded stages, or U-Net-style skip connections. We systematically examine video patch embeddings, register tokens, and adaptive layer normalization to identify design choices that balance generation quality and computational efficiency. The resulting JVT design achieves generation quality competitive with latent diffusion baselines in both class-conditioned and unconditional settings. The pixel-space formulation of JVT also naturally supports joint generation of pixel-aligned modalities. We demonstrate this capability through camera-conditioned RGB-disparity generation, which improves camera following relative to RGB-only baselines and yields explicit scene geometry without an additional reconstruction model at inference. These results highlight the potential of simple DiT-style Transformers for pixel-space video diffusion, and we hope our findings offer useful insights and practical guidance for future research.

</details>


### [46] [Understanding Contextual Bias in Agricultural CNNs Through Hidden-Neuron Concept Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18530)
*Abhilekha Dalal,Avishek Das,Samatha Ereshi Akkamahadevi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究用概念归纳（Concept Induction）从知识图谱背景知识中推导逻辑表达式，刻画CNN隐藏神经元在上下文图像块上的可解释视觉属性，并在5个农业数据集、4种架构、3个深度、5个位置共48,013个神经元-特征对上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究仅通过分类准确率发现CNN利用背景主导的上下文信息（contextual bias），但对隐藏层究竟表征什么缺乏解释；作者探究概念归纳能否揭示与隐藏神经元激活相关的可解释视觉属性。

Method: 选取5个具有高、中、低上下文偏置的农业数据集，分析4种CNN架构在3个网络深度与5个图像块位置上的表现；将每个图像块的定量图像描述符编码进知识图谱，作为概念归纳的背景知识，推导刻画神经元激活的逻辑表达式，并用Mann-Whitney U检验与Cliff's delta在留出图像上验证。

Result: 48,013个神经元-特征对中13,164个（27.4%）通过验证；中间层验证率最高（中层均值40.2%，深层仅12.0%），无某一图像块位置在各数据集上占优；边缘、Gabor与Tamura纹理特征的验证率最高；上下文偏置越强并不对应更高验证率——中等偏置的Sugarcane最高（40.5%），高偏置的Cassava最低（19.2%）。

Conclusion: 行为层面的上下文依赖与隐藏神经元的语义可解释性反映CNN行为的不同侧面；概念归纳可作为基于图像块的偏置检测的补充手段。

Abstract: Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) can classify images using background-dominated patches that contain little or none of the target object, showing that they exploit contextual information. Previous studies detected this behavior through classification accuracy but say little about what CNN hidden layers represent. We investigate whether Concept Induction can reveal interpretable visual properties associated with hidden-neuron activations on contextual image patches. Using five agricultural datasets with high, moderate, and low contextual bias, we analyze four CNN architectures at three network depths and five patch locations. Quantitative image descriptors of each patch are encoded in a knowledge graph and used as background knowledge for Concept Induction, which derives logical expressions characterizing neuron activation. These expressions are tested on held-out images using the Mann-Whitney U test and Cliff's delta. Of 48,013 tested neuron-feature pairs, 13,164 (27.4%) were validated. Validation was most frequent at intermediate depths (mean 40.2% at mid layers versus 12.0% at deep layers), and no patch location dominated across datasets. Among the tested descriptors, edge, Gabor, and Tamura texture features had the highest validation rates. Stronger contextual bias did not correspond to higher neuron-feature validation rates: moderately biased Sugarcane had the highest rate (40.5%) and highly biased Cassava the lowest (19.2%). These results suggest that behavioral context dependence and the semantic interpretability of hidden neurons capture different aspects of CNN behavior, and that Concept Induction can complement patch-based bias detection.

</details>


### [47] [ALoMa: Affine Correspondences Beyond Patches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18170)
*David Nordström,Georg Bökman,Viktor Larsson,Fredrik Kahl,Jonathan Ventura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文重新审视仿射对应（AC）估计问题，将估计从局部图像块推广到图像级与图像对级，并联合学习完整的4自由度仿射矩阵，提出基于LoMa关键点描述的AC估计器ALoMa。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AC能约束点对应的局部几何，使多视图几何求解器更鲁棒高效，但现有研究主要关注其在最小求解器中的应用，AC估计本身研究不足；且传统方法通常分模块估计仿射变换的各个分量并局限于局部图像块。

Method: 将AC估计扩展到图像级和图像对级；不再分别估计仿射变换各分量，而是联合学习完整4-DoF仿射矩阵；在此基础上提出ALoMa，利用LoMa关键点描述进行AC估计。

Result: 在广泛的下游任务中取得一致的性能提升，代码已开源。

Conclusion: AC估计可有效超越局部图像块层面，联合学习4-DoF仿射矩阵并结合LoMa描述能稳定改进多视图几何相关下游任务，为AC估计研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: Affine correspondences (ACs) enable robust and efficient solvers for multiple-view geometry by constraining the local geometry of point correspondences (PCs). Despite their utility in minimal solvers, AC estimation remains understudied. We revisit AC estimation beyond local image patches, extending it to the image and image-pair levels. Rather than estimating the components of the affine transformation separately, we learn the full 4-DoF matrix jointly. Building on this formulation, we introduce ALoMa, an AC estimator using LoMa keypoint descriptions, demonstrating consistent improvements across a wide range of downstream tasks. Code at https://github.com/davnords/loma.

</details>


### [48] [Speed-Variation Distillation for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17868)
*Sukit Seripanitkarn,Phonphrm Thawatdamrongkit,Supasorn Suwajanakorn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种"全程可识别"的生成框架：用缩减变异的退化过程把多样图像收缩到共享原型，再蒸馏逆向的"多样化算子"从原型生成多样样本，实现逐步可识别、可早停与速度-多样性可控权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的去噪轨迹会经过远离图像流形的损坏中间态，作者质疑生成过程能否在整条轨迹上始终保持"可识别的图像"。

Method: 设计一种缩减变异（variation-reducing）的退化过程，将多样图像收缩至共享原型且保留可识别中间态；因该流形遍历无解析形式，借助基于生成先验的图像变形（morphing）构造轨迹；将逆过程蒸馏为多样化算子，把原型变换为日益多样的样本；针对逆过程一对多歧义，将每条轨迹与其初始噪声潜变量关联并施加扰动以提升对累积误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 推理时每一步都输出可识别图像，支持早停，并形成可控的速度—多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 以"可识别轨迹"替代传统扩散的损坏中间态，为生成建模提供了新范式，兼顾中间态可解释性、早停能力与多样性调节。

Abstract: While diffusion models excel at image generation, their denoising trajectories pass through corrupted intermediate states far from the image manifold. We ask whether generation can instead proceed through recognizable images throughout the entire trajectory. We introduce a generative framework built around a variation-reducing degradation process that contracts diverse images toward a shared prototype while preserving recognizable intermediate states. Because such manifold traversal lacks a simple analytical form, we construct the trajectories using generative-prior-based image morphing. We then distill the reverse process into a diversification operator that transforms the prototype into increasingly varied samples. To resolve the one-to-many ambiguity of the reverse process, we associate each trajectory with its initial noise latent and apply perturbations to improve robustness against accumulated errors. At inference, every step yields a recognizable image, allowing early termination and enabling a controllable speed--variation trade-off.

</details>


### [49] [Signal Scaffolding: Unlocking the Potential of Lightweight Models for Self-Supervised Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17828)
*Zhijie Wang,Li Zhang,Yujie Chen,Shibiao Deng,Xin Gao,Houzhi Zhou,Jiangshuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Signal Scaffolding (SS) 三阶段训练策略，用于轻量级自监督单目深度估计，在不增加数据与参数的前提下提升 ReForgeDepth 的精度-效率权衡，并在边缘设备实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备需求增长推动轻量自监督单目深度估计发展，但现有方法精度普遍较差。作者认为根因在于常规训练范式未能充分挖掘轻量模型的固有潜力，而非参数分配问题。

Method: 设计三阶段训练策略 Signal Scaffolding (SS)，并将其应用于自研轻量网络 ReForgeDepth；SS 不引入额外数据集或额外参数。

Result: 在 KITTI 与 Cityscapes 数据集上取得精度-效率权衡的 SOTA，并可在边缘设备上实时运行。

Conclusion: 轻量模型在既有容量内仍有提升空间；核心问题或许不在于参数如何分配，而在于如何通过训练释放其潜在能力。

Abstract: With the rising demand for edge devices, lightweight self-supervised monocular depth estimation methods have continued to evolve; however, existing methods generally suffer from poor accuracy. We find that the root cause may lies in the failure of conventional training regimes to fully exploit the inherent potential of lightweight models. To address this, we propose a novel three-phase training strategy for lightweight self-supervised monocular depth estimation, termed Signal Scaffolding (SS). Without introducing any additional dataset or parameters, we apply SS to ReForgeDepth, a lightweight network of our own design. Experiments on the KITTI and Cityscapes datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in the accuracy-efficiency trade-off and running in real time on edge devices. These results suggest that lightweight models can be improved further within their existing capacity, and that the central question may be less about how parameters are allocated than about how to unlock this latent potential through training.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Endpoints: Bézier Paths for Vision Foundation Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17825)
*Chenzhi Zheng,Xi Xiao,Chih-Ting Liao,Qizhen Lan,Jingwen Lin,Tianyang Wang,Hao Xu,Xin Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究共享提示（shared prompts）在适配冻结视觉基础模型时能否沿特征空间最短路径移动，而非仅看端点可达性。作者在可解析注意力模型中证明，可达特征构成一个锥，展开为平面后可得到任意端点对的最小作用量；发现内容归一化存在尖锐阈值，同号端点在至多两个不同层级时才能走"测地线"，三个以上层级必然产生严格正额外代价。ViT-B/16 + VTAB-1k 上，最小化特征作用量的二次Bézier提示路径优于线性与交叉熵训练路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有理论只关心提示能把图像特征推到哪些端点，而提示随数据漂移或任务变化被更新时，特征会从一个端点移动到另一个端点；问题在于能否沿最短路径迁移。由于共享提示与每张图像自身内容争夺注意力，往往无法走最短路径，需要量化这一限制。

Method: 将共享提示与图像内容的注意力竞争建模为可精确求解的注意力模型：可达特征集合构成锥，将其展开为平面后解析计算每对端点间的最小作用量，并给出短弧上的显式最优提示；以"共享注意力相对最短路径带来的额外传输代价"为度量，分析内容归一化（图像自身 token 吸收的注意力比例）的影响；在 ViT-B/16 + VTAB-1k 上，用最小化特征作用量的二次Bézier 路径规划提示轨迹。

Result: 内容归一化存在尖锐阈值：同号端点下至多两个不同层级可使提示走最短路径，三个及以上层级则除非两端点仅差缩放，必有严格正的额外代价；该间隙在更多提示、小内容扰动、对齐多头输出下依然存在。实验上，二次Bézier 路径相对线性与交叉熵训练路径降低了留出传输作用量，并在无标签分布漂移下提升中间与在线准确率；但若有标注校准数据，交叉熵训练路径的最低准确率更高。

Conclusion: 可达端点不等于可达最短路径：提示如何移动（路径）与停在哪里（端点）同等重要，路径规划应纳入提示迁移设计；简单线性或端到端交叉熵训练可能引入非最小的传输代价。

Abstract: Frozen vision foundation models can be adapted by learning a few prompt tokens shared across all images. When prompts are updated as data shift or tasks change, the distribution of image features moves from one endpoint to another. Prior theory asks which endpoints prompts can reach; we ask whether they can travel between them along the shortest path. Because shared prompts compete with each image's own content for attention, they often cannot. We quantify this restriction as the extra transport cost that shared attention imposes over the shortest path. In an exactly solvable attention model, the reachable features form a cone; unrolling it into a plane gives the exact minimum action for every pair of endpoints and explicit optimal prompts on short arcs. The analysis reveals a sharp threshold in content normalization, which measures how much attention each image's own tokens absorb: for same-sign endpoints, at most two distinct levels allow prompts to follow the shortest path, whereas three or more force a strictly positive extra cost unless the endpoints differ only by rescaling. This gap persists with more prompts, small content perturbations, and aligned multihead outputs. On ViT-B/16 with VTAB-1k, a quadratic Bézier prompt path that minimizes feature action reduces held-out transport action relative to linear and cross-entropy-trained paths between the same endpoints and improves intermediate and online accuracy under unlabeled distribution shift, while cross-entropy-trained paths retain higher minimum accuracy when labeled calibration data are available. Reachable endpoints do not guarantee a reachable shortest path: how prompts move matters, not only where they end. Project page: https://xixiaouab.github.io/Beyond-Endpoints/.

</details>


### [51] [HarmonyText: Harmonious Scene Text Editing via Reverse-to-Refined Pair Synthesis and Region-Decoupled Frequency Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17733)
*Ziqi Yi,Huiguo He,Guitao Xu,Peirong Zhang,Cheng Da,Xinyao Yu,Zhanpeng Wang,Changqian Yu,Huan Yang,Kun Gai,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无掩码自然语言引导场景文本编辑框架HarmonyText，通过区域解耦优化(RDO)与级联高频纹理引导(CHFTG)解决伪GT错位与生成过平滑问题，并构建STE25K数据集与免GT评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言引导的无掩码场景文本编辑受限于三类问题：(1)伪GT监督不可靠，伪目标与背景存在差异；(2)基于MSE的速度回归抑制高频细节，导致文字边界模糊、局部纹理弱化；(3)缺乏免GT评测手段。

Method: 1) Region-Decoupling Optimization (RDO)：编辑区域对齐伪目标、保留区域锚定源图，缓解伪GT背景差异干扰；2) Cascaded High-Frequency Texture Guidance (CHFTG)：在小波域施加级联高频约束，保持锐利边界与细粒度纹理；3) Reverse-to-Refined Pair Synthesis (RePair)：合成高质量25K配对数据集STE25K；4) 免GT评测协议：颜色分布散度(Color Distribution Divergence)与Harmony Assessment Model。

Result: 大量实验表明，HarmonyText在文本编辑准确性、背景保真度、颜色一致性与感知和谐度上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 区域与频率感知的监督设计可有效突破伪GT偏差与过平滑瓶颈，配合高质量配对数据与免GT评测，为无掩码场景文本编辑提供了更可靠的训练与评价范式。

Abstract: Natural language-guided, mask-free scene text editing aims to modify text in real-world images using only textual instructions, yet remains challenged by unreliable pseudo-GT supervision, over-smoothed text generation, and the absence of GT-free evaluation. Existing methods often rely on imperfect pseudo-targets with background discrepancies, while standard MSE-based velocity regression tends to suppress high-frequency details, leading to blurred text boundaries and weakened local textures. To address these issues, we propose HarmonyText, a mask-free scene text editing framework with region- and frequency-aware supervision. HarmonyText introduces Region-Decoupling Optimization (RDO), which aligns edited text regions with pseudo-targets while anchoring preserved regions to the source image, and Cascaded High-Frequency Texture Guidance (CHFTG), which applies cascaded wavelet-domain constraints to preserve sharp boundaries and fine-grained textures of textual content. We further propose Reverse-to-Refined Pair Synthesis (RePair) to construct STE25K, a high-quality 25K paired dataset, and introduce a GT-free evaluation protocol with Color Distribution Divergence and a Harmony Assessment Model. Extensive experiments show that HarmonyText outperforms existing methods in text editing accuracy, background fidelity, color consistency, and perceptual harmony.

</details>


### [52] [RaFL: An Empirical Study of Random Filter Losses for Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17707)
*Ruibo Wang,Fengbei Liu,Aleksei Tiulpin,Mert R. Sabuncu,Heejong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出随机滤波器损失RaFL，通过随机权重滤波比较预测与目标，无需预训练特征或损失权重调参，在5个2D/3D重建任务上优于或持平像素损失，开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素损失是目标分布的点统计量，不确定下会平均掉细节纹理；感知损失依赖域特定的预训练特征。需要一种既避免预训练特征、又免去任务特定损失工程与权重网格搜索的域无关损失。

Method: RaFL结合随机卷积、随机Fourier特征与随机小波滤波器，并通过梯度范数匹配自动平衡各分量贡献，消除人工损失加权。系统考察三个设计选择：滤波器族、权重重采样调度、权重初始化；依据验证PSNR与随机种子稳定性选定：使用全部三类滤波器、每步重抽滤波器、高斯初始化。

Result: 在3个2D与2个3D重建任务上，RaFL在3个任务上优于像素损失、2个持平，且5个任务跨随机种子性能稳定。开销仅增加骨干前向FLOPs的<2%，无推理成本，损失FLOPs比VGG19感知损失少182倍。

Conclusion: RaFL是一种简单、免调参、域无关的2D与3D图像重建损失目标。

Abstract: Pixel losses train image reconstruction networks toward a point statistic of the target distribution, often averaging away fine textures under uncertainty. Perceptual losses alleviate this issue using pretrained features, but those features are inherently domain-specific. We study a random filter loss (RaFL) that compares prediction and target through filters with random weights, avoiding both pretrained features and task-specific loss engineering. RaFL combines random convolutions, random Fourier features, and random wavelet filters, automatically balancing their contributions through gradient-norm matching, eliminating manual loss-weight tuning and grid search. We systematically investigate three design choices: filter family, weight resampling schedule, and weight initialization. Based on validation PSNR and stability across random seeds, we select the configuration that uses all three filter families, redraws filters every optimization step, and employs Gaussian initialization. We evaluate RaFL on three 2D and two 3D image reconstruction tasks. RaFL outperforms a pixel loss on three tasks and matches it on two, and demonstrates stable performance across random seeds on all five tasks. RaFL adds less than 2% to the backbone's forward FLOPs and no inference cost, requiring $182\times$ fewer loss FLOPs than a VGG19 perceptual loss. These findings suggest that RaFL is a simple, tuning-free, and domain-agnostic loss objective for 2D and 3D image reconstruction.

</details>


### [53] [Provable Cross-Modal Transfer of Hierarchical Concept Structure in CLIP Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17671)
*Nghia Nguyen,Tianjiao Ding,Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文将CLIP嵌入展现出的与语义概念层级一致的结构形式化为"层级角度分离"(HAS)几何性质，并从理论与实验两方面解释：在图像嵌入固定、仅训练文本嵌入的无约束特征模型中，全局最优解处文本侧HAS违反比例不超过图像侧违反与近违反比例，其机制是图文对的层级共现结构使文本嵌入偏向以根为中心的原型；对线性对比损失给出闭式解，并推广到InfoNCE损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等对比视觉-语言模型的训练目标是图文对齐而非表征分类体系（taxonomy），但实证显示其嵌入呈现与语义概念层级一致的结构。为何非层级目标会涌现层级结构，缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 提出HAS（层级角度分离）定义：每个非叶概念嵌入与其后代的相似度高于与异支概念。实验观察原始图像与随机初始化CLIP图像嵌入近似满足HAS，而文本嵌入仅在对比学习后近似满足。理论采用图像嵌入固定、仅训练文本嵌入的无约束特征模型；利用图文对的层级共现结构分析全局极小点，对线性对比损失推导闭式解，并扩展至InfoNCE损失（在充分正则化条件下）。

Result: 全局极小点处，每个节点文本侧HAS违反比例不超过图像侧HAS违反与近违反比例；线性损失下有显式闭式解；InfoNCE在充分正则化下获得类似保证；合成实验验证理论，并显示HAS可迁移至分析未覆盖的情形。

Conclusion: 层级结构可从非层级对比目标中由共现结构自然产生，图像的既有层级性被"转移"到文本嵌入，为CLIP的层级语义特性提供了理论解释。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language models such as CLIP are trained to align images and text, not to represent taxonomies. Yet empirical studies show that CLIP embeddings exhibit hierarchical structure aligned with the hierarchy of semantic concepts. A theoretical characterization of why such hierarchy emerges from a non-hierarchical objective remains open. In this paper, we formalize this phenomenon as Hierarchical Angular Separation (HAS), a geometric property whereby each non-leaf concept embedding is more similar to its descendants than to off-branch concepts. Empirically, we show that HAS holds approximately in raw images and randomly initialized CLIP image embeddings, while in text embeddings it holds approximately only after contrastive learning. To theoretically explain this asymmetry, we analyze an unconstrained features model in which the image embeddings are fixed and only the text embeddings are trained. At the global minimizer, the text-side HAS violation percentage at each node is at most the image-side HAS violation and near-violation percentage. The key mechanism is the hierarchical co-occurrence structure of image-text pairs, which biases text embeddings toward root-centered image prototypes. For a linear contrastive loss, we derive a closed-form solution that makes this mechanism explicit. We extend the analysis to the InfoNCE loss and establish analogous guarantees under sufficient regularization. Synthetic experiments validate the theory and show that HAS is transferred even beyond the regimes covered by our analysis.

</details>


### [54] [Toward On-Device Markerless 6DoF Tracking of a Physical Manipulative on a Mixed-Reality Headset: A Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18438)
*Guangxi Feng,Philip Yasskin,Wei Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AR-Classroom 在 Meta Quest 3 上实现无标记 6DoF 追踪：YOLO 从 128×128 RGB 裁剪预测 LEGO 飞机 20 个关键点，PnP 解算位姿。裁剪使检出率由 32.5% 升至 46.2%，关键点 F1 由 42.9% 升至 44.6%，2 Hz 追踪下保持约 72 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR-Classroom 需用物理模型+虚拟叠加辅助学习者探索空间变换，但独立头显上叠加层需与实物精确对齐，故需为 LEGO 飞机设计无标记 6DoF 追踪器。

Method: 基于合成 Unity 渲染训练紧凑 YOLO 模型，从动态 RGB 裁剪（缩放至 128×128）预测 20 个关键点；再用 PnP 求解器结合 3D 地标估计位姿；在 1000 张合成验证图上做配对评估，并进行头显初步测试与演示。

Result: 真值框引导的裁剪使标注图像检出率从 32.5% 提升至 46.2%，关键点平均 F1 从 42.9% 提升至 44.6%，但裁剪视图下关键点精度略降；头显测试中 2 Hz 为最高测试追踪目标，此时应用维持约 72 FPS；演示显示手动旋转前后虚拟叠加均能对齐实物飞机。

Conclusion: 裁剪引导可有效提升检出率与 F1，系统在低追踪频率下满足实时渲染需求；真实头显素材上的定量 6DoF 位姿精度留待后续工作。

Abstract: AR-Classroom uses physical models and virtual overlays to help learners explore spatial transformations. To align these overlays on a standalone headset, we developed a markerless six-degree-of-freedom(6DoF) tracker for a LEGO airplane on the Meta Quest 3. A compact YOLO model trained on synthetic Unity renders predicts 20 keypoints from dynamic RGB crops resized to 128 by 128 pixels. A perspective-n-point solver estimates the airplane's pose from these predictions and the corresponding 3D landmarks. In a paired evaluation on 1,000 synthetic validation images, cropping guided by ground-truth boxes increased the detection rate on annotated images from 32.5 to 46.2 percent. Mean keypoint F1 increased from 42.9 to 44.6 percent, with lower keypoint precision in the cropped view. In preliminary headset tests, 2 Hz was the highest tested tracking target at which the application maintained approximately 72 frames per second. A headset demonstration illustrates virtual overlay alignment with the physical airplane before and after a manual rotation. Quantitative 6DoF pose accuracy on real headset footage will be measured in future work.

</details>


### [55] [Structural Self-Teaching for Compositional Generation in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18261)
*Jaeyoo Park,Bohyung Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SST 将统一多模态模型理解路径的短语级定位能力转化为生成路径的结构监督，无需外部标注与推理开销即可提升组合式图像生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式统一多模态模型（UMM）的生成路径在密集组合提示下易出现物体缺失、属性绑定错误与空间错位，而其理解路径却能对同一提示进行准确的短语级定位，这一能力未被视为监督来源。

Method: 提出 Structural Self-Teaching：从理解路径的内部注意力图提取短语条件支撑掩码，并通过两种机制利用：(1) 短语局部对比对齐，以掩码为池化区域，将生成的短语区域与目标图像同一区域对齐；(2) 结构脚手架，由同一掩码构建短语级布局，在训练中调制生成路径的中间空间状态，并逐步退火、推理时移除。

Result: 在多个 UMM 上实验表明，SST 在相同数据下优于 LoRA 微调的组合保真度，且推理阶段无额外输入与计算成本。

Conclusion: 利用模型自身理解路径作为结构监督是提升组合式生成的有效途径，训练时仅需内部信号，避免了外部结构标注，并保持推理零开销。

Abstract: We propose Structural Self-Teaching (SST), a novel framework that enhances compositional image generation in diffusion-based unified multimodal models (UMMs) by leveraging their internal understanding path as a source of structural supervision. The generation path of UMMs often struggles with dense compositional prompts, leading to missing objects, attribute binding failures, and spatial errors. In contrast, their understanding path localizes the same phrases in images that satisfy these prompts. SST converts this phrase-level grounding into training-time structural signals for the generation path. Specifically, we extract phrase-conditioned support masks from internal attention maps derived from the understanding path and employ them through two mechanisms. Phrase-local contrastive alignment uses these masks as pooling regions and aligns each generated phrase region with the same region of the target image. A structural scaffold builds a phrase-level layout from the same masks and modulates an intermediate spatial state of the generation path during training. The scaffold is phased out during training and removed at inference. SST requires no external structural annotations for training and no additional input at test time. Extensive experiments across multiple UMMs demonstrate that SST improves compositional fidelity over LoRA fine-tuning on the same data, with no inference-time cost. Project page is available at https://bellos1203.github.io/sst

</details>


### [56] [SCAR: Bridging Coarse Retrieval and Fine Localization through Candidate Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18240)
*Shuhan Dong,Tianyi Shang,Zhenyu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCAR（空间一致性感知重排序），将"候选单元格—Fine坐标"对视为定位假设，在骨干网络实际Fine点处读取证据并以23维特征训练共享打分器，在不改变已有坐标的前提下对候选假设重排序，从而缓解检索顺序主导最终输出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言点云定位通常先检索候选地图单元再在单元内预测坐标，最终输出由检索排序决定；但检索分数只衡量区域—描述相关性，并不反映该区域产出的坐标是否准确——相关区域可能给出偏移预测，排序较低的区域反而可能包含成功定位。因此需研究候选池与Fine预测固定后的最终选择问题。

Method: SCAR把每个"单元格—Fine坐标"对当作一个定位假设；将方向关系解释为位置的函数，并在骨干实际Fine点处读取空间支持（Fine条件化证据读取）；把中心支持、语义存在、检索分数与几何偏移组织为23维表示；用定位成功标签训练共享打分器，推理时重排现有假设并返回其原始坐标。

Result: 实验覆盖四个公开骨干及修正版Des4Pos复现，跨五个域。除一个协议参照配置外，其余24个配置的探索性TEST点估计中，Top-1@5m有23个优于Original，Cell R@1全部24个优于Original。开发集VAL上，在修正版Des4Pos*KITTI上Fine读取较中心代理提升Top-1@5m 5.77pp；在MambaPlace×nuScenesFull上完整打分方案较FSR提升13.98pp（后者衡量的是完整方案而非仅学习排序）。

Conclusion: 在候选与Fine预测固定时，通过空间一致性感知的重排序可稳定改善最终坐标选择，方向关系应作为位置函数在实际Fine点处评估；增益在多数骨干与域上成立，但上述TEST结果为探索性点估计，需进一步验证其稳健性。

Abstract: Natural-language point-cloud localization typically retrieves candidate map cells and then predicts a position inside each candidate, yet the final output is still determined by retrieval order. A retrieval score measures region-description relevance, not whether the coordinates a region yields are accurate: a relevant region may produce a displaced prediction, and a lower-ranked region may contain a successful one. We study the final selection problem after the candidate pool and Fine predictions are fixed---how to choose the coordinate most likely to localize successfully, and under which conditions the gain holds. We propose SCAR (Spatial Consistency-Aware Reranking). SCAR treats each cell--Fine-coordinate pair as a localization hypothesis, interprets directional relations as functions of position, and reads spatial support at the backbone's actual Fine point (Fine-conditioned Evidence Readout). It organizes this readout with center support, semantic presence, retrieval score, and geometric offsets into a 23-D representation, trains a shared scorer with localization-success labels, and at inference reranks existing hypotheses and returns their original coordinates. Experiments cover four public backbones and a corrected Des4Pos reimplementation across five domains. Excluding one protocol-reference configuration, exploratory TEST point estimates on the remaining 24 configurations show Top-1@5\,m above Original on 23 and Cell R@1 above Original on all 24. On development-set VAL, the Fine readout improves Top-1@5\,m by 5.77 percentage points (pp) over a center surrogate on corrected Des4Pos*KITTI, and the complete scoring scheme improves it by 13.98\,pp over FSR on MambaPlace$\times$nuScenesFull; the latter measures the complete scheme, not learned ranking alone.

</details>


### [57] [DIRT: Depth-completed Image-based Rendering using Two-band Blending for Live Novel-view Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18340)
*Mathias Harrer,Laura Fink,Noah Lewis,Paul Himmler,Tim Weyrich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练、免优化的实时新视角合成方法，基于稀疏点几何在屏幕空间补全后进行频率感知双频段融合，实现低延迟渲染并适配多种传感器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先进NVS方法需要逐场景优化，难以用于直播/即时场景；而实时NVS要求低延迟与强泛化能力。此前基于训练或优化的适配方案以质量换低延迟，且依赖网络泛化能力。

Method: 遵循传统基于图像的渲染思路：从主动深度传感器获取显式稀疏点几何，在目标视角屏幕空间补全稀疏深度缓冲作为代理几何；将已定位图像投影至目标帧，再用频率感知的双频段融合策略合成。整个流程非神经、无优化，无需训练数据或场景特定优化。

Result: 在手持RGB-D、车载LiDAR、热成像等异构传感器配置及低光等挑战场景下验证有效；实时重建场景中优于既有方法，在常规NVS基准上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法降低了采集到渲染的延迟，摆脱对网络泛化与训练数据的依赖，具备跨传感器适用性，为实时/即时NVS提供了一条可行且高质量的几何驱动路线。

Abstract: State-of-the-art novel-view synthesis (NVS) methods commonly require per-scene optimization before rendering, limiting their applicability in live settings. Live or ad-hoc NVS, however, demands low-latency rendering during capture and strong generalization to new scenes. Some previous work tried to address this task by adapting training- and optimization-based methods, trading quality for low latency. We introduce a live NVS approach following traditional image-based rendering that operates on a posed set of depth sensor observations and images. Our method leverages explicit, sparse point-based geometry obtained from active sensors, resulting in a sparsely filled depth buffer that is completed in the (novel) target view's screen-space. Using this proxy, a posed set of images is projected into the target frame before fusing the resulting observations through a frequency-aware two-band blending strategy. The pipeline is entirely non-neural and optimization-free, requiring no training data or scene-specific optimization. As a result, our approach reduces the delay between capture and novel-view rendering and does not rely on favorable network generalization. We validate the method's applicability to heterogeneous sensor setups - including handheld RGB-D cameras, automotive LiDAR, and thermal cameras - as well as challenging scenarios, such as low light captures. Our experiments demonstrate that the approach outperforms prior methods in live reconstruction scenarios, while maintaining competitive performance in conventional NVS benchmarks.

</details>


### [58] [Cracks in Embeddings: Robustness and Stability of JEPA-Based Chest X-Ray Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17905)
*Zahid Mansur,Kalpana Yadav,Deepak Mishra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统评测了4个基于JEPA的胸片编码器在对抗攻击、图像损坏与标签稀缺下的鲁棒性，并提出在冻结编码器上训练小型预测器（对抗微调）以显著恢复嵌入一致性与AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA类自监督模型在医学影像中应用日增，但其对真实世界图像变化的鲁棒性认识不足。

Method: 在NIH ChestX-ray14（78,468张）上训练的4个JEPA胸片编码器，接受六项测试：两种黑盒对抗攻击（SPSA、Boundary）、三种图像损坏（高斯噪声、亮度偏移、对比度偏移）、一项标签稀缺测试；度量嵌入变化（余弦相似度）与分类AUROC。并比较两种在线性/小型预测器层面上的应对方案：混合干净与扰动图像的对抗微调，以及仅用干净图像训练的向量量化码本方法。

Result: 扰动造成严重且模型依赖的性能损伤：编码器层余弦相似度最低降至0.33，AUROC最大下降0.12（I-JEPA在Boundary测试下）。对抗微调在几乎所有情况下将嵌入一致性提升至0.97以上，并在多数模型上提升AUROC，最大增益+0.10（I-JEPA）。VQ码本方法在四种模型上均随失真退化，I-JEPA平均AUROC下降0.15。

Conclusion: 仅靠离散瓶颈不足以保证鲁棒性；直接使用扰动数据进行对抗微调是更有效的策略。

Abstract: Self-supervised Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) models are increasingly used for medical imaging, however there is a limited understanding about robustness of these models toward real-world image changes. We test four JEPA-based chest X-ray encoders, trained on 78,468 NIH ChestX-ray14 images, under six tests: two black-box adversarial attacks (SPSA, Boundary Attack), three image corruptions (Gaussian noise, brightness shift, contrast shift), and a label-scarcity test, measuring embedding change and classification AUROC. These perturbations cause severe, model-dependent damage: encoder-level Cosine Similarity drops as low as 0.33, and AUROC falls by as much as 0.12 in the worst case (I-JEPA under the Boundary Test). We test two ways of training the small predictor on top of each frozen encoder to address this. Adversarial fine-tuning, trained on clean and perturbed images together, raises embedding consistency above 0.97 in nearly every case and improves AUROC, relative to the same perturbed baseline, for most models, with the largest gain of $+0.10$ on I-JEPA. A Vector-Quantized codebook method, trained only on clean images and evaluated on perturbed test images for the first time, instead degrades under distortion for all four models, most noticeably for I-JEPA ($-0.15$ AUROC on average), showing that its discrete bottleneck alone does not provide the robustness that comes from training on perturbed data directly.

</details>


### [59] [Where to Predict in Retinal OCT? Anatomy-Guided Target Selection for I-JEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17586)
*Yunchu Feng,Wai Tak Lau,Kaveri A. Thakoor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在保持I-JEPA架构与潜空间预测目标不变的前提下，探索基于图像强度与预测视网膜分割的解剖引导目标区域选择，用于视网膜OCT自监督表征学习；线性探针青光眼分类实验中，强度引导的矩形目标取得最高AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: I-JEPA通过从可见上下文预测目标区域表征而非重建像素来学习表征，但其均匀的目标区域放置忽略了视网膜OCT的解剖结构——视网膜组织仅占据每张B-scan的一部分，导致大量目标落在无信息背景区域。

Method: 保留I-JEPA架构与潜空间预测目标，仅改变目标区域选择策略：利用图像强度与预测的视网膜分割生成解剖引导目标；对比三类方案——解剖引导矩形目标、解剖形状目标、覆盖度约束放置，与均匀掩码基线比较；在FairVision数据集上以线性探针评估青光眼分类性能。

Result: 在对比方案中，由图像强度引导的矩形目标取得了最高的线性探针AUC；更高的解剖特异性（如解剖形状目标）并未带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 解剖学意义的目标选择对视网膜OCT表征学习具有前景，但过度追求解剖形状特异性并非必要；简单、由强度引导的矩形目标已足够有效。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning aims to learn meaningful representations from unlabeled data, potentially allowing models to capture subtle anatomical variations across patients and reduce the amount of labeled data needed. Image-based Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (I-JEPA) learns by predicting target-region representations from visible context rather than reconstructing pixels. However, its uniform target placement does not account for the anatomical structure of retinal OCT, where retinal tissue occupies only part of each B-scan. We investigate anatomy-guided target selection using image intensity and predicted retinal segmentation while retaining the I-JEPA architecture and latent prediction objective. We compare anatomy-guided rectangular targets, anatomy-shaped targets and coverage-constrained placement with uniform masking, and evaluate the learned representations using linear probing for glaucoma classification on FairVision. We found that rectangular targets guided by image intensity achieved the highest linear-probe AUC in our comparisons. We show that anatomically meaningful target selection is promising for retinal OCT representation learning, while greater anatomical specificity did not consistently improve performance.

</details>


### [60] [Deep Bundle Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18256)
*Pak Hong Chui,Hector Andrade-Loarca,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeepBA 是一个仅 2.04M 参数的图神经网络，将 bundle adjustment 摊销为可学习的迭代图网络，可从任意数量视图的 2D 匹配中恢复相机位姿、内参与场景结构，冷启动收敛，并与匹配器解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 SfM 需逐场景求解非线性最小二乘问题，更新解析计算且依赖精细初始化；现有学习式 SfM 与匹配器耦合或依赖归一化。作者希望用学习方式摊销 BA，实现冷启动、可跨不同度量尺度数据集混合训练、且模块化的方案。

Method: 构建 GNN，以几何残差为输入迭代精化位姿/内参/结构估计，学习经典 BA 的解析更新；采用尺度不变的监督（真值旋转、平移方向、内参）加重投影自一致性，从而混合任意度量单位的数据集而无需逐场景归一化；模型与匹配器解耦。

Result: 在场景重建和多数位姿指标上优于先前基于匹配的学习式 SfM；在中等等精度阈值下与经典 SfM 相当，并随视图数增加在运行时间上反超；以约 1.2B 参数的大型图像到几何 Transformer（如 VGGT）为目标，以更高模块化程度接近其性能。

Conclusion: 将 BA 学习化为轻量图网络是可行且高效的：冷启动、尺度不变监督、匹配器无关的模块化设计，使其在精度与效率间取得竞争力，并显示摊销优化相对逐场景数值优化的优势。

Abstract: We present \textbf{DeepBA} (\textbf{Deep} \textbf{B}undle \textbf{A}djustment), a graph neural network (GNN) that recovers camera poses, intrinsics, and scene structure from 2D matches across an arbitrary number of views. Rather than solving a non-linear least-squares problem per scene, DeepBA amortizes bundle adjustment into a learned graph network that iteratively refines its estimate from geometric residuals. Where classical SfM computes its updates analytically and needs careful initialization, DeepBA learns them and converges from a cold start. It is trained with scale-invariant supervision (ground-truth rotation, translation direction, and intrinsics, plus reprojection self-consistency), so datasets with arbitrary metric units can be mixed without per-scene normalization. DeepBA is decoupled from the matcher. At 2.04M parameters, it outperforms prior learned SfM from matches on scene reconstruction and on most pose metrics, is competitive with classical SfM at moderate accuracy thresholds and overtakes it in wall time as the view count grows, and approaches monolithic image-to-geometry transformers such as VGGT ($\sim$1.2B parameters) with greater modularity. Project~page: https://cphjsn.github.io/deep-ba/

</details>


### [61] [InfiHall-Bench: A Benchmark for Hallucination Evaluation in Autoregressive Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18053)
*Yuxia Qiao,Xinhao Zhong,Junhao Li,Yi Sun,Jiaxin Hong,Shuoyang Sun,Bin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向视觉自回归(VAR)文生图模型的幻觉基准 InfiHall-Bench（411条提示，基于5类可验证技能组合构建），揭示VAR幻觉的"尺度持续性"现象，并提出置信度掩码幻觉缓解方法CMHM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图幻觉基准主要针对扩散模型，对VAR模型的诊断价值有限；且提示构建存在两大缺陷：部分提示对现代生成器已不具挑战性；类别式构建依赖固定的"物体-属性-关系"模板，很少测试单张图内多视觉要求的交互。

Method: 系统组合五种基础且视觉可验证的技能（属性绑定、空间关系、动作关系、部件一致性、场景一致性）构建411条受控提示，平均每提示覆盖4.05个标注技能；基于该基准分析发现VAR幻觉呈尺度级持续模式（语义错误在不同自回归尺度产生且很少被后续细尺度步骤纠正）；据此提出CMHM，在教师强制微调中估计各尺度目标视觉token的预测置信度，降低可靠预测token权重，使优化聚焦于跨尺度的不确定预测。

Result: InfiHall-Bench 能区分被现有组合性基准评为相当的模型；CMHM 在 VAR 文生图任务上稳定优于均匀 LoRA 微调。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了VAR模型幻觉评测的空白，揭示了尺度持续性这一关键机制，并给出与之匹配的轻量缓解方案，兼具诊断与改进价值。

Abstract: Hallucination benchmarks for text-to-image generation are predominantly designed around diffusion models, limiting their diagnostic value for visual autoregressive (VAR) models and the development of mitigation methods. We identify two limitations in existing prompt construction paradigms: many prompts are no longer challenging for modern generators, while category-based construction relies on recurring object-attribute-relation templates and rarely tests interactions among multiple visual requirements within a single image.
  We introduce InfiHall-Bench, a controlled benchmark of 411 prompts constructed by systematically composing five foundational and visually verifiable skills: attribute binding, spatial relation, action relation, part consistency, and scene consistency. Each prompt covers 4.05 annotated skills on average, enabling the evaluation of hallucinations under diverse combinations of visual requirements. Using this benchmark, we find that hallucinations in VAR models exhibit a scale-wise persistence pattern: semantic errors can emerge at different autoregressive scales and are rarely corrected by subsequent fine-scale steps.
  Motivated by this observation, we propose Confidence-Masked Hallucination Mitigation (CMHM), which estimates the prediction confidence of target visual tokens at each autoregressive scale during teacher-forced fine-tuning. CMHM downweights reliably predicted tokens, allowing optimization to focus on uncertain predictions across scales. Experiments show that InfiHall-Bench distinguishes models that existing compositional benchmarks rate as comparable, while CMHM consistently outperforms uniform LoRA fine-tuning for VAR-based text-to-image generation.

</details>


### [62] [Multi-level latent-diffusion adaptation for cross-region point cloud segmentation of ancient terraces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18033)
*Sina Mohammadi,Roderik Lindenbergh,Javier Ruiz-Pérez,Marco Madella,Sebastian Fajardo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MELD方法，通过多层级嵌入空间潜扩散实现跨区域机载LiDAR古代梯田检测的无监督域适应，无需目标域标签即可显著提升F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化LiDAR特征检测可支撑考古调查，但在一个景观上训练的模型往往因地形、植被、采集特性和特征形态差异而难以迁移到另一个景观，且目标域缺乏标注数据。

Method: 采用转导式无监督域适应，利用未标注的目标域测绘数据。提出MELD（多层级嵌入空间潜扩散），在PointNet++的四个层级特征上分别训练目标域扩散去噪器，将其作为目标分布先验，在监督训练时精炼源域表征、在推理时精炼目标域表征，使适应直接作用于分割解码器所消费的多尺度特征；同时引入基于K近邻邻域的局部几何描述子（表面法向、坡度、坡向）。

Result: 在哥伦比亚Sierra Nevada de Santa Marta的Teyuna与Miraflores两个LiDAR测绘间进行双向评估，对比仅源域模型与九种既有域适应配置。MELD将F1从0.397提升至0.459（Teyuna→Miraflores），反向从0.557提升至0.594，双向均为所有方法中最高；加入局部几何后分别提升至0.542与0.606。多层级消融显示同时对四个PointNet++层级进行精炼在双向均取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 在层级点云特征空间中显式建模目标域特征分布，并辅以局部几何，可在无目标域标签条件下实现有效的跨区域考古制图。

Abstract: Automated lidar feature detection from lidar point clouds can support archaeological surveys, but models trained in one landscape may transfer poorly to another because of differences in terrain, vegetation, acquisition characteristics, and feature morphology. We address this problem for cross-region detection of ancient terraces using transductive unsupervised domain adaptation that exploits an unlabelled target survey. We propose MELD (Multi-level Embedding-space Latent Diffusion), which trains separate diffusion denoisers on target-domain representations at four hierarchical PointNet++ feature levels. These target distribution priors are then used to refine source representations during supervised training and target representations during inference, allowing adaptation to act directly on the multi-scale features consumed by the segmentation decoder. We additionally investigate local geometric descriptors derived from K-nearest-neighbour neighbourhoods, including surface normals, slope, and aspect. MELD is evaluated bidirectionally between the Teyuna and Miraflores lidar surveys in the Sierra Nevada de Santa Marta, Colombia, against a source-only model and nine configurations of established domain adaptation approaches. MELD raises F1 over the source-only model from 0.397 to 0.459 for Teyuna-to-Miraflores transfer and from 0.557 to 0.594 in the reverse direction, achieving the highest F1 among the evaluated adaptation methods in both directions. Adding local geometry further raises F1 to 0.542 and 0.606, respectively. Multi-level ablation shows that refining all four PointNet++ levels provides the strongest performance in both directions. These results show that explicit modelling of target feature distributions in hierarchical point cloud feature space, complemented by local geometry, provides an effective route to cross-region archaeological mapping without target labels.

</details>


### [63] [MacroGS: Macro-Pixel Shared Gaussian Primitives for Arbitrary-Scale Light Field Super-Resolutio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17957)
*Zeqiang Wei,Angulia Yang,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MacroGS，一种前馈解码器，将高斯原语从"逐视角独立生成"改为"逐宏像素共享单一集合"，使原语数量与视点数解耦，实现任意尺度光场空间超分并大幅提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯原语方法虽支持连续重建，但为每个视角生成独立原语集合，导致跨视角共享场景结构被冗余编码，且原语数量随视点数线性增长。

Method: 以宏像素为单位，前馈解码器联合预测单一共享高斯集合；通过视差相关的替换机制与角度条件的外观建模，使同一集合可复用于所有输出视角；解码器迭代预测每个原语的视差、检索对应多视图特征并更新原语状态，再由高斯泼溅与残差细化重建输出光场；训练不使用几何监督。

Result: 在五个基准上与多种光场编码器结合均有效；固定尺度重建质量提升，多数任意尺度设置达到领先性能；相比SOTA隐式光场解码器提速约2–10倍，相比逐视角高斯基线提速10–18倍；预测视差与真实场景几何高度相关。

Conclusion: 共享高斯原语表征可在保持任意尺度重建能力的同时消除跨视角冗余、使原语数独立于视点数，兼具质量与效率优势。

Abstract: Arbitrary-scale light field spatial super-resolution reconstructs high-resolution views at any requested scale from low-resolution multi-view observations. Gaussian primitives enable continuous reconstruction, but generating separate primitive sets for each view redundantly encodes shared scene structure across views and causes the primitive count grow linearly with the number of views. To avoid such redundancy, we propose MacroGS, a feed-forward decoder that jointly predicts a single shared Gaussian set per macro pixel rather than generating separate sets for each view. Disparity-dependent replacement and angle-conditioned appearance allow each set to be reused across all output views, keeping the predicted primitive count independent of the number of views at each requested scale. The decoder iteratively predicts per-primitive disparities, retrieves the corresponding multi-view features, and updates the primitive states. Gaussian splatting and residual refinement then reconstruct the output light field. Despite being learned without geometric supervision, the predicted disparities strongly correlate with underlying scene geometry. Experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate that MacroGS integrates effectively with diverse light field encoders, improving reconstruction quality at fixed scales while achieving leading performance across most evaluated arbitrary-scale settings. The decoder also achieves speedups of approximately $2$-$10\times$ over state-of-the-art implicit light field decoders and $10$-$18\times$ over the view-wise Gaussian baseline.

</details>


### [64] [DoubleTake: Focused Verification of Visual Evidence for Long-Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17853)
*Kejun Xue,Ang Li,Jinghua Tang,Xincheng Wang,Yongbin Gao,Yanghua Xiao,Zhijun Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DoubleTake：一种免训练的长文档视觉问答聚焦验证框架，通过"初读—规划事实性问题—盲验—推理"两轮流程提升答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长文档视觉问答中，即使检索到的页面包含充分证据，视觉语言模型仍可能答错；而直接带着原问题重读这些页面，模型难以获知应核对哪一个事实，缺乏聚焦指导。

Method: 免训练框架。(1) 初读产生候选答案，并生成独立的证据记录用于指导二次阅读的检索；(2) Planner 对比两个候选答案及其支撑证据，提炼出一个事实性问题；(3) Verifier 仅依据源图像回答该问题，不接触候选答案与证据记录；(4) Reasoner 基于该观察结果解决原始问题。骨干模型为冻结的 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct。

Result: 在四个基准上，DoubleTake 的整体准确率均优于所评估的其他方法。在使用相同页面与相同模型调用次数的条件下，提出聚焦的事实性问题在四个基准上均比重复提问原问题取得更高准确率。

Conclusion: 将"重读"转化为针对具体事实的聚焦验证，并配合无候选偏见的验证器，可有效提升长文档视觉问答的可靠性，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: In long-document question answering, a vision-language model can answer incorrectly even when the retrieved pages contain sufficient evidence. Rereading those pages with the original question gives the model little guidance about which fact to check. We introduce DoubleTake, a training-free framework for focused verification of visual evidence. An initial reading produces a candidate answer and a separate evidence record that guides retrieval for a second reading. A planner compares the two candidates and their supporting evidence to formulate a factual question. A verifier answers it from the source images without seeing either candidate or evidence record, and a reasoner uses this observation to resolve the original question. With a frozen Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct backbone, DoubleTake achieves the highest overall accuracy among the evaluated methods on each of four benchmarks. With the same pages and number of model calls, asking a focused factual question yields higher accuracy on all four benchmarks than asking the original question again. Our code is available at https://github.com/small-code-cat/double_take.

</details>


### [65] [VACO: Vertex-Aligned Decoding for Dense Hand Contact Estimation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17755)
*Binhong Ye,Jin-Chuan Shi,Tao Liu,Xin Wang,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VACO提出顶点对齐解码器：每个MANO顶点在其投影位置采样局部图像特征以预测稠密手部接触，单一模型在五个基准达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单RGB图像的稠密手部接触估计是灵巧操作与3D交互分析的基础。但现有直接预测器从单一共享query解码整张逐顶点接触图，导致顶点缺乏图像 grounding，接触被扩散到相邻的非接触表面。

Method: 提出vertex-aligned decode：每个MANO顶点在其投影位置采样局部图像特征，使每个顶点与图像对齐。使用联合训练重建与接触的手部重建backbone，推理时直接预测顶点投影位置，无需辅助重建器。训练时采用噪声扰动的GT投影与端到端特征自适应，提升鲁棒性并获得接触敏感特征。

Result: 单一VACO checkpoint在涵盖手-物、手-人、手-场景、双手交互的五个基准上取得新SOTA。在Hi4D上F1较最强直接预测器提升+20.7 pp，并泛化到未见的HOT3D与HOCap，分别提升+5.5与+2.7 pp。所得接触预测亦改善下游HOI重建。

Conclusion: 以逐顶点图像特征采样替代共享query解码可显著缓解接触扩散问题，提升精度的同时增强跨场景泛化能力，并可直接用于下游交互重建。

Abstract: Dense hand contact estimation from a single RGB image is a foundational primitive for dexterous manipulation and 3D interaction analysis. Direct predictors typically decode the entire per-vertex contact map from one shared query, leaving individual vertices ungrounded in the image and smearing contact across adjacent, non-touching surfaces. We present VACO, a vertex-aligned decoder in which each MANO vertex samples local image features at its projected location. A hand-reconstruction backbone, jointly trained for reconstruction and contact, predicts these locations at inference without an auxiliary reconstructor. Training uses noise-perturbed ground-truth projections and end-to-end feature adaptation to improve robustness and contact-sensitive features. A single VACO checkpoint sets a new state of the art on five benchmarks spanning hand-object, hand-human, hand-scene, and bimanual interactions. It improves F1 by +20.7 pp over the strongest prior direct predictor on Hi4D and generalizes to the held-out HOT3D and HOCap, with gains of +5.5 and +2.7 pp. The resulting contact predictions also improve downstream HOI reconstruction.

</details>


### [66] [QCAP-VQA: Question-Conditioned Capped Prior-Mass Supervision for Medical Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17708)
*Yining Zhao,Catarina Moreira,Mukesh Prasad,Ali Braytee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QCAP-VQA，用问题条件化的分割提议先验对部分交叉注意力头施加"封顶先验质量"监督，在不损精度的前提下提升对命名解剖结构的注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VQA模型可能在不关注问题所指解剖结构的情况下答对；自动区域先验可免专家掩码提供空间监督，但本身有噪声，其注意力约束强度难以确定且缺乏可靠的验证方式。

Method: 基于排序分割提议构建问题条件先验，仅监督部分交叉注意力头；采用封顶先验质量目标——仅当先验内注意力低于阈值时才施加惩罚。用SLAKE结构框（不参与训练）做独立评估，区分"对命名解剖的注意力"与"同图不同问题间的注意力转移"；扫描64倍正则强度与13种配置，并与池化区域质量目标对比。

Result: 准确率与无空间监督基线持平（84.26% vs 84.23%）；所有先验监督模型对标注结构注意力更高；正则强度64倍变化下封顶目标将先验质量稳定在0.67–0.77，而池化目标升至0.97且精度下降。代价在问题条件性：约束越宽/越强，注意力转移与专家对比一致性越差；先验参照与专家参照评估的排序近乎相反（Spearman -0.87），被封顶目标偏好的模型会延续先验自身错误。

Conclusion: 封顶先验质量能在维持精度的同时提升解剖定位，但会牺牲问题条件性；自动先验监督的效果必须以独立解剖参照来评判，而非以先验自身为参照。

Abstract: Medical visual question answering models can answer correctly without attending to the anatomy that the question names. Automatic region priors allow spatial supervision without expert masks, but they are noisy, and it is unclear how strongly they should shape attention and how their effect should be verified. We present QCAP-VQA, which supervises a subset of cross-attention heads with a question-conditioned prior built from ranked segmentation proposals. Its capped prior-mass objective penalizes insufficient attention inside the prior only below a threshold. We evaluate attention against SLAKE structure boxes that play no role in training, separating attention on the named anatomy from how attention shifts between questions about the same image. QCAP-VQA matches the accuracy of a baseline without spatial supervision (84.26% vs. 84.23%), and every evaluated prior-supervised model places more attention on the annotated structures. Over a 64-fold range of regularization strength, the capped objective keeps prior mass between 0.67 and 0.77, whereas a pooled region-mass objective drives it to 0.97 and loses accuracy. The cost lies in question conditioning: as the constraint widens or strengthens, attention shifts agree less with the expert-defined contrast. Across 13 configurations, prior-referenced and expert-referenced evaluation rank the models in largely opposite order (Spearman correlation of -0.87), and the capped models it favors track the prior's own errors. Because clinical use of medical visual question answering requires answers grounded in the queried anatomy, supervision from automatic priors should be judged against independent anatomical references.

</details>


### [67] [See and Detect: Image-Driven Open-Vocabulary Multi-View 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17897)
*Yating Xu,Na Zhao,Chenhan Jiang,Qingwen Zhang,Jiankang Deng,Jifei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以多视角图像为主要证据的"See and Detect"框架，用于开放词汇多视角室内3D检测，通过多视角一致性框融合、框质量评估与上下文感知语义学习提升定位与分类，性能SOTA且速度提升500倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇多视角室内3D检测采用点云驱动流程：伪3D框由重建或估计几何生成，语义标签从投影区域推断。该设计使框生成依赖点云完整性，语义学习局限于局部外观，导致3D定位与开放词汇分类效果不佳。

Method: 以多视角图像为主证据：(1) 从单张图像发现物体候选，召回点云缺失的物体；(2) 多视角一致性框融合（multi-view consensus box consolidation）融合跨视角空间一致预测，抑制单视角噪声与冗余；(3) 框质量评估降低部分覆盖框的影响；(4) 上下文感知语义学习，从全图上下文查询语义标签。

Result: 在ScanNet与ARKitScenes上取得开放词汇多视角3D检测的SOTA性能，且运行速度较此前SOTA方法快500倍以上。

Conclusion: 图像驱动范式可有效替代点云驱动的伪框生成，缓解点云不完整与局部外观语义的局限，在精度与效率上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Recent open-vocabulary multi-view indoor 3D detection methods typically follow a point-cloud-driven pipeline, where pseudo 3D boxes are generated from reconstructed or estimated geometry and semantic labels are inferred from projected regions. However, such a design makes box generation dependent on the completeness of point clouds and restricts semantic learning to local appearance, causing inferior 3D object localization and open-vocabulary classification. In this paper, we present See and Detect, an image-driven framework that uses multi-view images as the primary evidence for 3D box and semantic label generation. For box generation, we first discover object candidates from individual images, enabling objects missing in point clouds to be recovered from visual evidence. However, single-view predictions are often noisy and redundant. To address this, we propose multi-view consensus box consolidation to fuse spatially consistent predictions across views, and box quality assessment to reduce the impact of boxes with partial object coverage. For semantic label generation, we introduce context-aware semantic learning, where semantic labels are queried from full image context, enabling more accurate semantic prediction. Extensive experiments on ScanNet and ARKitScenes demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art open-vocabulary multi-view 3D detection performance while running over 500$\times$ faster than the previous state-of-the-art method.

</details>


### [68] [Domain Adaptation Based Pipeline for Character Classification and Handwritten Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17952)
*Florent Imbert,Simon Corbillé,Hui Han,Elisa H. Barney Smith*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于无监督域适应的三阶段流水线，缓解源/目标域分布差异，在字符分类与历史手写文本行识别任务上取得SOTA（Safran-MNIST-DLS 76.17% Macro F1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字符分类与手写文本识别（尤其专业场景）标注数据稀缺，而大量无标注目标域数据未被利用，源域与目标域之间存在分布差异。

Method: 三阶段：1）在源数据上预训练专家模型以获得通用表征；2）对无标注目标数据进行无监督域适应，使模型贴合目标域特性；3）在目标风格化的源数据上重训专家模型。通过对齐源/目标特征分布实现跨数据集泛化；无监督训练仅增加训练耗时，不影响推理。

Result: 在字符级与历史手写文本行识别两类任务上验证了方法的灵活性；在DAGECC竞赛Track 2的Safran-MNIST-DLS数据集上以76.17% Macro F1刷新SOTA，并在READ 2018各具体数据集上在不使用目标标签的情况下取得有竞争力结果。

Conclusion: 所提无监督域适应流水线可有效利用无标注目标数据、缩小域间差距，通用性强且推理阶段无额外开销，适用于低资源字符分类与手写文本识别。

Abstract: Character classification and handwritten text recognition, particularly in specialized tasks, often suffer from limited labeled data, while a large amount of unlabeled data remains unused. To address this challenge, we propose a novel unsupervised domain adaptation-based pipeline to reduce the distribution gap between source and target domains. Our pipeline follows three main steps. In the first step, we pretrained an expert model on source data, to learn a strong general representation. In the second step, we perform unsupervised domain adaptation to leverage unlabeled target data, adapting the model to the specific characteristics of the target domain. In the third step, we trained the expert model on source data in the target style. By aligning the feature distributions between the source and target, our method enables the model to generalize effectively across diverse datasets. The unsupervised training increases the computation time only during training not in inference. We evaluate the pipeline on both character-level and historical handwritten line recognition tasks, demonstrating its flexibility. We establish a new state-of-the-art on the Safran-MNIST-DLS dataset defined in Track 2 of the DAGECC competition with 76.17% Macro F1-score and achieve competitive results on each of the specific datasets of READ 2018 without using the labels of the target data. Code is available at https://github.com/simon-corbi/DRANet-for-classification-and-htr

</details>


### [69] [RSSA: Robust Semantic and Spatial Aligner for Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18023)
*Zhihao Yang,Zhiyu Xiang,Peng Xu,Tianyu Pu,Kai Wang,Eryun Liu,Dongping Zhang,Yong Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出即插即用模块RSSA，通过语义变换与概率空间变换实现V2X协同感知中鲁棒的BEV特征对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X协同感知方法多用简单空间warp对齐协同特征，导致融合时语义错位；且确定性warp对位姿噪声高度敏感。

Method: RSSA包含：SemT块依据观测相对位姿调制特征通道以纠正语义不一致；P-SpaT块在高斯建模的相对位姿空间中采样进行概率warp；并引入RT-Aug相对变换增广策略辅助训练。

Result: 在仿真与真实数据集上，无论干净位姿还是含噪位姿配置，RSSA均显著提升SOTA方法性能。

Conclusion: 语义与概率空间对齐相结合可缓解语义错位与位姿噪声敏感问题，作为插件模块有效增强现有协同感知方法。

Abstract: V2X collaborative perception improves single-vehicle perception by aggregating multi-view features from collaborative agents. However, existing methods typically rely on simple spatial warping to align the collaborative features, which can cause semantic misalignment during fusion. Meanwhile, these deterministic warping-based methods are highly sensitive to the positional noises. In this paper, we propose a plug-in module called Robust Semantic and Spatial Aligner (RSSA), which performs semantic and probabilistic spatial transformations for robust feature alignment. Specifically, a Semantic Transformation (SemT) block modulates feature channels based on the observed relative pose to correct semantic inconsistencies. Subsequently, a Probabilistic Spatial Transformation (P-SpaT) block performs probabilistic warping by sampling from the Gaussian-modeled relative pose space. A Relative Transformation Augmentation (RT-Aug) strategy is further introduced to augment the relative poses and facilitate the training of the RSSA. Extensive experiments on both simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate that RSSA improves the state-of-the-art methods by a large margin under the configuration of both clean and noisy positions.

</details>


### [70] [BAL: Bidirectional Autoregression in Latent Space for Learning Human Movement Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18559)
*Genki Kinoshita,Ko Nishino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BAL：利用日常行为天然时序顺序结构作为内在监督的双向自回归自监督框架，在语义抽象隐空间中预测，用于3D人体动作表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督方法依赖人工假设（一定范围内的增广不变性、特定时间尺度的掩码可恢复性）构造监督，引入启发式超参数，使学到的语义对其设置敏感。

Method: 提出BAL框架：(1) 在语义抽象隐空间中自回归预测，模拟人类预判他人动作而非精确关节配置；(2) 双向（前向与后向）自回归预测，充分利用顺序约束，推理时可同时利用过去与未来上下文；(3) 将每个预测目标分解为捕捉序列级语义的全局分量与捕捉上下文无关运动的局部分量，以抑制表示坍缩；(4) 理论证明局部分量在表示未完全坍缩时保持非平凡的角度多样性。

Result: 在大型数据集上，BAL表示在动作描述生成、运动预测与插值任务中高效，多数评价指标优于基线。

Conclusion: 以人类运动内在的顺序结构替代人工假设作为自监督信号，可得到不依赖启发式超参数的语义化动作表示，并在多下游任务上验证有效性。

Abstract: Learning semantically meaningful human movement representations from 3D pose sequences is essential for human behavior modeling.
