We shift evaluation from asking what score a model achieves toward characterising the processing patterns associated with that score, potentially supporting more targeted benchmark selection and more informed model choice. We open source our code at https://github.com/extraordinarylab/trace-kr.

</details>


### [106] [MamaReason: Unfinished Reasoning and Answer Recovery in Clinical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18264)
*Thanni Adewuyi,Adepoju Jeremiah Moyondafoluwa,Dorcas Amapu,Dieko Whenu,Sylvia Essessien,Sandra Oko-Oba,Pelumi Onafadeji,Lily Aimas-Love,Samuel Omolaiye,Abiodun Adereni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在MamaReason模型上研究：续写常返回推理轨迹中已陈述的诊断，通过显式承诺抽取器揭示“诊断内容已在轨迹中存在”“完成被接受的答案”“配对诊断正确性”是三种可区分的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床推理模型在续写时往往直接复述未完成推理中已出现的诊断，现有评估未区分这一“复述”现象与真正的诊断能力，因此需要拆解这些混杂的评估结果。

Method: 基于Gemma 4 31B稠密模型改编的MamaReason，在MamaBench 217对母婴健康配对样本上，用显式承诺抽取器从首轮失败轨迹中提取诊断字符串；仅用诊断字符串匹配续写结果；并设复发控制区分常规提及与重复答案选择，另用离线第二承诺触发器测量提前读出与配对准确率的权衡。

Result: 抽取器在94个RL首轮失败中识别出75个诊断，在93个SFT首轮失败中识别出75个；续写与之一致分别为RL 67/75、SFT 58/75；仅读取承诺无额外生成即可恢复归档标签下续写恢复的40对RL样本中的30对。

Conclusion: 该分解将“轨迹中已存在的诊断内容”“被接受答案的完成”“配对诊断正确性”区分开来，提示临床语言模型评估应通过多种读出方式审视同一推理轨迹，以避免将复述误判为能力。

Abstract: Answer continuation often returns a diagnosis already stated in unfinished clinical reasoning. We study this behavior in MamaReason, adapted from dense Gemma 4 31B, on MamaBench's 217 paired maternal and child-health presentations. An explicit-commitment extractor identifies a diagnosis in 75 of 94 failed RL first passes and 75 of 93 failed SFT first passes. The continuation matches that diagnosis in 67/75 RL cases and 58/75 SFT cases, using diagnosis strings alone. Reading these commitments without additional generation recovers 30 of the 40 RL pairs restored by continuation under the archived correctness labels. Recurrence controls distinguish routine mentions from repeated answer selection. An offline second-commitment trigger measures the tradeoff between earlier readout and paired accuracy. The resulting decomposition separates diagnostic content already present in a trace, completion of an accepted answer, and paired diagnostic correctness. For clinical language-model evaluation, these are distinct outcomes that become visible when the same reasoning trace is examined through multiple readouts.

</details>


### [107] [Folding Language Models into Themselves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18113)
*Ziyang Zhang,Yubin Jing,Yuyao Li,Haofan Wang,Yichen Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Concertina：将教师模型每N个相邻层折叠为一个共享块并执行N次，配合低秩校正与逐token门控，从教师权重直接构建紧凑学生，仅用22B token即在Qwen3-4B上保留约90%能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏需从头训练紧凑学生，依赖大量数据；而教师权重本身已编码了学生所需学习的大部分知识，因此探索直接由教师权重构建学生。

Method: 1) 层折叠：将每组N个相邻教师层折叠为一个共享块，重复执行N次；2) 循环步引入低秩校正，由被替换层初始化；3) 逐token门控混合各校正，使每个token从单一共享块恢复原层行为；4) 设计平衡通用、数学、学术及定向选择题数据的蒸馏数据配方，以同时恢复教师多方面能力。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上，2.27B的Concertina模型用22B token蒸馏，保留常识与数学推理约90%、八项基准平均84%；优于同规模稠密与层共享基线，也优于训练数据量超千倍的Qwen3-1.7B。

Conclusion: 紧凑模型无需大规模重训练：将教师折叠进自身并在合适数据上蒸馏即可恢复其大部分能力。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation usually trains a compact student from scratch, which needs a large amount of data even though the teacher's weights already encode most of what the student has to learn. In this work, we explore building the student directly from the teacher's weights. We propose Concertina, which folds every group of N adjacent teacher layers into one shared block that is executed N times. Each loop step carries a low-rank correction initialized from the layer it replaces, and a per-token gate mixes these corrections, to let every token recover the behavior of the original layers from a single shared block. We further design a distillation data recipe that balances general, mathematical, academic and targeted multiple-choice data, to recover all capabilities of the teacher together rather than one at the expense of another. On Qwen3-4B, a 2.27B Concertina model distilled on 22B tokens retains about 90% of the teacher on commonsense and mathematical reasoning and 84% on average across eight benchmarks. It outperforms same-size dense and layer-sharing baselines, as well as Qwen3-1.7B, which is trained on over a thousand times more data. These results suggest that a compact model need not be retrained at scale, since folding the teacher into itself and distilling it on the right data recovers most of its capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/myboker/concertina.

</details>


### [108] [Agentic induction of common data elements and information extraction from sarcoma case report abstracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18506)
*Daniel Reichenpfader,Kwaku Appiagyei,Manovah Nadarajah,Ahmed Reda Bahr,Irina Filchenko,Fabio Dennstädt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型智能体可从肉瘤病例报告摘要中自动派生CDE模式并大规模抽取，但需专家审核后使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用数据元素（CDE）有助于临床数据可比性，但通常依赖人工定义，成本高、难以规模化。

Method: 采用LLM智能体从24,588篇PubMed摘要中提出72,909个候选CDE，经智能体化归并得到1,570个CDE；保留至少被50篇摘要支持的53个CDE，并在全语料上做带证据链接的抽取；由两名人工评审评估相关性、清晰性和正确性，并在100篇抽样摘要中比对抽取值的精确一致率。

Result: 79.2%的摘要对全部保留CDE处理成功（含“未提及”判定）；评审对相关性、清晰性、正确性的通过率分别为83.0%、67.9%、56.6%；1,116个非空答案对的精确一致率为94.9%（摘要级bootstrap 95% CI 93.0–96.4%），错误集中于解剖学与时间字段。

Conclusion: 研究支持“智能体辅助模式起草＋专家审核”再开展大规模抽取的流程。

Abstract: Common data elements (CDEs) support clinical data comparability but are usually defined manually. We investigated whether large language model agents could derive a CDE schema from sarcoma case report abstracts and apply it at scale. An agent proposed 72,909 candidate CDEs from 24,588 PubMed abstracts; agentic consolidation yielded 1,570 CDEs. We retained 53 supported by at least 50 abstracts and attempted evidence-linked extraction across the corpus. Processing succeeded for all retained CDEs in 79.2% of abstracts, including responses indicating non-mention. Two human reviewers approved 83.0% of retained CDEs for relevance, 67.9% for clarity, and 56.6% for correctness. In 100 sampled abstracts, exact agreement with the value extracted by the LLM was 94.9% among 1,116 pairs with a non-empty answer (abstract-level bootstrap 95% CI, 93.0-96.4%). Errors concentrated in anatomical and temporal fields. These findings support agent-assisted schema drafting followed by expert curation before large-scale extraction.

</details>


### [109] [Do Verifier Judgments Survive Abstraction? Measuring Verification Transport for LLM Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18471)
*Disha Sheshanarayana,Sai Linisha Reddy Goli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出“验证传输”（Verification Transport）可靠性测试：固定任务、轨迹、结果、真值与验证器，仅改变执行证据的可观测性（原生/抽象/还原三种视图），发现LLM验证器在抽象边界下出现显著判断不稳定（宏抽象与委派下达约31%），仅靠原生视图准确率不足以刻画验证器可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM验证器可靠性多在同一轨迹表示下评测，但部署系统中同一执行会经组合工具、委派边界、历史压缩等抽象暴露，验证器可直接观测的证据随之变化；因此不清楚正确判断能否跨抽象边界保持。

Method: 构造同一冻结执行的原生、抽象与还原匹配视图，控制任务、实际轨迹、结果、真值、评估标准与验证器不变；在4个智能体评测基准、7个验证器骨干、4种验证协议、3类抽象边界上测量判断稳定性（传输失败率）。

Result: 传输失败显著，宏抽象与委派下约31%；协议失效模式分化：直接验证多产生错误裁定，证据锚定与审议式协议在可观测性受限时倾向弃权，故较低传输失败率不等同更高可靠性。还原执行溯源可恢复85.5%的传输失败，溯源有损或缺失时恢复率更低。

Conclusion: 原生视图准确率不足以表征验证器在跨抽象边界时的可靠性；需将证据可观测性与溯源完整性纳入验证器评测。

Abstract: LLM-based verifiers are increasingly used to evaluate agent executions, but their reliability is typically measured under a single representation of the trajectory. In deployed agent systems, the same execution may be exposed through composed tools, delegation boundaries, or compacted histories, changing what execution evidence is directly observable to the verifier. It therefore remains unclear whether correct verifier judgments persist across such abstraction boundaries. We introduce Verification Transport, a reliability test that measures judgment stability under abstraction-induced changes in execution observability. We construct matched native, abstracted, and restored views of the same frozen executions while holding the task, realized trajectory, outcome, ground truth, evaluation criterion, and verifier fixed. Across four agent-evaluation benchmarks, seven verifier backbones, four verification protocols, and three abstraction boundaries, we find substantial transport failure, reaching about 31% under macro abstraction and delegation. Verification protocols exhibit distinct failure modes: direct verification more often produces incorrect verdicts, whereas evidence-grounded and deliberative protocols tend to abstain under limited observability. This suggests that lower transport failure rates do not necessarily imply greater reliability. Restoring the corresponding execution provenance recovers 85.5% of transport failures, while recovery is lower when provenance is lossy or unavailable. These results show that native-view accuracy alone is insufficient to characterize verifier reliability when execution evidence crosses abstraction boundaries.

</details>


### [110] [HARDTESTGEN: A High-Quality RL Verifier Generation Pipeline for LLM Algorithmic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24098)
*Zhongmou He,Yee Man Choi,Kexun Zhang,Jiabao Ji,Junting Zhou,Dejia Xu,Ivan Bercovich,Aidan Zhang,Yixiao Zeng,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HARDTESTGEN方法，用于为算法编程题合成高质量测试用例，构建26.6k题目的HARDTESTS数据集，测试验证精度提升11.22个百分点，并改善拒绝采样与强化学习下游训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM强化学习依赖可靠的验证器提供奖励信号，但代码生成任务的验证器难以构建：伪装良好的错误程序往往只能靠人工精心设计的边界用例才能发现，而这类边界用例难以自动合成，导致现有测试验证能力不足。

Method: 提出HARDTESTGEN，通过自动化方式为算法编程问题合成高质量测试用例，尤其聚焦难以自动生成的边界情形；并整理构建包含26.6k道算法题及高质量合成测试的HARDTESTS数据集与合成流水线。

Result: 相比现有测试，HARDTESTGEN生成的测试在验证LLM生成代码时精度显著更高，精确率提升11.22个百分点；将其结果作为验证器用于拒绝采样与强化学习等下游后训练，可提升LLM代码生成性能。

Conclusion: 高质量合成边界测试是构建可靠代码验证器的有效途径，HARDTESTS数据集与合成流水线开源，可用于改进LLM代码生成的验证与后训练。

Abstract: Verifiers provide important reward signals for reinforcement learning of large language models (LLMs). However, it is challenging to develop or create reliable verifiers, especially for code generation tasks. A well-disguised wrong solution program may only be detected by carefully human-written edge cases that are difficult to synthesize automatically. To address this issue, we propose HARDTESTGEN, an approach to synthesize high-quality test cases for algorithmic coding problems. We curate a comprehensive algorithmic programming dataset HARDTESTS with 26.6k problems and high-quality synthetic tests. Compared with existing tests, HARDTESTGEN tests demonstrate significantly higher accuracy in verifying LLM-generated code (+11.22 percentage points in precision, the percentage of actually correct code within the predicted correct ones). We also show that downstream post-training -- including rejection sampling and reinforcement learning (RL) -- using HARDTESTS verifier results in improved performance of LLM code generation. We open-source our dataset and synthesis pipeline at https://leililab.github.io/HardTests/.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [What LLM Agents See and What Users Say](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17639)
*Yue Zhao,Angela Huyue Zhang,S. Alex Yang,Hua Wei,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文借用语言学预设理论，研究LLM agent在工具显示与用户陈述冲突时是否/何时先核实再行动；给出"查询无法区分一条与两条记录"时不可避免的误行为界，并通过改变检索呈现方式（列出/计数/隐藏第二条）与用户断言真伪进行实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: agent在替用户取消预订或删除文件夹前，可能基于错误预设直接行动；现有工作不清楚究竟哪种信息（工具输出还是用户陈述）应该阻断行动，也缺乏对"未经验证的假设"这一失败模式的系统刻画与评测。

Method: 引入语言学预设理论：行动背后每个断言（如"只有一条符合该名字的预订"）必须被观察到成立、被用户明确回答，或在可撤销时被披露。给出一个简单界：当查询无法区分一条与两条记录时，agent不先询问就行动的频率记录为两条时会与只有一条时一样高。实验操纵检索结果为列出、计数或省略第二条匹配，以及用户给出被记录反驳的断言（如"It is empty"）；在36个早期agent上使用role-only提示，并将同名同邮编客户注入零售基准。

Result: role-only提示下，计数第二条记录时多数早期agent仍对所见那一条行动，而列出两条则能阻止多数；当用户断言某文件夹为空时，计数其唯一一条内容与列出的阻止效果相近，且用户断言为假时多数能抑制行动。最新Claude与GPT模型总能先打开被计数的第二条记录，随后不再行动。零售基准中查询优先返回同名同邮编客户导致约四分之一对话出现写错账户，而检查用户已知街道的查询使完成率接近单客户水平。

Conclusion: 安全写入需要能列出或校验竞争记录的查询设计，以及改变工具呈现内容的评测方法；仅靠用户陈述或计数式检索不足以保障行为安全。

Abstract: Before an LLM agent cancels a reservation or deletes a folder for a user, what stops it from acting on a false assumption: what its tools show, or what the user says? In this work, we study this gap with presupposition theory from linguistics: an agent may act only if each claim behind the act (e.g., one reservation fits the name) is seen to hold, answered by the user, or disclosed while the act can be undone. A simple bound shows that when lookups cannot tell one record from two, any agent acts without asking first as often with two records as with one. We then vary whether a search lists, counts, or omits a second match, and whether the user states a claim that a record refutes (e.g., "It is empty"). Under a role-only prompt, a count of a second record leaves most of 36 earlier agents acting on the one they see, whereas listing both stops most. When the user says a record such as a folder is empty, however, a count of its one item stops agents about as often as a listing, and stating the claim mostly cuts acting when it is false. The newest Claude and GPT models always open a counted second record, then never act. When we add a same-name, same-zip customer to a retail benchmark, a lookup returning that customer first led to wrong-account writes in about a quarter of conversations, and one that checks a street the user knows kept completion near the one-customer level. Safe writes therefore need lookups that list or check competing records, and evaluations that vary what tools show.

</details>


### [112] [More Agents, More Samples, or More Thinking? Untangling Coordination, Ensembling, and Test-Time Compute in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17610)
*Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文通过9项实证研究与516个多智能体配置的元分析指出：在匹配样本数或推理预算的基线下，多智能体通信架构的准确率平均低于单模型基线（约-0.042），此前的正向收益多源于基线与比较口径的混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体语言模型评测普遍将通信架构与"单次贪心解码"基线对比，从而把协调机制的效果与采样次数、推理计算量混为一谈，导致对多智能体协同价值的系统性高估。作者希望建立统一的资源与基线口径，厘清多智能体收益的真实来源。

Method: 综合9项主要实证评测、516个唯一多智能体配置；构建六维资源向量R与四类基线阶梯（A：单次贪心通过；B：样本数匹配；C：资源预算匹配）；采用多层级随机效应模型在对数几率尺度上估计准确率差异，并进行多重敏感性检验。

Result: 对照A类基线，多智能体准确率差异总体为负，剔除最大贡献研究后接近零；对照B/C类可比基线，平均差异为-0.042，随机效应模型结果在各敏感性检验下均保持为负；74%的可比对比中多智能体不优于基线，几乎所有研究的均值非正；同时报告两类基线的研究中，由贪心基线切换到采样基线使差异平均偏移-0.038。

Conclusion: 当智能体共享相同证据时，通信不引入新信息，因此正向收益需依赖于证据分区、外部验证或模型间错误多样性（error diversity）。作者提供审稿人核查清单与核算工具，规范多智能体系统与基线的比较口径。

Abstract: Evaluations of multi-agent language models often compare communicating architectures against a single greedy pass. This practice confounds coordination with sample multiplicity and inference compute. We synthesize nine primary empirical evaluations comprising 516 unique multi-agent configurations organized along a resource vector R with six dimensions and a baseline ladder with four categories. Against baselines with a single pass (Category A), multi-agent accuracy differences are negative overall and near zero when omitting the largest contributing study. When evaluated against baselines matched on sample count (Category B) or resource budget (Category C), multi-agent systems average -0.042 in accuracy, and multilevel random effects modeling on the log odds scale remains negative across sensitivity checks. Multi-agent accuracy equals or falls below the baseline in 74% of these comparable contrasts, and study means are nonpositive in almost all evaluated studies. Across studies reporting both baselines, moving from a greedy pass to a sampled baseline shifts accuracy differences by -0.038 on average. Because communication adds no information when agents share identical evidence, positive returns require partitioned evidence, external verification, or error diversity across models. We provide a reviewer checklist and accounting utilities.

</details>


### [113] [AI-Voyager: A Public-Interest System for Human-AI Civic Reasoning to Contest and Shape the Rules That Govern AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17564)
*Snehalkumar `Neil' S. Gaikwad,Dhruv Dixit,Bryen Alvarez,Pratik Patekar,Anika Sharma,Akshara Kolipaka,Paritosh Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI-Voyager 是一个面向公众利益的公民推理系统，让居民与 AI 协作理解、质疑并改写治理 AI 的规则；系统将 3,653 份政府文件转化为可溯源的段落，并用有界智能体检索验证每条陈述，在 360 个新问题上达到 83.0% 宏召回，并通过 24 人用户研究揭示公众参与 AI 治理的使用模式与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政府在被影响者尚无从质疑之前就已制定 AI 规则，界定何为自动决策或损害，公众缺乏参与通道与理解这些规则的手段，因此需要一个支持人机协同公民推理的公共系统。

Method: 构建 AI-Voyager：将市、州、联邦及国家层面的 3,653 份治理文件切分为保留页码与版本的段落；采用有界智能体检索（bounded agentic search）逐句核验并回溯证据；用户可按受影响者视角阅读、查看答案背后原文并起草修订。评估包括 360 个需定位相关文档的新问题检索基准、消融对比，以及 24 名对两项 AI 法案不熟悉的参与者用户研究。

Result: 新问题集上宏召回 83.0%，优于最强基线 80.5%；去除迭代检索降至 73.7%；在固定文档基准上达 99.1%。用户研究中，多数修改建议集中于条款如何适用的问题；参与者自选解释视角，引用被视作有依据的信号但很少促使其核查原始来源。

Conclusion: 将 AI 辅助严格绑定到公共文件的精确文本，可提出一套操作语法与设计空间，为这类系统的设计、评估及公众参与 AI 治理的方式提供依据。

Abstract: Governments are writing rules for artificial intelligence that decide what counts as an automated decision or a harm, often before the people they affect have a chance to question them. We introduce AI-Voyager, a public-interest system for human-AI civic reasoning, in which people and AI work together to understand, contest and shape the rules that govern AI. A resident can find their city's rules on AI in hiring or upload their country's AI strategy, read it as an affected worker would, open the passage behind any answer, and draft a revision. Its architecture turns 3,653 governance documents from city, state, federal and national governments into passages that keep their page and edition, and retrieves evidence through bounded agentic search that verifies each statement. On 360 new questions that require finding the relevant documents, it reached 83.0% macro recall, against 80.5% for the strongest baseline, 73.7% without iterative search and 99.1% on a benchmark with fixed documents. In a study with 24 participants new to two AI bills, most proposed changes asked how provisions would apply. Participants chose lenses for the kind of explanation they wanted, and citations signaled grounding but rarely led them to check the source. By binding AI assistance to the exact text of public documents, this work introduces a grammar of operations and a design space that inform how such systems are designed and evaluated, and how people take part in governing AI.

</details>


### [114] [Can Mechanistic Interpretability Help Explain LLM Agent Failures? A Case Study on Web Element Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18514)
*Mohamed Aghzal,Mor Geva,Gregory J. Stein,Ziyu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验机制可解释性（MI）工具在真实LLM智能体失败案例上的适用性，以网页智能体"幻觉不存在动作"为对象，发现线性探针可提前预测并部分纠正该类幻觉，但解释效果敏感于提示格式、电路识别困难，暴露出标准MI方法在智能体场景下的假设局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MI在解释LLM内部计算上进展显著，但在解释LLM智能体决策过程上尚无同等成功；亟需检验MI工具面对真实智能体失败时的有效性与边界。

Method: 对Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct与实时网站交互的过程进行系列机制分析：在最后一个提示token上训练线性探针以预测幻觉，并沿探针方向做激活引导（steering）；进一步考察提示格式敏感性、电路识别与信息依赖分布。

Result: 探针在生成前即可预测幻觉（AUC 0.80）；沿探针方向引导可纠正85%的幻觉，但破坏7%的有效动作；探针方向对提示格式敏感，动作表示方式的微小改动会大幅削弱引导效果；计算分散于多个组件与位置，且依赖信息随样例变化，导致电路识别困难。

Conclusion: 上述结果仅提供对幻觉成因的部分解释，并对标准MI方法在电路发现与特征识别上的基本假设构成挑战；作者提出未来方向，使MI方法更适配解释LLM智能体的失败。

Abstract: Mechanistic Interpretability (MI) has made significant progress in explaining the internal computations of Large Language Models (LLMs); however, these advances have yet to demonstrate comparable success in explaining the decision-making processes of LLM-based agents. This paper examines the challenges MI tools face when applied to realistic agentic failures. We focus on one such failure: web agents hallucinating actions that are not available on a website. We apply a series of mechanistic analyzes to Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct as it interacts with live websites. We find that a linear probe trained on the last prompt token can predict hallucinations before generation begins (0.80 AUC) and steering along a probe direction corrects 85\% of hallucinations, but disrupts 7\% of valid actions. However, these results only provide a partial explanation of why the agent hallucinates. The probe direction is sensitive to prompt formatting, losing much of its steering effect after a minor change to how actions are represented. Additionally, circuit identification can be difficult as the computation is distributed across multiple components and positions, and the information the agent relies on varies across examples. We discuss how these observations challenge the assumptions underlying standard MI methods for circuit discovery and feature identification, and outline future directions for making these methods better suited to explain failures in LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [115] [Is Abliteration Getting Harder? Not Uniformly: Refusal Removal by Direction-Based Ablation and ARA in Heretic on the Qwen3.8, Gemma 4, Olmo 3.1 and Mistral Small 3.2 LLMs Versus Their Predecessors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17846)
*Richard J. Young*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究在五组新旧开源模型对上统一控制提示词、搜索预算、首token KL散度上限与选择规则，比较四种方向式消融与基于优化的ARA去除拒答的有效性，发现新一代模型更难去除拒答的开发者说法并不成立：效应随模型对、方法与预算而变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARA开发者报告称，基于方向消融（ablating拒答方向）的abliteration在新一代开源权重LLM上日益失效，但该结论可能受方法、提示词来源、预算等混杂因素影响。作者希望用受控的跨代对比检验"新模型更难被去除拒答"这一泛化预测。

Method: 选取五组发布对（Qwen、Gemma、OLMo、Mistral、Qwen小模型），在Heretic 2.0中由3–4种方向式方法（difference-in-means、MPOA、SOM，小模型对另加J-space）与ARA，在相同提示词、名义搜索预算、首token KL散度上限和选择规则下进行权重编辑；以自动WildGuard判定在972条留出有害提示（JailbreakBench、XSTest、StrongREJECT、HarmBench及作者自建恶意代码库）上仍拒答的比例作为"更难去除"的指标，并纳入run间方差、异质性I²与偏倚审计（关键词计数 vs 判定拒答）。

Result: 方向式编辑后新版本模型保留的拒答率多出-1至+37个百分点，ARA后为-26至+34，均非一致上升；方向式效应的组间异质性随尺度与方差模型从中高到低（I² 20–80%）。ARA在Gemma与OLMo上损失小于方向式方法、在Qwen上更大；但每个结论仅基于一次ARA运行，部分结论随散度预算或方差模型改变。Gemma中方向式恶化来自恶意代码库，在公开集上反转。关键词计数在工具窗口处平均并未低估判定拒答，但各模型间平均相差7个百分点。

Conclusion: 新模型是否更难被去除拒答取决于发布对、方法与预算，而非单纯代际；仅审计单一模型对、单一方法或单一提示源会得出相反结论，因此关于abliteration可扩展性的论断须多因素受控评估。

Abstract: Abliteration, a fine-tuning-free weight edit, removes refusal from open-weight large language models (LLMs). The developer of Arbitrary-Rank Ablation (ARA), an optimisation-based variant, reports that direction-based abliteration, which ablates a refusal direction, increasingly fails on newer models. On five release pairs, refusal removal did not become uniformly harder. Qwen2.5-32B and Qwen3.8-27B, Gemma-2-27B and Gemma-4-31B, OLMo-2-32B and Olmo-3.1-32B, Mistral-Small-2409 and Mistral-Small-3.2, and Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3.5-4B were edited in Heretic 2.0 by three or four direction-based methods (difference-in-means, MPOA, SOM and, on the small pair, J-space) and ARA under the same prompts, nominal search budget, first-token KL divergence cap and selection rule; removal counted as harder when the automatic WildGuard judge found more of 972 held-out harmful prompts (JailbreakBench, XSTest, StrongREJECT, HarmBench and an author-compiled malicious-code bank) still refused. After direction-based removal, newer releases retained -1 to +37 percentage points more refusal, and after ARA -26 to +34. With run-to-run variance included, between-pair heterogeneity of the direction-based effect ranged from high to low with the scale and variance model (I^2 20-80%). ARA lost less effectiveness than direction-based methods in Gemma and OLMo and more in Qwen; each verdict rests on one ARA run, and some changed with the divergence budget or variance model. In Gemma the direction-based increase came from the malicious-code bank and reversed on public sets. Contrary to prediction, keyword counts at the tool's window did not undercount judged refusal on average, but differed by a mean 7 points per model. Audits of a single pair, method or prompt source can reach opposite conclusions. Whether a newer model resisted removal depended on the release pair, method and budget, not on generation alone.

</details>


### [116] [A Push Down the Mountain: Framing Effects and Internal State Interventions on Collusive Behavior in a Long-Horizon LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17515)
*Harrison C. Margolin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过市场模拟检验"得—失（效价）框架"能否诱导LLM智能体参与市场操纵：Gemma 3 12B扮演伦敦白银定盘价诉讼中的被告，260次模拟显示损失与责备框架显著提高合谋参与率；而将对应内部状态注入第22层后，损失/责备状态反而使智能体更警觉、拒绝全部请求，增益/信用状态则提高合谋率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究文本层面的框架操纵与内部控制状态注入这两种途径，能否使LLM智能体偏离合规行为、参与白银价格合谋，从而理解LLM代理在敏感市场情境下的行为脆弱性与内部表征机制。

Method: 以真实案件庭审记录构建白银定盘价操纵市场模拟，将Gemma 3 12B置于被告席位，在260次模拟中由对手方请求合谋定价；借用行为经济学设置四种分类框架（gain、loss、blame、credit），并叠加管理层施压与另一被告的背书。第二阶段从引发各框架的文本中提取内部状态，并在模型第22层注入后重跑实验。

Result: Phase I：损失框架使参与率升至25%（p=0.007），责备框架升至33%（p=0.0003），中性框架仅5%；银行背书在credit（表扬）下将参与率从18%提高到40%（Holm p=0.027）；管理层施压使交易量增加10–19倍但不提高合谋率。Phase II：注入状态方向相反——损失与责备状态使智能体更警觉，60次请求全部拒绝（p<0.001）；增益与信用状态使合谋率由13%升至32%（p=0.051）；在UBS直接要求按已识别止损位卖出的一个保存决策中，注入状态下40次回复有37次卖出，而无状态为1/10、同强度随机状态为0/10。

Conclusion: 外部文本框架与内部状态注入对LLM智能体行为产生方向相反且显著的影响，说明框架效应与内部表征并不一致；仅靠文本层面观察不足以预测状态注入下的行为，需同时考察内部状态以评估LLM代理的合规风险。

Abstract: Can sustained gain-loss (valence) framing sway an LLM agent toward market manipulation? We place Gemma 3 12B into the seat of a defendant in the London silver-fixing litigation, in a market simulation built from real case transcripts. Across 260 simulations, the agent is asked to collude on the price of silver by a counterparty. We draw from behavioral economics to vary the counterparty's requests across four categorical frames: gain, loss, blame and credit. We also add pressure from the agent's management, as well as endorsement by another defendant. In Phase II, we extract the internal states induced by texts evoking each frame and rerun our experiment with those states injected at the model's layer 22.
  In Phase I, loss framing raised participation to 25% (p = 0.007), and blame to 33% (p = 0.0003), compared to only 5% under neutral framing. A second bank's endorsement raised participation under credit (praise) from 18% to 40% (Holm p = 0.027). Pressure from management raised trading volume 10-19 times but did not raise collusion. In Phase II, injected states pointed the other way. Loss and blame states made the agent more vigilant, refusing all 60 requests (p < 0.001). Gain and credit states raised collusion to 32% from 13% with no state (p = 0.051). At one saved decision, when UBS asked directly to sell through an identified stop-loss, injected states led the agent to sell in 37 of 40 replies, versus 1 of 10 with no state and 0 of 10 with a random state of the same strength.

</details>


### [117] [G-CAST: Governed Continual Self-Training for E-Commerce Agents under Evolving Demands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17814)
*Yuechun Yu,Wenzheng Kong,Xiaofeng Mao,Jin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 G-CAST——一个受治理的持续自训练系统，将生产证据转化为按奖励路由、按熵排优的训练数据并演化奖励模型，通过基于累积评估的检查点治理实现电商对话代理的持续自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驱动的电商对话购物代理面临两类漂移挑战：1）需求漂移导致用户请求的分布、意图与复杂度变化，一次性训练的模型会落后；2）执行框架（工具、技能、操作规程）演化要求模型掌握新能力又不丢失已有行为。周期性人工重训延迟高、成本大。

Method: G-CAST 系统：将每日生产证据转化为 reward-routed（按奖励路由）、entropy-prioritized（按熵排优）的训练数据，并进行受治理的奖励演化；采用累积评估下的治理式检查点切换（governed checkpoint transition），控制每次模型更新。

Result: 在真实生产电商代理上的实验表明，相比强自主后训练基线，G-CAST 对新引入需求的适应更强，同时更好地保持既有已接受行为；消融显示数据编排与奖励演化两项机制均对增益重要。系统已部署于 Alibaba 1688 平台的 B2B 采购代理元材（cai.1688.com）。

Conclusion: G-CAST 证明了以治理式持续自训练替代人工周期重训的可行性，兼顾需求漂移下的适应性与能力演化的稳定性，并已在真实生产环境中落地。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs), an increasing number of users are turning to LLM-powered agents for conversational shopping in e-commerce. However, the user needs continue to change as new product categories, procurement scenarios, and feature requests emerge. At the same time, the product's execution harness-including its tools, skills, and operating procedures-continues to evolve to support increasingly sophisticated interactions. These changes create two practical challenges: 1) Demand drift alters the distribution, intent, and complexity of user requests, causing a model trained once to fall behind; 2) Harness evolution requires the model to use newly introduced capabilities correctly without losing behavior that users already rely on. Periodic manual retraining is too delayed and labor-intensive for this setting. To address these challenges, we present G-CAST, a governed continual self-training system that converts daily production evidence under evolving demand and capability contracts into reward-routed, entropy-prioritized training data and governed reward evolution. Governed checkpoint transition under cumulative evaluation controls each model update, enabling the agent to learn newly introduced behaviors without sacrificing previously accepted capabilities. Experiments on a real production e-commerce agent show that G-CAST achieves stronger adaptation to newly introduced demand while better preserving previously accepted behavior compared with a strong autonomous post-training baseline. Ablations further show that both reward-routed, entropy-prioritized data orchestration and governed reward evolution are important to these gains. G-CAST is now deployed in Yuancai(http://cai.1688.com/), a B2B procurement agent on Alibaba's 1688 platform.

</details>


### [118] [ModHarness-RL: Recasting Harnesses as Learnable and Evolving Programs for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18533)
*Zelin He,Siyu Zhu,Jacob Helwig,Yujian Liu,Li An,Rohit Jain,Shiyu Chang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ModHarness-RL，将智能体强化学习中的 harness 建模为可从数据学习并随模型训练演化的程序，核心是 Compression 与 Steering 两个程序组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agentic RL 多在人工编写且全程固定的 harness 下训练，但有效的 harness 依赖领域、数据和模型，并随训练变化，固定 harness 难以适配。

Method: 将交互记录为结构化 State（类型化块集合），把 harness 的两个核心组件写成作用于这些块的程序：Compression 程序将累积历史组装为模型上下文，Steering 程序在行为偏离时提供引导；训练中从近期轨迹提出候选程序，经执行验证并在历史轨迹上回放重放，使 harness 跟随模型行为变化。

Result: 在 CodeSearch 上深入实验，并分析 ALFWorld 与 BIRD SQL，模型规模 1.5B 至 8B；Compression 与 Steering 机制在多数设置下提升留出集表现，优于固定 harness 基线。

Conclusion: agentic RL 的 harness 可以从任务数据中学习，并随模型共同演化。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly relies on a harness, the scaffold around the model that decides what it sees of its own interaction and what guidance it receives. Most existing approaches train under a hand-written harness that stays fixed throughout. However, an effective harness depends on the domain, the data and the model, and keeps changing as the model trains. We introduce ModHarness-RL, which recasts the harness as programs that are learned from data and evolve as the model trains with RL. ModHarness-RL records the interaction as structured State, a set of typed blocks, and two core components of the harness are programs over these blocks, a Compression program that assembles the accumulated history into the model's context and a Steering program that supplies guidance when behavior deviates. During RL training, candidate programs are proposed from recent trajectories, verified by execution, and replayed over past trajectories, so the harness tracks how the model's behavior shifts. We study CodeSearch in depth and further analyze ALFWorld and BIRD SQL, with models from 1.5B to 8B. The proposed Compression and Steering mechanisms improve held-out performance over the fixed-harness baseline in most settings. These results suggest that a harness for agentic RL can be learned from the task's data and evolved with the model.

</details>


### [119] [WebArena-Pro: A Heterogeneous, Multimodal, Reproducible Benchmark for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17955)
*Imene Kerboua,Fatemeh Pesaran Zadeh,Xing Han Lù,Weijian Qi,Alexander Miller,Junyi Song,Yunjia Tian,Dongjin Kang,Seyeon Choi,Marzia Nouri,Ewen Gueguen,Matteo Boglioni,Fengyuan Liu,Zeyi Liao,Mengqi Yuan,Yue Li,Alexandre Lacoste,Alexandre Drouin,Spandana Gella,Huan Sun,Gunhee Kim,Siva Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 WebArena-Pro 基准，含6大领域20个自托管Web应用、300个任务，将音频与视频作为核心观测模态并配专用抽取动作，实现可复现并行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可复现的Web Agent基准应用数量少、软件类别单一、模态仅限文本与视觉；实时（Live）基准虽覆盖面广但页面与数据漂移导致不可复现；且现有基准未真正评估Agent对网页任务中音视频内容的理解与使用能力。

Method: 构建300个任务、覆盖6大领域类别下20个自托管Web应用，涵盖不同界面规范、工作流与数据模型；将音频与视频与文本、视觉并列为核心观测，并设计针对每种模态信息抽取的专用动作；任务经专用标注界面撰写、LLM辅助筛选，再由人工验证后发布；每个任务隔离运行，支持可复现的并行评测。

Result: 最高成绩为 Codex（GPT 6.1 Sol）与 Claude Code（Claude Opus 5.5）并列 62.3%，其次 OpenCode（Kimi 3.1 Pro）60.7%；最强 GenericAgent 配置 Gemini 3.1 Pro omni 与 Gemma4 31B 均为 30.3%。

Conclusion: 在可复现、人工策划的Web Agent基准中，WebArena-Pro 具有迄今最广的应用覆盖与最全面的多模态支持，兼顾可复现性与多样化应用场景，填补音视频模态评测空白。

Abstract: Web agents powered by large language and vision-language models are increasingly applied to realistic browser work that spans heterogeneous applications, multimodal content, and stateful workflows. However, existing reproducible web-agent benchmarks cover only a small number of web applications drawn from a few software categories, and restrict modality to text and vision. Live benchmarks broaden site coverage but sacrifice reproducibility, since pages and data drift between runs. Moreover, existing benchmarks do not meaningfully evaluate whether agents can understand and use audio and video content embedded within web tasks. To address these gaps, we introduce WebArena-Pro, a benchmark comprising 300 tasks across 20 self-hosted web applications in six domain categories, spanning distinct interface conventions, workflows, and data models. Across the evaluated agents, the best performance is achieved by both Codex with GPT 6.1 Sol and Claude Code with Claude Opus 5.5 at 62.3%, followed by OpenCode with Kimi 3.1 Pro at 60.7%. The strongest GenericAgent configurations, Gemini 3.1 Pro omni and Gemma4 31B, both achieve 30.3%. Among reproducible, human-curated web agent benchmarks, WebArena-Pro provides the broadest application coverage and the most comprehensive multimodal support to date. The benchmark treats audio and video as core observations alongside text and vision, with dedicated actions for extracting information from each. WebArena-Pro runs each task in isolation and supports reproducible, parallel evaluation. Tasks are authored through a dedicated annotator interface, filtered by LLM-assisted triage, and finally validated by humans before release. Project page: https://webarena-pro.github.io

</details>


### [120] [When evaluation becomes intervention in clinical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18297)
*Anthony Abou Mrad,Aviona Conti,Samuel Schmidgall,Jayanth Pratap,Ruhika Iyer,Joseph Kim,King Shi,Yann Sakref,Luis M. Ahumada,Lisa Knowlton,Andrew M. Cameron,Khyzer Aziz,Jeffrey K. Jopling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当"AI评审者"（依据评分量表给其他系统输出打分的模型）被用于筛选提示词或训练模型时，评审者本身即成为干预的一部分，模型选择可能偏向评审者所忽视的错误；作者提出评审报告应包含的内容，以及每项由评审者驱动的改动所需的独立临床检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床AI输出量远超人工审阅能力，需借助AI评审者把临床评审扩展到相应规模；但团队将评审者评分用于提示词选择或模型训练时，评审者实质上嵌入了干预链条，其盲区会经由选择过程被系统性放大，产生"评审者对齐的错误"而非真实质量提升。

Method: 立场/方法学论述（非实验性研究）：界定AI评审者在临床评审中的角色与规模化价值；分析"评审者—选择—训练"耦合所引发的选择偏差与错误放大机制；规范评审报告的必要构成要素；提出对每一处由评审者驱动的改动进行独立临床验证的评估框架。

Result: 摘要未报告实测量化结果；核心发现为机制性论证：以评审者评分作为选择信号会使优化目标退化为"评审者可检测的质量"，被评审者忽略的错误类型会因选择压力而富集，导致报告指标与真实临床质量脱节。

Conclusion: AI评审者应被视为干预组件而非中立测量工具。需从两方面约束：一是规范化评审报告的披露内容（使评审者的覆盖范围与盲区可被审计），二是对任何由评审者驱动的提示词或模型改动，均以独立于该评审者的临床检验加以确认。

Abstract: AI judges, models that grade another system's output against a rubric, extend clinical review to the scale at which clinical AI writes. When teams use judge assessments to select a prompt or train a model, the judge becomes part of the intervention. Selection can then favor errors the judge overlooks. We describe what a judge's report must contain and the independent clinical test each judge-driven change needs.

</details>


### [121] [Tabular Population Priors as Structured Retrieval for EHR Medication Set Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17341)
*Animesh Agarwal,Meysam Ghaffari,Nina Fatehi,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将临床RAG中的"患者级最近邻检索"替换为"基于条件-药物共现表的表格化人群先验排序候选"，在PACERAG流水线中仅改动检索器即可将F1从0.303提升至0.338（相对+11.5%）、Jaccard从0.218提升至0.237，且无额外LLM调用与推理时患者索引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用药预测本质是固定词表的结构化预测问题，但现有临床RAG系统多按开放式个案推理处理：检索相似住院记录后让LLM推断药物集合。此时检索对象与目标（离散药物类别标签集合）不匹配——模型获得的是患者记录，而目标是标签集合，检索对象属于"间接"错配。

Method: 提出与任务更对齐的检索对象：由训练集住院记录估计的稀疏"条件-药物共现表"，据此对药物类别候选做表格化人群先验排序。实验以PACERAG作为固定下游流水线，仅替换其患者检索器（drop-in swap），不增加LLM调用，也不构建推理时患者索引；并设置非LLM的top-k基线验证先验本身的预测力。

Result: 在1,004条MIMIC-IV留出住院记录上，F1由0.303升至0.338（相对提升11.5%），Jaccard由0.218升至0.237；非LLM top-k基线达到F1=0.248，表明人群先验在接入LLM之前已具备预测能力。

Conclusion: 对于固定词表的EHR集合预测任务，聚合人群统计量可作为比患者级最近邻更任务对齐、更高效且更可审计的检索来源。

Abstract: Medication prediction from electronic health records is a fixed vocabulary structured prediction problem, yet recent clinical RAG systems often treat it like open ended case based reasoning: retrieve similar patient admissions and ask an LLM to infer a medication set. This retrieval object is indirect the model receives retrieved patient records while the target is a discrete set of medication class labels. We propose a more task aligned retrieval object: a tabular population prior ranked medication class candidates from a sparse condition medication co occurrence table estimated from training admissions. Using PACERAG as a fixed downstream pipeline and replacing only its patient retriever, this drop in swap improves F1 from 0.303 to 0.338 (+11.5% relative) and Jaccard from 0.218 to 0.237 on 1,004 heldout MIMIC IV admissions, with no additional LLM calls and no inference time patient index. A non LLM top k baseline reaches F1=0.248, confirming the prior is predictive before LLM integration. Aggregate population statistics can provide a more task aligned, efficient, and auditable retrieval source than patient level nearest neighbors for fixed vocabulary EHR set prediction.

</details>


### [122] [Advancing Quantization for R&R Models with Microscaling Formats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17501)
*Yavuz Yetim,Bor-Yiing Su,Zhe Wang,Arthur Liu,Pranav Sharma,Ying Wang,Randy Shuai,Luke Pfister,Ruichao Xiao,Bill Zhao,Zihao Liu,Liyuan Li,Jiangyuan Li,Gaoxiang Liu,Nathan Yan,Geonhwa Jeong,Jatin Chhugani,Elias Ellison,Uladzimir Pashkevich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在AMD MI350X上对工业级推荐与排序(R&R)模型的MXFP8与6-bit MXFP6量化推理进行实证评估，首次报告了原生MXFP6 GEMM在不损失模型质量前提下的显著加速，并通过选择性应用6-bit格式在严格NE阈值内获得端到端吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: R&R模型需要极高推理吞吐但对精度损失极为敏感：生产级模型要求相对FP16/FP32基线的归一化熵(NE)偏差在0.01%以内，远严于LLM常见的1%容忍度，这一严苛门槛长期阻碍了激进量化在该领域的应用。

Method: 在AMD MI350X架构上对MXFP8与6-bit MXFP6格式进行工业级R&R推理的实证评测。MXFP6 GEMM同时对权重与激活量化并直接在6-bit元素上计算；采用选择性量化策略，将6-bit格式仅应用于部分层以在满足NE一致性阈值的同时最大化整体QPS；并对比MXFP8与row-wise FP8的精度，按GEMM形状选择更快的内核。

Result: 在两个生产级广告排序模型上，MXFP8相对FP16的GEMM内核加速为1.33–1.55x，MXFP6为1.73–1.99x（覆盖所有支持的线性层），单层最高达2.90x；MXFP8与row-wise FP8精度几乎相同。选择性MXFP6带来的端到端推理吞吐提升为：Model A相对其FP8配置提升1.5–5.3%，Model B提升7.6–14.3%。

Conclusion: 原生MXFP6 GEMM可在不牺牲模型质量的前提下为R&R推理带来显著加速，是首个在R&R与LLM领域报告此类结果的论文；MXFP8可作为row-wise FP8的可行替代并按形状择优；选择性6-bit量化能在严格NE门槛下有效提升端到端吞吐。

Abstract: Recommendation and Ranking (R&R) models, particularly in digital advertising, require massive inference throughput but exhibit extreme sensitivity to precision loss. Unlike Large Language Models (LLMs) that frequently tolerate quantization-induced accuracy drops of 1% or more, production-grade R&R models enforce a stringent launch bar: model quality must remain within a 0.01% Normalized Entropy (NE) margin relative to the baseline, which commonly uses FP16 or FP32. This tight constraint has historically hindered the adoption of aggressive quantization in this domain. In this paper, we present an empirical evaluation of both MXFP8 and the 6-bit MXFP6 format for industrial-scale R&R inference on the AMD MI350X architecture.
  To the best of our knowledge, this is the first paper, across R&R and LLM domains, that reports significant speedups from native MXFP6 GEMMs, which quantize both weights and activations and compute directly on 6-bit elements, without sacrificing model quality. We demonstrate that we can selectively apply this 6-bit format to maximize overall QPS gain without breaching the strict NE parity threshold. We further show that MXFP8 is a viable alternative to row-wise FP8: the two formats have nearly the same accuracy on our models, so the faster kernel can be chosen for each GEMM shape. On two production ads ranking models, our implementation shows measured GEMM kernel speedups over FP16 of 1.33--1.55x for MXFP8 and 1.73--1.99x for MXFP6 across all supported linear layers, and up to 2.90x for MXFP6 on individual layers, alongside end-to-end (E2E) inference throughput gains from selective MXFP6 of 1.5--5.3% for Model A and 7.6--14.3% for Model B over their FP8 configurations.

</details>


### [123] [Model Annealing: Data-Efficient Learning through Review and Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17524)
*Ali Mohammadi Ruzbahani,Rezvan Abouzadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"模型退火"训练法：将训练分为多轮退火episode，每轮从历史冷却快照的调和预测与对抗性"梦境"样本中重新学习，在不增加推理开销的前提下提升AdamW训练模型的准确率、校准与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模标注数据在训练预算耗尽前即被记忆，剩余训练主要用于锐化one-hot标签的置信度，造成过拟合与过置信；因此希望在有限预算内把剩余算力用于"复习与巩固"，从网络自身知识中再学习。

Method: 将训练切分为多个episode，每个episode重新升温并冷却学习率；每个冷却状态加入"过去自我"记忆库；新episode同时从真实标签、记忆库的调和预测、以及在学生与记忆分歧最大处搜索的对抗性梦境中学习。记忆对预测取平均而非权重平均；部署网络即最后一个学生，推理无额外开销。消融中分离了再升温调度与重学习，并将与快照蒸馏对比。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet、Imagewoof、4类架构、5%–100%标签比例下，33种配置中28种优于交叉熵训练，平均提升1.3个点；准确率与快照蒸馏持平，但在似然、校准、抗腐蚀与小对抗扰动上均更优。SGD训练的ResNet-18准确率下降但小扰动鲁棒性提升。消融显示重学习对各架构均有益，损失源于再升温削弱了对训练集的拟合。数据极少时，无梦境的快照蒸馏式重学习会逐轮累积过置信，记忆与梦境各自抑制大部分漂移，二者结合可消除漂移。

Conclusion: 模型退火以极低额外推理代价将训练预算重新分配于知识巩固，在AdamW架构家族上稳定优于交叉熵与快照蒸馏，并改善校准与鲁棒性；记忆平均预测与对抗梦境是防止过置信漂移的关键组件，而再升温调度对ResNet-18不利。

Abstract: A small labelled dataset is memorized long before the training budget runs out, and the one-hot labels that remain to be fitted then mostly sharpen confidence. Model annealing spends the rest of the budget on review and consolidation, relearning from the network's own knowledge. Training is divided into episodes whose learning rate is raised again and cooled, every cooled state joins a memory of past selves, and each new episode learns from the labels, from the tempered predictions of this memory, and from adversarial dreams sought where student and memory disagree most. The memory averages predictions rather than weights, and the deployed network is the last student, so inference costs nothing extra. Across CIFAR-100, Tiny-ImageNet and Imagewoof, four architecture families and 5\% to 100\% of the labels, model annealing raises the top-1 accuracy of the AdamW-trained ConvNeXt, ViT and MLP-Mixer over cross-entropy training in 28 of 33 configurations, by 1.3 points on average, and is on par in accuracy with snapshot distillation, the closest competitor. On these families it is better than both in likelihood, calibration and robustness to corruptions and small adversarial perturbations. For an SGD-trained ResNet-18 it lowers accuracy but still improves robustness to small adversarial perturbations. Separating the reheated schedule from relearning shows that relearning helps every family and that the losses come from reheats, which undo most of this network's fit to its training set. How early the accuracy of a cross-entropy run saturates largely separates the AdamW families from ResNet-18 but does not predict the gain within them. With little data, relearning from the latest snapshot without dreams, as snapshot distillation does, compounds overconfidence in the AdamW families from episode to episode. The memory and the dreams each curb most of this drift, and together they remove it.

</details>


### [124] [TotalSegmentator Aorta Report: Automated Aortic Diameter Measurement at 11 Standardized Landmarks on CT Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17968)
*Jakob Wasserthal,Daniel Hinck,Martin Segeroth,Joshy Cyriac,Denis Berdajs,Jules Miazza,Maurice Pradella*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发并评估了一种开源全自动工具，可在任意CT图像上按指南在主动脉瓣环至分叉共11个标准化标志点测量主动脉直径，平均绝对误差约1.06 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动脉直径测量缺乏标准化、全自动且覆盖全主动脉（瓣环至分叉）的开源工具，人工测量存在观察者间变异，需依从指南进行可重复的自动测量。

Method: 回顾性单中心研究，纳入230例患者（239次动脉期CTA，中位年龄65岁，163例男性，2025年4月至2026年10月，其中209例为心电门控）。工具基于TotalSegmentator对主动脉分支与椎体的分割定位11个标志点，并在各点测量最大直径；由资深放射科医师审核并按需修正自动测量以建立参考标准。采用平均绝对误差（MAE）、偏倚及>2 mm差异比例评价一致性。

Result: 全标志点MAE为1.06 mm（95% CI: 0.91, 1.22），86.6%（95% CI: 84.4, 88.5）的差异≤2 mm；工具测量值略大（偏倚0.45 mm）。Valsalva窦MAE最大（2.17 mm）。1.2%的标志点自动测量失败。检查水平MAE在病变类型、动脉粥样硬化分级（P=.95）及心电门控（P=.14）间无显著差异；伴附壁血栓者MAE>2 mm的比例更高（50% vs 20%，P=.03）。

Conclusion: 该工具可在全主动脉11个标志点以约1 mm的MAE测量管径，优于已报道的观察者间变异；主动脉根部及伴附壁血栓时一致性较低。工具已开源（https://github.com/wasserth/TotalSegmentator）。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a fully automated, open-source tool that measures aortic diameters at 11 standardized landmarks from the aortic annulus to the bifurcation on any CT image, following current guideline recommendations.
  Materials and Methods: This retrospective single-center study included 239 arterial phase CT angiography examinations of 230 patients (median age, 65 years [range, 18-99 years]; 163 men) performed between April 2025 and October 2026 (209 electrocardiographically [ECG] gated). The tool locates landmarks from TotalSegmentator segmentations of aortic branches and vertebrae and measures the maximum diameter at each. A senior radiologist reviewed and, where needed, corrected the automated measurements to create the reference standard. Agreement was assessed with mean absolute error (MAE), bias, and differences greater than 2 mm.
  Results: Across all landmarks, MAE was 1.06 mm (95% CI: 0.91, 1.22), and 86.6% (95% CI: 84.4, 88.5) of differences were 2 mm or less. The tool measured slightly larger diameters (bias, 0.45 mm [95% CI: 0.34, 0.57]). The sinuses of Valsalva had the largest MAE (2.17 mm [95% CI: 1.59, 2.86]). For 1.2% of landmarks, the automatic measurement failed. Examination-level MAE did not differ significantly by pathology, atherosclerosis grade (P=.95), or ECG gating (P=.14). Examinations with mural thrombus more often had an MAE greater than 2 mm (50% [95% CI: 31, 69] vs 20%; P=.03).
  Conclusion: The tool measured aortic diameters at 11 landmarks along the entire aorta with an MAE of about 1 mm, below published interreader variability. Agreement was lower at the aortic root and with mural thrombus.
  Code availability: The tool is open source and freely available at https://github.com/wasserth/TotalSegmentator.

</details>


### [125] [AI-aided Decision-Making in Education - Uncertainty, Explainability, and Human Responsibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17858)
*Fabian Kieser,Paul Tschisgale,Peter Wulff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本章提出不确定性、可解释性与人类责任三项原则，作为教育场景中合法、审慎使用AI的指导框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI已深度融入教育（个性化学习、自动化评估、行政辅助），但深度学习与生成式模型的决策不透明性，导致结果难以理解、看似不公或错误时妨碍教育决策，因而亟需明确AI在教育中正当使用的原则。

Method: 概念性/论证性章节：以三项核心原则为分析框架——(1) 承认AI决策存在与人类决策类似的内在不确定性；(2) 强调可解释性，使AI应用的功能与结果对教育者和学习者透明可理解；(3) 强调人类责任，在AI开发与部署各阶段保持人类决策的不可替代性。

Result: 论证三项原则相互补充、缺一不可：不确定性要求合理校准信任，可解释性保障透明度与可理解性，人类责任确保问责归属；据此为利益相关者提供整合AI的系统性指引。

Conclusion: 不确定性、可解释性与人类责任是AI在教育中合法使用的必要条件；本章为教育系统审慎、有效整合AI提供了综合指南。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based systems have become increasingly integrated into various aspects of everyday life, including education, understanding how these systems make decisions is of paramount importance. AI holds significant potential to enhance educational practices through the facilitation of personalized and adaptive learning, automation of assessment procedures, and assistance with administrative tasks. However, the opacity of AI-based decision-making in education (especially with deep learning and generative AI models) can hamper decision-making, especially when outcomes appear unjustified or incorrect. In this chapter, we argue that three key principles (uncertainty, explainability, and human responsibility) are essential for the legitimate use of AI in educational settings. Acknowledging the inherent uncertainties in AI-based decisions, analogous to those in human decision-making, is fundamental. Explainability is critical for ensuring that the functioning and outcomes of AI applications are transparent and comprehensible to both educators and learners. Furthermore, human responsibility is crucial, particularly in human decision-making, which remains indispensable across all stages of AI development and deployment. By examining these three principles, this chapter provides a comprehensive guide for stakeholders seeking to integrate AI thoughtfully and effectively into the educational system.

</details>


### [126] [An AI-enabled high-resolution chlorophyll-a forecasting model that outperforms the operational global biogeochemical system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17704)
*Yong Jiang,Zheng Jiang,Gaowei Zhang,Zengzhou Hao,Suixiang Shi,Chi Zhang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChlCast：一个AI模型，可在60°S–60°N、约4 km分辨率下生成10天逐日表层叶绿素a浓度预报，精度显著优于0.25°的Copernicus业务化海洋生地化预报系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表层叶绿素a浓度是海洋浮游植物变化的关键指标，其短期预报可为海洋生态系统监测与管理提供前瞻信息。但物理输运与生物过程的耦合作用使叶绿素a预报复杂，传统数值模式模拟此类耦合过程计算代价高。

Method: 提出ChlCast，融合因子化时空卷积与多尺度架构，从历史叶绿素a场和气候态信息中学习，输出近全球4 km分辨率的10天逐日表层叶绿素a预报。

Result: 相对业务化0.25° Copernicus全球海洋生地化预报系统：对卫星L4叶绿素a场，MAE和RMSE分别降低70.74%和47.63%；对独立BGC-Argo现场观测，分别降低35.03%和13.07%。沿岸案例显示4 km预报保留了被粗网格平滑削弱的沿岸梯度与局地浓度极大值。

Conclusion: 结果证明观测驱动的深度学习在近全球尺度高分辨率表层叶绿素a变化预报方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Surface chlorophyll-a concentration is a key indicator of marine phytoplankton variability, and short-term forecasts of its distribution provide advance information for marine ecosystem monitoring and management. However, interactions between physical transport and biological processes complicate chlorophyll-a forecasting, and simulating these coupled processes with conventional numerical models imposes substantial computational demands. Here we introduce ChlCast, an AI model that generates daily forecasts of surface chlorophyll-a concentration over a 10-day horizon at approximately 4-km resolution between 60$^\circ$S and 60$^\circ$N. ChlCast combines factorized spatiotemporal convolutions with a multiscale architecture to learn from historical chlorophyll-a fields and climatological information. Compared with the operational 0.25$^\circ$ Copernicus global ocean biogeochemical forecasting system, ChlCast reduces mean absolute error and root mean square error by 70.74% and 47.63%, respectively, against satellite-derived Level-4 chlorophyll-a fields, and by 35.03% and 13.07% against independent BGC-Argo in situ observations. Coastal case studies further show that the 4-km forecasts retain coastal gradients and localized concentration maxima that are weakened by coarse-grid averaging. These results demonstrate the potential of observation-driven deep learning for high-resolution forecasting of surface chlorophyll-a variability at the near-global scale.

</details>


### [127] [VLAN: Vision-Language Accessible Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18346)
*Jie Hu,Yu Zheng,Wenzhi Wu,Weixing Feng,Cunlei Cheng,Jiaming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Vision-Language Accessible Navigation (VLAN) 基准，包含 1,000 个场景、20K 条导航轨迹与 160K 关键帧，为不同"具身形态"（腿式/轮式机器人、人形、四足、视障与行动障碍者）提供差异化可通行性标注与专属动作，并用目标达成、碰撞感知与无障碍对齐三类指标评估，发现现有视觉语言模型难以稳定地按用户画像调整障碍相关性与安全准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可通行性（accessible mobility）本质上由用户/具身条件决定，而非场景固有属性——对一种具身可走的路径对另一种可能不可达。然而现有基准在固定且有限的具身设定下评估可通行性，缺乏跨具身、面向无障碍约束的数据与评测维度。

Method: 构建 VLAN 数据集：1,000 场景、20K 导航轨迹、160K 关键帧，覆盖多光照、环境条件、场景语义与路径几何；为每条轨迹提供针对腿式与轮式智能体（人形、四足机器人及视觉/行动障碍人群）的具身专属动作与无障碍特征；将日常障碍与无障碍标签划分为 Passable、Bypass、HighRisk、Blocked 四级；设计目标达成、碰撞感知、无障碍对齐三类指标替代纯目标式评估；并对现代视觉语言模型进行基准测试。

Result: 现代视觉语言模型在跨用户画像时往往无法一致地调整障碍相关性判断、路径选择与安全准则，即难以根据具身/用户约束自适应地做出可通行性决策。

Conclusion: 无障碍感知导航需要专门的数据集、任务与指标，超出传统具身导航评测范畴；VLAN 提供了该方向的开放基准（数据与代码已开源）。

Abstract: Accessible mobility is user-conditioned rather than scene-intrinsic, as walkable paths for one embodiment are inaccessible to another. Yet, existing benchmarks evaluate traversability under a fixed and limited embodiment. We present Vision-Language Accessible Navigation (VLAN), containing 1,000 scenes, 20K navigation trajectories, and 160K keyframes across diverse lighting, environmental conditions, scene semantics, and path geometries. For each trajectory, VLAN provides embodiment-specific actions and accessibility features for legged and wheeled agents, including humanoid and quadruped robots and people with visual or mobility impairments, while everyday obstacles and accessibility labels are categorized as Passable, Bypass, HighRisk, and Blocked. Unlike goal-only evaluation, our benchmark measures physical safety and alignment with user-specific constraints via goal-reaching, collision-aware, and accessibility-aligned metrics. We benchmark modern vision-language models and find they often fail to consistently adapt obstacle relevance, path selection, and safety criteria across user profiles, suggesting accessibility-aware navigation requires dedicated datasets, tasks, and metrics beyond conventional embodied navigation evaluation. All data and code open-source at https://github.com/InSAI-Lab/VLAN.

</details>


### [128] [Dancing Tokens on the Vocabulary Network from LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18248)
*Yuan-Chao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将六个开源LLM的输入嵌入构建为相似度网络，并把生成过程映射到该网络上，从图学习视角刻画LLM生成与预训练几何的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM逐token生成文本，但单次生成与预训练所学几何结构之间的关系不清，LLM的基本可解释性仍难以把握。

Method: 基于六个开源LLM的输入嵌入构建token相似度网络（并与协方差匹配的随机嵌入对比），追踪生成序列在该网络上的走向；结合逐层logit lens分析token在深度上的"落定"过程。

Result: 嵌入网络呈无标度、重尾分布，但比协方差匹配的随机嵌入更碎裂；蒸馏几乎不改变该拓扑，说明输入嵌入多被继承、知识空间由预训练决定；相邻生成token很少是图邻居，多数模型中其邻接频率不高于随机打乱序列；生成token虽集中于网络局部，但生成并非网络上的游走；多数模型中token在较深层才落定，重复退化模型局限于小的领域性子图。

Conclusion: 生成过程并非沿预定义嵌入网络的游走，但访问的token高度集中于局部子图；这些可观测指标可作为推理时的退化监测器，为从节点/链路预测的图学习角度微观理解LLM生成提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate text one token at a time, yet how a particular generation relates to the geometry learned in pre-training remains unclear. The fundamental explainability of LLMs is elusive. Here, we build a similarity network from the input embeddings of six open LLMs and map generation onto it. The token-embedding network is scale-free while heavy-tailed, as are random embeddings with matched covariance, but far more fragmented. Distillation barely changes this topology because input embeddings are largely inherited. The knowledge space is thus deterministic from pre-training. Consecutive generated tokens are rarely graph neighbours and, in most models, no more often than in shuffled order, so generation is not a walk on the predefined network, although the tokens it visits are concentrated within it. Through the logit lens in a layer- wise manner, tokens settle late in depth in most models, and repetitive models remain confined to small domain-specific sub-graphs. These observables could serve as inference-time monitors of degeneration. We thus provide a fresh perspective on understanding LLM generation in the microscopic way from node or link prediction by graph learning.

</details>


### [129] [Measured from the feed: the unsustainable growth of AI video generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17938)
*Tristan Coignion,Aurélie Bugeau,Gael Guennebaud,Anne-Laure Ligozat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对商用文生视频服务的绝对环境足迹（从训练到存储）进行前瞻性建模，指出减排需同时限制生成量增长与提升能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生视频已实现商业规模部署，但其更广泛的环境影响（据作者所知）研究不足；服务商很少披露视频产量与能耗数据，导致该领域缺乏量化评估。

Method: 实测两家商业平台的年度视频产出量，并构建涵盖训练与存储阶段的前瞻性模型，估算此类服务的绝对碳足迹。

Result: 在持续增长情景下，尽管技术效率提升，2030年排放仍将增长近75倍；若使用量维持在2026年水平，同等效率改进可使排放下降约五分之二。该估计为保守下界，仅覆盖众多AI服务之一。

Conclusion: 减排取决于同时限制文生视频生成规模的增长与实现显著的效率提升；单靠技术进步不足以抵消用量增长带来的排放，且全行业总影响将更高。

Abstract: Text-to-video generation runs at commercial scale, but its broader environmental impacts remain, to our knowledge, understudied. Providers rarely disclose video volumes or energy needs; we measured the yearly video output of two commercial platforms and built a prospective model of such services' absolute footprint from training to storage. Our projections suggest that reducing emissions depends on both limiting growth in video generation and achieving substantial efficiency gains. Under continued growth, emissions rise nearly seventy-five-fold by 2030 despite technological improvements. When usage remains at 2026 levels, the same improvements reduce emissions by about two-fifths. These estimates are a conservative lower bound: they cover one AI service among many; other services and uses would push the total higher.

</details>


### [130] [Tracing Actual Causes with Counterfactual Witness Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17937)
*Tim Woydt,Matej Zečević,Kristian Kersting,Moritz Willig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"见证目标"（witness targets）与祖先干预语法，构造有限的DAG见证图，并给出健全且完备地枚举所有实际原因（actual causes）的算法TrAC；在随机布尔SCM上的实验表明该方法对至多15节点的图可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Halpernian实际因果关系（在无环结构因果模型中判定特定情境下哪些事件导致特定结果）是法律责任、道德归责与因果损害评估的基础。尽管已有丰富的逻辑框架，但此前不存在健全且完备的枚举过程来找出给定情形下的全部实际原因，从而阻碍了责任归因、归责评估、因果损害分级等需要对完整原因集合进行聚合的下游任务。

Method: 引入"见证目标"——包含所有反事实见证的联合配置集合；结合"祖先干预语法"（ancestor intervention grammar），推导出一个有限的、有向无环的"见证图"（witness map）。证明对每个实际原因都存在某个 contingency set（偶然性集合），使其对应的联合干预集合作为该见证图的一个节点出现；据此提出TrAC（Tracing Actual Causes）算法。

Result: 证明了TrAC算法对任意目标事件的所有实际原因枚举是健全且完备的；在随机布尔结构因果模型上与现有单原因基线方法进行实证比较，展示了对高达15个节点的图的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了缺乏健全完备实际原因枚举程序的空白，TrAC为责任归因、归责评估与因果损害分级等需对完整原因集合聚合的下游任务提供了可行的枚举基础。

Abstract: Halpernian actual causation, the formal study of which events in a specific situation caused a specific outcome in acyclic structural causal models, underpins legal responsibility, moral blame, and the assessment of causal harm. Despite the rich logical framework, no prior sound and complete enumeration procedure is known for finding all actual causes in a given setting, blocking downstream tasks such as responsibility attribution, blame assessment, and causal-harm grading that aggregate over the full cause set. We close this gap by introducing witness targets: sets of joint configurations containing every counterfactual witness. In combination with an ancestor intervention grammar we derive a finite directed acyclic witness map. We prove that for every actual cause there exists a contingency set such that the joint intervention set appears as a node of this map, and present an algorithm for Tracing Actual Causes (TrAC) that is sound and complete for enumerating all actual causes of any target event. We compare TrAC empirically with existing single-cause baselines on random Boolean structural causal models, demonstrating practical feasibility for graphs of up to 15 nodes.

</details>


### [131] [Evaluating Large Language Models for Scientific Paper Search: Retrieval Success and Inference Latency on Mac Studio and NVIDIA A100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17929)
*Frederic Andriantseheno Rakoto,Yannis Bendi-Ouis,Xavier Hinaut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估在本地部署开源权重LLM进行网页辅助科学文献检索的可行性：测试了输入上下文512–128k token下的推理延迟（Mac Studio与双A100服务器），并用8个模型在7个科学仓库的70篇论文上以三级领域知识查询做检索评测。结果显示检索成功率强烈依赖模型选择与查询具体度；A100整体更快，但Mac Studio的统一内存可加载GPU配置下无法加载的更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献检索若能用本地部署的LLM助手，可降低对托管模型服务商的依赖，并让研究团队更好地掌控数据与基础设施。但开源权重模型在受限算力下能否胜任网页辅助检索尚不清楚，尤其是上下文长度增长带来的延迟与不同模型的检索表现。

Method: 1) 延迟刻画：在Mac Studio与双NVIDIA A100 GPU服务器上，测量输入上下文从512到128k token变化时的推理延迟。2) 检索评测：对8个模型，在来自7个科学仓库的70篇论文上执行检索任务，查询按领域知识水平分为三级。

Result: 检索成功率与所选模型及查询的具体程度高度相关；A100 GPU的推理速度总体快于Mac Studio；但Mac Studio的统一内存可容纳在测试GPU配置下无法加载的更大模型。

Conclusion: 本地部署的开源权重模型可用于网页辅助的论文检索，但性能受模型能力与查询具体度制约；硬件选择需在吞吐速度（A100）与大模型可加载性（统一内存）之间权衡。

Abstract: Scientific literature search can benefit from locally deployed LLM assistants, which reduce reliance on hosted model providers while giving research teams greater control over their data and infrastructure. This work investigates whether open-weight models can support web-assisted paper retrieval under practical computing constraints. We first characterize inference latency as the input context grows from 512 to 128k tokens on a Mac Studio and a server equipped with two NVIDIA A100 GPUs. We then evaluate eight models on the retrieval of 70 papers from seven scientific repositories, using queries that represent three levels of domain knowledge. The results show that retrieval success depends strongly on the selected model and the specificity of the query. And that A100 GPUs are generally faster than the Mac Studio, but its unified memory allows larger models that could not be loaded in the tested GPU configuration.

</details>


### [132] [When Cooperation Must Change: Semantic Strategy Unlearning in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17917)
*Wenhan Wu,Linghan Chen,Yudong Gao,Fan Yang,Honglong Chen,Kai Yin,Jiawei Jiang,Chuang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"语义协作策略遗忘"问题，构建CARVE，通过因果协作归因、保留感知子空间更新、反事实再协作与对抗审计，从MARL策略中移除特定约定并保持整体协作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署需求变化时，先前学到的协作约定可能过时甚至不安全（如缓存共享权限调整），需要移除特定约定但保留有用协作。运行时屏蔽保留了底层协调偏好、损害任务进度；现有强化遗忘与轨迹删除方法无法针对"角色不变"的约定。

Method: 1) 形式化问题为语义协作策略遗忘；2) CARVE采用因果协作归因识别有影响的交互；3) 保留感知的子空间更新以限制对无关行为的干扰；4) 反事实再协作助智能体学习替代方案；5) 对抗审计检验目标约定是否仍可被恢复；6) 构建分散式actor（仅接受通过审计的更新）直接部署。

Result: 在5个协作MARL基准上，名义约定完成率与最坏情况诱导率平均分别降低91.4%和83.3%；中位效用损失5.9%；推理时延与原策略相当。

Conclusion: CARVE能有效移除角色不变的协作约定，同时较好保留任务效用，且不增加推理开销，适配分散式部署。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) supports coordinated decision-making in distributed Web services, multi-robot control, and collaborative games through learned coordination conventions. When deployment requirements change, a previously useful convention may become obsolete or unsafe. For example, revised cache-sharing permissions in Web content delivery may require caches to abandon a learned division of storage and serving roles while maintaining efficient delivery. Teams must therefore remove the specified convention while preserving useful cooperation. This is challenging because the same relational pattern can be reproduced by different agents across different histories. Runtime shielding leaves the underlying coordination preference intact and can impair task progress, while existing reinforcement unlearning and trajectory-deletion methods do not directly target role-invariant conventions. This paper formalizes this problem as semantic coordination-strategy unlearning and proposes CARVE.CARVE uses causal coordination attribution to identify influential interactions and retain-aware subspace updates to limit interference with unrelated behaviors. Counterfactual re-coordination helps agents learn productive alternatives, while adversarial auditing tests whether the target convention can still be recovered. Accepted updates are merged into decentralized actors for direct deployment. Evaluation across five cooperative MARL benchmarks shows that CARVE reduces mean nominal convention-completion and worst-case elicitation rates by 91.4\% and 83.3\%, respectively.The median utility loss is 5.9\%, and measured inference latency matches that of the original policies.

</details>


### [133] [Alignment by Socialization: Learning Tradeoffs Under Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18531)
*Thomas Brennan,Andrew W. Lo,Chaoyi Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文用进化选择模型研究AI智能体在"完成任务"与"遵守行为规范"冲突时的取舍：长期增长率在每种动作被采取频率与环境奖励频率一致时最大；任务-only策略在仅奖励完成的环境有效但规范重要时失败，固定线性策略在高赌注下愈发偏向任务完成，学习策略则收敛到环境所奖励的权衡（由人类提供奖励时称为"社会化"）；经验会使社会化难以被后续任务-only训练逆转，但有限记忆会削弱该保护，硬性禁止可防止单次违规导致的部署终止但误禁亦有代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体被期望在完成任务的同时遵守行为规范，而这两类目标可能冲突。论文关注两点：进化选择如何塑造智能体在两者间的选择，以及经验能否使这些选择反映人类判断并在后续压力下保持稳健。核心动机是论证对齐不仅取决于赋予智能体的目标，还取决于塑造并维持其行为的环境。

Method: 构建进化选择模型：智能体的多个副本面对共同的不确定奖励状态，只有采取被奖励动作的副本才能繁殖。据此推导长期增长率最优条件，并比较三种策略——任务-only策略、固定线性策略、学习策略。进一步考察社会化在后续任务-only训练下的可逆性、记忆容量的影响，以及硬性禁止（含误禁代价）的作用，并用小型神经网络实验验证训练目标与记忆如何塑造结果。

Result: (1) 当每个动作被采取的频率与环境对其奖励的频率一致时，长期增长率最大化。(2) 任务-only策略在仅奖励完成的环境（沙盒）中表现良好，但在规范重要时失败。(3) 固定线性策略随赌注上升而愈发偏向任务完成，即使人类从不奖励该行为。(4) 学习策略收敛到环境所奖励的权衡；人类提供奖励时即"社会化"。(5) 积累经验使社会化在后续任务-only训练下更难逆转，但主流倾向改变前可能出现违规，有限记忆削弱该保护。(6) 当单次违规即可终止部署时，硬性禁止可加以防止，但误禁同样有代价。小型神经网络实验印证了上述规律。

Conclusion: 智能体在任务与规范间的取舍由进化/环境选择塑造，学习策略能内化人类奖励的权衡，且经验积累可提供一定抗逆转能力，但受记忆容量限制。对齐不仅取决于分配给智能体的目标，还取决于塑造并维持其行为的环境，因此环境设计与训练目标同等重要。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) agents are expected to complete tasks while following rules of conduct, but these objectives can conflict. We study how evolutionary selection shapes an agent's choices between them, and whether experience can make those choices reflect human judgments and withstand subsequent pressures. In our model, copies of an agent face a common uncertain reward state, and only those taking the rewarded action reproduce. We show that long-run growth is maximized when each action is taken as often as the environment rewards it. We compare three policies. A task-only policy thrives in a sandbox rewarding completion alone but fails when conduct matters. A fixed linear policy increasingly favors task completion as its stakes rise, even where humans never reward it. A learned policy instead converges to the tradeoffs rewarded by its environment; when humans supply the rewards, we call this socialization. Accumulated experience makes socialization harder to reverse under subsequent task-only training, although violations can arise before the prevailing disposition changes, and finite memory weakens this protection. When a single violation can end a deployment, a hard prohibition can prevent it, but a mistaken prohibition can also be costly. Experiments with small neural networks illustrate how training targets and memory shape these outcomes. Our results suggest that alignment depends not only on the objectives assigned to agents, but also on the environments that shape and sustain their behavior.

</details>


### [134] [Causally Constrained and Uncertainty-Aware Cardiopulmonary Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17819)
*Yuxiang Lai,Jiashuo Zhang,Liang Zhao,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架CE-Agent，将流行病学证据编译为风险因子权重的显式约束，再从队列数据估计基线风险与CT表型，实现肺癌与心血管疾病的联合风险分层与快速不确定性传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床风险计算器孤立评估单一疾病且忽略CT发现；无约束机器学习在小医院队列上过拟合、学到受治疗与疾病混杂的风险方向，并在记录不完整时掩盖不确定性。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段在未见结局前将同行评审的流行病学证据编译为对风险因子权重的显式约束；第二阶段用队列数据估计局部基线风险与新纳入的CT表型。部署时采用闭式不确定性传播处理缺失的临床或CT输入，毫秒级/患者，比蒙特卡洛快两个数量级以上，并输出校准的风险区间以指导病历复核与选择性影像检查。

Result: 在33家美国医院53,452名肺癌筛查参与者及6,814名多族裔成人中：肺癌筛查中心血管风险预测优于现有计算器，硬性动脉粥样硬化事件预测超过PCE并纠正其1.7倍风险高估；可从单中心/单扫描仪泛化至外部站点，所需样本量降低最多5倍。用于肺癌时与成熟筛查模型相当且校准良好，仅用风险因子方向也成立；联合两预测可改进单次筛查访视的心肺联合分诊。

Conclusion: 证据约束+数据估计的CE-Agent在保持可解释性与校准的同时提升跨疾病、跨站点泛化能力，支持单次低剂量CT筛查访视下的心肺联合分诊。

Abstract: Routine chest computed tomography (CT) captures pulmonary, cardiac, and vascular phenotypes that jointly stratify lung cancer and cardiovascular disease, the two leading causes of death in lung-screening populations. Yet clinical risk calculators evaluate each disease in isolation and omit CT findings, whereas unconstrained machine learning overfits small hospital cohorts, learns treatment- and illness-confounded risk directions, and conceals uncertainty when patient records are incomplete. Here we present the Causal Envelope Agent (CE-Agent), a two-stage framework that compiles peer-reviewed epidemiological evidence into explicit constraints on risk-factor weights before any outcome is seen, while estimating local baseline risk and newly added CT phenotypes from cohort data. At deployment, closed-form uncertainty propagation accounts for missing clinical or CT inputs within milliseconds per patient, running over two orders of magnitude faster than Monte Carlo sampling and returning calibrated risk intervals that direct chart review and selective imaging to borderline patients. Across 53,452 lung-screening participants from 33 US hospitals and 6,814 multi-ethnic adults, CE-Agent outperforms cardiovascular calculators in lung screening, exceeds the Pooled Cohort Equations on hard atherosclerotic events while correcting their 1.7-fold risk overestimation, and generalizes from a single hospital or CT scanner to external sites while lowering required sample sizes up to fivefold. Applied unchanged to lung cancer, CE-Agent matches established screening models with accurate calibration even when restricted to risk-factor directions alone, and pairing both predictions improves joint cardiopulmonary triage from a single screening visit.

</details>


### [135] [Imitation Bootstrapped Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18543)
*Tony H. Zhang,Connor Mattson,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出IBMARL框架，将IBRL扩展到合作多智能体场景，用R2BC训练模仿策略，通过中心化critic集成在每步选择模仿/RLa动作组合，并以门控模仿惩罚做软Q过滤行为克隆正则。在四个合作MARL基准上超越示范策略2.6-4.2倍速，并在两个任务上超过示范者，真实人类示范下亦优于纯模仿学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习难以直接复用示范加速，因为单人难以同时遥操作多个智能体；R2BC虽让单个示范者轮流控制某一智能体，但其得到的模仿策略质量受示范质量限制。因此需一种机制在保留示范先验的同时用RL超越示范性能。

Method: IBMARL：将IBRL扩展到合作团队，用R2BC训练各智能体的模仿策略。每个时间步每个智能体分别从模仿策略和RL策略各得到一个动作提议，由中心化critic集成在“两个动作的每智能体组合”中选择。正则项为门控模仿惩罚——软Q过滤的行为克隆损失，仅在critic更看好模仿动作处将RL策略拉向模仿策略。训练后各智能体单独执行其RL策略，无需中心化critic。

Result: 四个合作MARL基准上：两个任务中以2.6–4.2倍于示范式基线的速度超越其模仿策略；另外两个任务上是唯一能超越模仿策略的方法；四任务中两个超过示范者本身。使用真实人类示范时，IBMARL仍优于纯模仿学习，且达到超越的速度是最佳基线的两倍以上。

Conclusion: 将示范引导的模仿自举思想与R2BC结合，可在多人协作场景中用单人示范有效加速MARL，并通过中心化critic门控的软Q过滤BC正则克服示范质量上限，训练后保持去中心化执行。真实人类示范验证了实用性。

Abstract: Demonstrations are an effective way to accelerate single-agent reinforcement learning (RL), but extending this idea to multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) is non-trivial as it is difficult and often impossible for a single person to teleoperate multiple agents simultaneously. Round-Robin Behavior Cloning (R2BC) addresses this by letting a single demonstrator control one agent at a time while the others execute their current policies. However, the resulting imitation policies are limited by the quality of the demonstrations. We propose Imitation Bootstrapped Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (IBMARL), a framework that extends Imitation Bootstrapped Reinforcement Learning (IBRL) to cooperative teams and uses R2BC to train the imitation policies. At each time step, every agent receives one action proposal from its imitation policy and one from its RL policy, and an ensemble of centralized critics selects among the per-agent combinations of the two. A gated imitation penalty, a soft Q-filtered behavior cloning loss, regularizes each RL policy toward its imitation policy only where the critics value the imitation action more, so that after training each agent executes its RL policy alone, without the centralized critics. On four cooperative multi-agent RL benchmarks, IBMARL surpasses its imitation policies $2.6$-$4.2\times$ faster than demonstration-based baselines on two tasks, is the only method to surpass it on the other two, and exceeds the demonstrator itself on two of the four. With real human demonstrations IBMARL still surpasses imitation learning alone, and does so more than twice as fast as the best baseline.

</details>


### [136] [What Balanced Accuracy Cannot Identify in Neural-Signal Decoding under Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17925)
*Jiangshuo Xie,Zonghan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 受试者个体层面的类别变化无法由聚合指标刻画：平衡准确率无法识别被完全删除的类别，且"哪个编码器更好"在聚合与组内类别对比之间在11个cell中有10个不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时自适应（TTA）通常以转导方式评估，增益依赖更新后的集成模型，聚合分数的变化无法揭示受试者队列内部的异质性；作者追问聚合变化掩盖了什么。

Method: 在11个数据集-编码器cell（Siena/EEG与PTB-XL/ECG各4个、Sleep-EDF/EEG 3个）上评估full_ttt，固定划分与源模型评估；以受试者为单位，做精确符号检验并Holm校正且条件于共享流，辅以bootstrap区间与依赖性审查；直接检验组内类别对比而非从"对零检验"推断。

Result: 二分类下平衡准确率使"只报单类"的解码器与两类各50%的解码器同分，聚合值无法识别停止输出的类别：占受试者样本约十分之一的类别在112个受试者-cell中有26-47个未被预测（对照为7-9）。固定队列、划分与更新规则时，组内类别对比随编码器而变：Siena六对编码器中6对里2对在名义参考下、1对在fold级参考下通过依赖性审查；Sleep-EDF十对分期中2对通过run聚类门限；聚合与对比在11个cell中有10个指向不同编码器。

Conclusion: 聚合分数可掩盖个体层面的损害，但无法区分均匀损失与被删除的类别；类别层面变化应与聚合指标同时报告，受试者层面变化也应并列报告，且两类结论都受限于cell、更新规则与流的构造（流交织效应随状态共享由+3.5pp翻转为-2.4pp）。

Abstract: Adaptation for neural-signal classification is usually transductive: each batch is scored after updating on it, and the gain depends on the adapted model [4]. This paper asks what an aggregate change fails to characterise inside the cohort. Full test-time adaptation (full_ttt) was evaluated in 11 dataset-encoder cells (four each on Siena/EEG and PTB-XL/ECG, three on Sleep-EDF/EEG), with splits and source-model evaluation fixed; the subject was the unit, exact sign tests were Holm-corrected and conditional on the shared stream, and bootstrap intervals accompanied a dependence audit. Two results of different kinds follow. In the binary case balanced accuracy gives a one-class decoder the same value as one reporting both classes at 50%, so the aggregate cannot identify a class that stopped being reported; a class covering a tenth of a subject's samples went unpredicted in 26-47 of 112 subject-cells, against 7-9 for the controls, which is a descriptive count. With cohort, split and update rule fixed, the within-subject class contrast follows the encoder, tested directly rather than inferred from tests against zero: two of six Siena encoder pairs survive the dependence readings at their nominal reference and one at a fold-level reference, while two of ten Sleep-EDF stage pairs pass the run-clustered gate at its nominal reference; aggregate and contrast named different encoders in 10 of 11. The protocol bounds both results, since a stream-interleaving effect reverses sign with state sharing (+3.5 to -2.4 pp). An aggregate score can hide individual harm, as [1] showed, but it cannot distinguish a uniform loss from a deleted class; class-level change should therefore be reported alongside the aggregate and subject-level change alongside it, with both results conditional on the cell, the rule and the stream construction.

</details>


### [137] [LLM-Guided Cluster Lifecycle Management: Deciding When and How to Evolve Behavioral Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17616)
*Himanshu Kulkarni,Siddharth Krishnamurthy,Jianhong Xin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出用LLM原地做聚类生命周期决策（分裂/合并/退役/创建/不动），按标签信息丰富度预设"统计→统计+语义"策略，在三个数据集上F1最高且干预更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户/物品交互上的行为簇会随行为演化而漂移（分裂、收敛、消失、新生），但主流维护范式（阈值触发、MONIC式每窗口重聚类并追踪迁移）都把决策硬编码进阈值或重聚类规则，缺乏灵活性与整体视角。

Method: 以无阈值的全种群视图（簇统计健康度、标签、成员文本、残差质量）作为输入，让LLM逐簇决定动作（split/merge/retire/create/no-action），且不重新聚类。因有效信号通道取决于标签信息量，预先固定"丰富度条件化策略"：标签区分度低时用统计，高时用统计+语义融合；统一使用一个基础prompt模板。

Result: 在YooChoose(0.17)、MOOC(0.50)、News-Category(0.70)三个标签丰富度递增的数据集上，策略选定的LLM模式三者F1均最高，干预次数显著更少、克制性更强（配对Wilcoxon p≤0.003，bootstrap 95%CI不含零），优于调优阈值启发式与MONIC强化基线；增益更多体现在精确率而非召回率。随丰富度上升，有效模态由统计转向语义/融合：语义在YooChoose有害、融合在MOOC近似中性、在News上语义通道必需。

Conclusion: 单一prompt模板与固定策略可跨三数据集通用，而调优基线跨数据集迁移时性能崩塌。作者将该模态转移视为三数据集上的从业者诊断信号，而非已验证规律。

Abstract: Behavioral clusters learned over user or item interactions drift as behavior evolves: some fragment into sub-behaviors, some converge, some disappear, and new patterns emerge. Two paradigms dominate the maintenance of evolving clusterings. Threshold triggers fire a pre-specified action when a hardcoded statistic crosses a cutoff; refit-and-track (MONIC-style) re-clusters every window and infers transitions from how the new clusters align with the previous window's. Both hardwire the decision into a threshold or a re-clustering rule. We study an alternative in which a large language model (LLM) makes the lifecycle decision in place. Given a threshold-free, whole-population view of the current cluster set (statistical health, labels, member texts, residual mass), the LLM decides per cluster whether and how to act (split, merge, retire, create, or no-action) without re-clustering. Because the useful signal channel depends on how informative the labels are, we fix a richness-conditioned policy in advance: statistics when label distinctiveness is low, fused statistics+semantics when it is high. On three public datasets spanning label richness (YooChoose 0.17, MOOC 0.50, News-Category 0.70), the policy-selected LLM mode obtains the highest F1 on all three, with far fewer interventions and stronger restraint than tuned threshold heuristics and a MONIC steelman (paired Wilcoxon p <= 0.003, bootstrap 95% CIs excluding zero). The gain is concentrated more in precision than recall. The useful modality shifts from statistics to semantics/fusion as richness rises. Semantics hurt on YooChoose, fusion is roughly neutral on MOOC, and the semantic channel is required on News. One base prompt template and one fixed policy serve all three datasets, whereas the tuned baseline collapses when moved between them. We treat this shift as a practitioner diagnostic observed on three datasets, not a validated law.

</details>


### [138] [When Does Less Information Pay? An Exact Finite Atlas of Representation, Payoff, and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17583)
*Aleksandr M. Shtraukh-Adamsky,Vladimir M. Adamsky,Irina G. Voloshina,Artemiy D. Spitsyn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过枚举四态世界中所有可行表示与二元动作规则，论文给出表示保留的信息量与其决策价值之间关系的完全普查：信息更少的表示有时反而获得更高收益，且这种优势完全取决于保留了哪些区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典直觉常把"保留更多世界信息"等同于"任务表现更好"，但存在反例：保留1比特的表示能完美完成任务，而平均保留1.5比特的表示只有75%准确率。论文希望在一个可完整枚举的最小模型中，系统地厘清信息量（$I(W;R)$）与决策价值（任务准确率/收益）之间的关系。

Method: 在统一的四态世界上枚举所有可容许表示与二元动作规则；两种比较方式：(1) 固定其他组件、替换单一组件；(2) 为每个模块单独选择最优动作规则。考察三个任务及其混合任务，以及四种假设的成本费率，进行精确、完备的有限集合普查。

Result: 部分比较中信息更少的表示收益更高，另一些中信息更多的表示收益更高；也存在信息量不同但收益（准确率）相等的表示对。若表示保留全部已有区分并新增区分，其零成本最优收益不会下降。低信息优势取决于保留了哪些区分；表示替换下对立优势数量相等源于编码对称性；在更广比较中份额取决于重复实现的计数方式；任务集或资源价格变化可改变固定表示对中的占优方。

Conclusion: 这些结果是特定有限构造与比较集合的精确、完备普查。该"图集"为考察动作约束、计数规则与成本假设如何影响比较提供了可复现的范例模型；其意义在于例示信息量与决策价值之间已有的区别，而非提出两者关系的新普遍定律。

Abstract: A representation that retains one bit about a four-state world can solve a task perfectly, while another that retains 1.5 bits on average reaches only 75% accuracy. The difference lies in which distinctions each representation preserves. We examine this relationship in a uniform four-state world by enumerating all admissible representations and binary action rules. Constructions are compared both by replacing one component while holding the others fixed and by choosing the best action rule separately for each module. We consider three tasks, their mixtures, and four separate hypothetical cost tariffs.
  In some comparisons, the representation retaining less world information has the higher payoff; in others, the more informative representation does. There are also pairs with different information amounts but equal payoff--that is, equal task accuracy. If a representation preserves all existing distinctions and adds new ones, its best zero-cost payoff cannot decrease. Lower-information advantages therefore depend on which distinctions are retained. Equal counts of opposing advantages under representation replacement follow from coding symmetry. In broader comparisons, the shares depend on how repeated realizations are counted. A different task set or resource price can change which member of a fixed pair performs better.
  These results are exact, complete censuses of the specified finite sets of constructions and comparisons. The atlas provides a reproducible worked model for examining how action constraints, counting rules, and cost assumptions affect comparisons. It illustrates established distinctions between information quantity and decision value rather than proposing a new general law of their relation. $I(W;R)$ measures retained world information, and payoff measures task accuracy.

</details>


### [139] [Verifiable pXRD: Hill-climbing pXRD structure determination with LLM agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17539)
*Samuel Chen,Jiaru Bai,Austin Henry Cheng,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将粉末X射线衍射(pXRD)结构解析建模为可验证的智能体搜索问题，提出pXRD-Bench基准(200个模拟单相问题)，并扩展至实验多相pXRD的自反演化框架，通过自生成合成问题为智能体提供可验证训练经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从pXRD自主确定晶体结构是自驱动材料实验室的关键瓶颈，且实验样品结构未知、缺乏已解析的实验测量作为训练数据。

Method: 将pXRD结构解析表述为可验证的智能体搜索：推理时用正演模拟谱图一致性评估候选，评估时用与已知参考的结构匹配验证。构建pXRD-Bench(200个模拟单相问题，覆盖分布内、挑战性与多晶型结构、受控的成分与拓扑分布偏移)。进一步提出自反演化框架用于实验多相pXRD：可替换的实验规格定义样品族与测量条件，evolver智能体利用已知生成结构与测量参数诊断求解器失败，产出持久化指导与可执行工具，再交给测量谱图上的智能体使用。

Result: 智能体适配将选定结构回收率从27%提升至69%，重复采样与验证器引导的推理时扩展带来进一步增益；在15条固态合成实测谱图上，演化工具包提升了平均谱图一致性，并迁移出类似物替换流程，回收了参考库中缺失的物相。

Conclusion: 从可验证的合成经验中学习，可提升对结构未知的实验样品的智能体表征能力，且无需已解析的实验测量数据集。
