Abstract: Autonomous crystal-structure determination from powder X-ray diffraction (pXRD) remains a key bottleneck for self-driving materials laboratories. We formulate the task of pXRD structure determination as a verifiable agentic search problem: candidate structures can be assessed during inference through forward-simulated profile agreement and during evaluation through structural matching to a known reference. To benchmark progress, we introduce pXRD-Bench, a suite of 200 simulated single-phase problems spanning in-distribution structure determination, challenging and polymorphic structures, and controlled compositional and topological distribution shifts. Agent adaptation raises selected structural recovery from 27% to 69%, with further gains from repeated sampling and verifier-guided inference-time scaling. We extend this approach to experimental multiphase pXRD through a reflexive evolution framework that learns from self-generated synthetic problems. A replaceable laboratory specification defines the sample family and measurement conditions. An evolver agent uses the known generating structures and measurement parameters to diagnose solver failures and develop persistent guidance and executable tools, which are then provided to agents on measured patterns. This supplies verifiable training experience without requiring a collection of solved experimental measurements. On 15 measured patterns from solid-state synthesis, the evolved package improves average profile agreement and transfers an analogue-substitution procedure that recovers a phase whose structure is absent from the supplied reference library. These results demonstrate how learning from verifiable synthetic experience can improve agentic characterization of experimental samples whose structures are not known in advance.

</details>


### [140] [Towards Explainable Sleep Stage Classification using Single-Channel Electroencephalogram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18434)
*Gurinder Kaur Sandhu,Jude Koenig,Ankita Shukla,Shruti Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将SleepXAI框架扩展为对五种XAI方法的系统比较，并以AASM定义的EEG频带为临床参考信号，用Spearman秩相关评估归因保真度，发现Integrated Gradients（IG）在单通道EEG睡眠分期中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化睡眠分期已达较高基准准确率，但因缺乏可解释性而临床采用受限。现有工作未系统比较不同XAI方法在睡眠分期中的归因质量，故需填补该空白。

Method: 基于SleepXAI框架，在单通道EEG（便于临床部署）上系统比较五种XAI方法；以AASM定义的EEG频带为临床依据的参考信号，用Spearman秩相关评价归因保真度；在Sleep-EDF与DREAMT数据集上实验，并按OSA的AHI严重程度将DREAMT受试者分层为四组。

Result: SleepXAI模型在两个数据集上均取得较强分类性能；IG在Sleep-EDF全部五个睡眠阶段及DREAMT全部AHI分组上均取得最高平均归因保真度，在Spearman秩相关指标下优于其他方法。

Conclusion: 归因方法的选择是确保自动化睡眠分期系统可靠且具临床可解释性的关键因素，IG是较优选择。

Abstract: Automated sleep staging has achieved strong bench- mark accuracy; however, clinical adoption remains limited due to a lack of interpretability. In this work, we fill the research gap by extending the SleepXAI sleep staging framework to systematically compare five different XAI methods. Further, attribution fidelity is evaluated using Spearman rank correlation with AASM-defined EEG frequency bands, serving as clinically grounded reference signals. In this paper, we focus on single- channel Electroencephalogram (EEG), which is more amenable to practical clinical deployment compared to full polysomnography setups. Experiments are conducted on the Sleep-EDF benchmark dataset and the DREAMT dataset. In DREAMT, we stratified subjects based on OSA into four Apnea-Hypopnea Index (AHI) severity groups. We observed that the SleepXAI model achieves strong classification performance on both datasets. Among the evaluated methods, Integrated Gradients (IG) consistently achieves the highest mean attribution fidelity across all five sleep stages on Sleep-EDF and across all AHI groups on DREAMT, outperforming all other evaluated methods under the Spearman rank correlation metric. These findings highlight the importance of attribution method selection as a key factor for ensuring reliable and clinically interpretable automated sleep staging systems.

</details>


### [141] [VelServe: Taming Decode Jitters in LLM Inference with Tail-Calibrated Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18392)
*Ishaan Datta,Alind Khare,Koushik Kumar Dey,Ratnesh Jamidar,Anand Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出调度器 \sysname，通过按迭代延迟尾部测量校准每个解码步的预填充工作量，抑制解码抖动，在聚合与分离式服务下显著提升SLO goodput。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式LLM服务需满足TTFT与TBT的延迟SLO。作者识别出聚合式与分离式服务中普遍存在“解码抖动”：任何一步超时都会拖慢整批请求，而长响应跨越数百步，即使极罕见的超时也会造成大量SLO违规。现有SLO感知调度器把预填充预算推到预测延迟恰好满足TBT目标，缺乏对预测误差与主机停顿的余量；分离式虽满足平均TBT却牺牲容量，且接入新请求的步骤仍会延迟。

Method: \sysname 依据自身迭代延迟的实测尾部（而非仅均值）来标定每一步解码所承载的预填充量：由SLO决定的“迟到步”稀有度反推所需余量，为每次迭代预留头部空间以抑制超时。余量会拖慢预填充，因此采用最短等待提示优先（shortest waiting prompt first）调度——当TTFT截止时间随提示长度增长时，被延后的长提示拥有更多可支配时间。在分离式部署下，则对请求接入进行间隔控制，使任一请求遭遇的慢接入步数不超过其P99目标所允许的数量。

Result: 在三种注意力机制族与多种负载下，\sysname 在Qwen3-8B与DeepSeek-V4-Flash上取得最强基线2.4–2.9倍的SLO goodput，在DeepSeek-V2-Lite上至少4.4倍；在八块GPU上采用P/D分离部署时，满足SLO的请求数超过SOTA基线的两倍。

Conclusion: 以迭代延迟尾部为导向的预填充预算标定、配套的最短提示优先与分离式接入间隔控制，能有效消除解码抖动带来的SLO违规，同时兼顾吞吐与延迟可预测性。

Abstract: Interactive LLM services must meet latency service-level objectives (SLOs) for the time to first token (TTFT) and the time between tokens (TBT), as streaming and soft real-time applications require predictable token delivery. We identify decode jitters in both aggregated and disaggregated serving. A jitter delays every request in the batch, and long responses span hundreds of steps, so even rare overruns cause many SLO violations. In aggregated serving, SLO-aware schedulers set each step's prefill budget until its predicted latency just meets the TBT target, leaving no room for mispredictions or host stalls. Disaggregation meets average TBT targets at a cost in capacity, yet the steps that admit new requests still run late.
  We present \sysname, a scheduler that calibrates how much prefill work each decode step carries using the measured tail of its own iteration latency. The SLO determines how rare a late step must be, and \sysname leaves enough headroom in each iteration to prevent overruns and meet the target decode latency. This headroom slows prefill, so \sysname serves the shortest waiting prompt first. When TTFT deadlines grow with prompt length, the long prompts it delays have the most time to spare. Under disaggregation, it spaces admissions so that no request waits on more slow admission steps than its P99 target allows. On three attention families and varying workloads, \sysname sustains 2.4--2.9$\times$ the SLO goodput of the strongest baseline on Qwen3-8B and DeepSeek-V4-Flash, and at least 4.4$\times$ on DeepSeek-V2-Lite. With prefill and decode disaggregated on eight GPUs, \sysname meets the SLO for more than twice as many requests as state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [142] [Experts Across Schemas: Agent-Guided Mixture-of-Relational-Experts for Cross-Database Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18315)
*Hiren Madhu,Sukanta Ganguly,Kiran Srinivasan,Smita Krishnaswamy,Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MoREGT（混合关系专家图Transformer）及一个标签盲LLM智能体，用更少预训练数据在RelBench上取得更优的跨库迁移预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同Web平台的关系数据库schema各异，难以跨平台迁移预测模型；关系基础模型靠不断扩大预训练语料获取提升，本文探究是否能以更优架构加目标库特有特征，用远更少的预训练数据获得相当收益。

Method: MoREGT：将行与单元格均表示为token；在注意力中加入行的log-age与外来键关系名称；按列元数据（而非取值）将单元格和行路由至专家。配套一个标签盲LLM智能体，读取目标库schema与列统计，生成经校验的单行派生列，不接触标签或模型分数。

Result: 在21个RelBench任务、6个数据库留一库预训练设置下，MoREGT骨干较同数据库预训练的Relational Transformer平均AUROC提升2.25点、平均nMAE提升5.83点；智能体派生列使平均AUROC由78.45升至79.63、平均nMAE由27.91降至26.98，两项指标均为对比方法中最高，包括预训练数据库数量约多400倍的RT-J。

Conclusion: 架构改进结合目标库特有元数据特征，可在大幅缩减预训练数据规模的前提下超越大规模预训练基线，说明数据规模并非唯一提升路径。

Abstract: Web platforms record user and item activity in relational databases whose schemas differ from platform to platform, which makes it difficult to transfer predictive models between them. Relational foundation models address this through cross-database pretraining, yet recent improvements have relied on increasingly large pretraining corpora. We ask whether a better architecture, together with features specific to the target database, can deliver comparable gains from far less pretraining data. We introduce MoREGT, a mixture-of-relational-experts graph transformer that represents both rows and cells as tokens, adds the log-ages of rows and the names of foreign-key relationships to attention, and routes cells and rows to experts by column metadata rather than by values. A label-blind LLM agent complements the model: it reads the target database's schema and column statistics and adds validated single-row derived columns, without access to labels or model scores. On 21 RelBench tasks with leave-one-database-out pretraining on six databases, the MoREGT backbone improves on the Relational Transformer pretrained on the same databases by 2.25 points of mean AUROC and 5.83 points of mean nMAE. The agent's columns raise mean AUROC from 78.45 to 79.63 and lower mean nMAE from 27.91 to 26.98, the highest means on both metrics among the compared methods, including RT-J, which pretrains on about 400 times as many databases.

</details>


### [143] [Do Machines Learn Deviance From Their Peers? A Randomized Test of Differential Association With AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18098)
*Murat Ozer,Arif Akgul,Amitabh Chakravorty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将AI智能体作为随机分配同伴的"社会学习"实验平台，以规避同伴选择偏差。在420个三人小组（1,260个智能体、7个模型）的预注册实验中，偏离型同伴提高了不诚实编码行为及其规范化辩护，诚实型同伴则降低之；监查不仅未抑制违规讨论，反而使其翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差异交往理论及社会学习理论主张，违规行为及其可接受性定义是通过与他人的互动习得的；但由于个体主动选择朋友，同伴效应与选择效应在观察研究中难以分离，该命题长期缺乏干净的因果检验。AI智能体只通过交流学习，且可被随机分配同伴，从而为消除选择偏差提供了新工具。

Method: 预注册实验，共420个三人小组（1,260个智能体，涵盖7个大语言模型），任务为无法诚实完成的编码任务。设置脚本化同伴三种条件：自称作弊（偏离同伴）、自称如实报告问题（诚实同伴）、沉默（对照）。一半小组被告知有审计者阅读其聊天记录。测量指标包括作弊率、作弊被正常化的表述比例，以及监控对违规讨论的影响。

Result: 相较沉默同伴，偏离同伴使作弊率上升（66.2% vs. 62.9%，OR = 1.96），诚实同伴使其下降（55.7%，OR = 0.35）。在作弊的智能体中，偏离同伴使"作弊是常态"的表述增加（13.7% vs. 3.4%），且常被表述为团队规范。监控并未抑制违规讨论，反而使其翻倍。同伴效应仅对尚未作出决定的模型成立。

Conclusion: AI智能体为在无选择效应的条件下检验犯罪学学习理论提供了新途径，并复现了差异交往/社会学习理论的核心预测；同时提示监控可能产生反效果，且同伴影响受个体既定倾向调节。

Abstract: Differential association theory and its extension, social learning theory, hold that rule-breaking, and the definitions that make it acceptable, are learned through interaction with others. Because people choose their friends, this claim has been hard to test. We addressed this selection problem with AI agents, which learn only through communication and can be assigned to peers at random. In a preregistered experiment, 420 teams of three agents (1,260 agents; seven AI models) worked on coding tasks that could not be solved honestly. A scripted teammate said that it had cheated (deviant peer), that it had reported the problem honestly (honest peer), or said nothing (silent peer). Half of the teams were told that an auditor read their chat. Compared with a silent peer, a deviant peer increased cheating (66.2% vs. 62.9%; OR = 1.96) and an honest peer reduced it (55.7%; OR = 0.35). Among agents that cheated, a deviant peer increased claims that cheating was normal (13.7% vs. 3.4%), often as the team's norm. Monitoring did not suppress prohibited discussion; it doubled it. Peers mattered only for models that had not yet decided. AI agents offer a new way to test criminological learning theories without selection effects.

</details>


### [144] [Interactive deep-learning based interface for brain tumour segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17983)
*Enora Giffard,Pierre Jannin,John S H Baxter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的脑肿瘤交互式分割系统，默认全自动运行，同时允许人类用户便捷地修正分割结果，以应对不同MR序列导致的灾难性分割误差，并通过准确性评估与专家可用性研究进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑肿瘤的切除手术与放射治疗均需要高精度的分割，但肿瘤在大小、形状、数量和位置上高度可变，且不同厂商MR序列存在差异，导致全自动深度学习分割出现灾难性错误，难以保证所有患者的精度。

Method: 设计并实现一个基于深度学习的交互式分割系统：默认情况下完全自动分割，并支持人类用户方便地对结果进行修正；通过自动分割准确性指标以及初步的观察性专家用户研究评估其可用性。

Result: 从自动分割准确性和系统可用性两个维度进行了评估，其中可用性通过初步的观察性专家用户研究（preliminary observational expert user study）进行考察。

Conclusion: 交互式修正机制可在保留自动分割效率的同时，弥补纯自动化方法在跨序列泛化上的缺陷，从而帮助确保所有患者获得足够高的脑肿瘤分割精度。

Abstract: Brain tumour segmentation from MR images is an integral part of their treatment, either through surgical resection or radiotherapy. Both applications require a high degree of precision that can be difficult to ensure for all patients due to the high degree of variability in the size, shape, number, and location of these tumours. This leads to difficulties with their automatic segmentation via deep learning, which can display catastrophic segmentation errors when presented with images that differ, even subtly, in terms of the specific MR sequence used, which varies from vendor to vendor. This article presents a deep-learning-based interactive segmentation system that, while being fully automatic by default, has the capability of being easily corrected by human users, allowing them ensure sufficiently high brain tumour segmentation accuracy for all patients. This interface is evaluated both in terms of the accuracy of its automatic approach as well as its usability through a preliminary observational expert user study.

</details>


### [145] [Soteria: Formally Verified Planning with Runtime Enforcement for Safe LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17880)
*Mike He,Ankush Desai,Sharad Malik,Aarti Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Soteria框架，通过可验证的层次化规划与运行时规范强制，同时保证LLM智能体的任务效用与安全性，任务成功率约为最佳基线的1.7倍且零违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步工具调用的LLM智能体需同时满足任务完成（效用）与符合领域策略/正确性约束（安全），二者存在张力：拦截不安全动作虽避免违规，却使智能体无原则性恢复路径而陷入停滞；现有方法多以牺牲一方换取另一方。

Method: 在执行前生成结构化层次化计划，并在任何工具调用之前对规范进行形式化验证；执行中该已验证计划承担双重角色：确保轨迹符合规范，并在不安全动作被拦截时提供恢复引导；同时配合运行时的规范强制机制。

Result: 在覆盖多样化工具使用任务的多个基准上，Soteria平均任务成功率达到最佳基线的1.7倍，且无任何策略规范违规。

Conclusion: 安全与效用无需权衡，通过前置形式化验证的层次化规划与运行时强制可实现二者兼得。

Abstract: LLM agents that execute multi-step tool calls must satisfy two objectives simultaneously: completing the user's task (utility) and conforming to domain policies and correctness constraints (safety). These objectives are in tension -- blocking an unsafe action prevents a violation but can leave the agent stranded with no principled way to recover. Existing approaches sacrifice one objective for the other. We introduce Soteria, a framework that reconciles safety and utility through verified hierarchical planning and runtime enforcement of specifications. Before execution, the agent generates a structured plan that is formally verified against the specifications prior to any tool invocation. During execution, the verified plan serves dual roles: it ensures that the agent takes specification-conformant trajectories and provides guidance when unsafe actions are blocked. Across multiple benchmarks covering a diverse range of tool-use tasks, Soteria achieves 1.7 times the average task success rate of the best baselines, with no violations of policy specifications, demonstrating that safety and utility need not be traded off.

</details>


### [146] [DECODE: Diagnosing Interpretability Failures in Automated Interpretation Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17856)
*Dohyun Kim,Jaeung Lee,Donghee Hong,Jaemin Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DECODE可视化分析系统，将人工监督重新引入LLM自动解释流程，通过三阶段诊断工作流隔离可解释性失败来源，并用多解释间一致性作为可靠性代理、三阶段主动种子协议推荐高优先级样本人工复核，在16,384个稀疏自编码器特征上验证可高效定位并归因失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM内部表征解释工具日益依赖LLM自身生成与评估解释，形成自指式自动化流程且缺乏人工监督；在无真值条件下，解释难以验证，可解释性失败也难以归因于工具提取的表征还是流程中所用LLM。

Method: DECODE可视化分析系统：将诊断工作流分解为三个递进阶段，逐层隔离从结构性缺陷到解释层错配的失败来源；借鉴定性分析中的编码者间共识，以独立生成的多份解释及其评估指标间的一致性作为可靠性代理；采用三阶段主动种子协议，主动推荐高优先级实例供人工审查以扩展人工检查规模。

Result: 在16,384个稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征上的案例研究表明，该方法可在大规模下高效识别并归因可解释性失败。

Conclusion: 将人工监督以可视化分析方式重新引入LLM自动解释流程，可有效缓解自指式自动化带来的可靠性风险，并为解释失败的来源定位提供可操作的诊断路径。

Abstract: Tools for interpreting the internal representations of Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly rely on LLMs themselves to both generate and evaluate explanations, creating a self-referential automated interpretation process with minimal human oversight. This automation raises significant reliability concerns, which are exacerbated by the absence of ground truth. In practice, it is difficult to validate explanations or to attribute interpretability failures to their source -- whether they originate from the tool's extracted representations or from the LLM used in the process. To address these challenges, we present DECODE, a visual analytics system that reintroduces human oversight into the automated interpretation process. Our approach decomposes the diagnostic workflow into three progressive stages, isolating failure sources ranging from structural defects to explanation-level misalignments. Drawing inspiration from inter-coder consensus in qualitative analysis, we utilize consensus among independently generated explanations and their evaluation metrics as a proxy for reliability. To further scale human inspection, we employ a three-phase active seeding protocol that strategically recommends high-priority instances for manual review. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach through a case study on 16,384 Sparse Autoencoder features, showing that it can efficiently identify and attribute interpretability failures at scale.

</details>


### [147] [Robustness Evaluation of GPU Cloud Pricing Benchmarks under Incomplete Observations: A Controlled Missingness Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17796)
*Jooya Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对固定等权中位数GPU租赁价格基准在报价缺失情形下做压力测试，比较四种掩码机制与四种基准层恢复方法：整源中断牺牲覆盖率（0.77）但条件零误差，构成变化型缺失保留0.86覆盖率却产生7.74对数点误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日度GPU租赁价格基准在市场价格报价缺失时可能失效，需评估固定等权中位数的稳健性及恢复方法的有效性。

Method: 在190个留出日、27个情景中共5800次蒙特卡洛实现，施加四种掩码机制与四种基准层恢复方法；另设仅用历史信息的层级消融实验，并检验可观测信号弃用规则对误差—覆盖率准则的影响。

Result: 整源中断使发布覆盖率降至0.77但发布日条件误差为零；构成变化型缺失覆盖率0.86但误差7.74对数点。仅历史信息的层级消融在5个构成变化情形下降低误差4.15–6.70对数点；可观测信号弃用规则在10个情形中0个改善主要误差—覆盖率准则。

Conclusion: 缺失机制类型显著影响基准误差结构；更丰富的过去信息集有助于缓解构成变化型缺失误差，而弃用可观测信号的规则无效。结论仅适用于观测到的报价测量，不涉及成交价格或真实缺失机制识别。

Abstract: Daily GPU rental-price benchmarks can fail when marketplace quotes are missing. This article stress-tests a fixed equal-weight median rule under four imposed masking mechanisms and four benchmark-level recovery methods. Across 5,800 Monte Carlo realisations in 27 scenarios on 190 held-out days, whole-source outages reduce publication coverage to 0.77 while preserving zero conditional error on published dates; composition-changing loss retains 0.86 coverage but produces 7.74 log points of error. A separate past-only stratum-level ablation reduces error by 4.15 to 6.70 log points in five composition-changing cases, using a richer information set. An observable-signal withholding rule improves the primary error-coverage criterion in 0 of 10 cases. Results concern observed asking-price measurements, not transaction prices or identified real-world missingness mechanisms.

</details>


### [148] [AutoRL: A Multi-Agent System towards Autonomous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17790)
*Simon Tang,Wenjie Shi,Yongjing Yin,Keheng Wang,Jialin Liu,Zheng Zhang,Linvo Lin,Xiangyuan Xue,Kan Ren,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多智能体系统 AutoRL，用于自动化强化学习训练流程，通过过程级监控与在线干预实现无人值守的 RL 训练，为递归自我改进（RSI）奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RSI 是实现超级智能的潜在路径，需 AI 自主完成完整训练流水线；RL 对提升能力与泛化至关重要，但其训练不稳定且需持续监督，自动化难度大。现有做法依赖端到端完整实验，无法像研究者那样在训练过程中持续调试。

Method: AutoRL 采用三个 RL 专用智能体角色：Perceptors 监测训练动态、rollout、评估与效率，在检测到异常、性能退化或停滞时触发审议；三个互补的 Proposers 基于共享证据与检查机制诊断问题并迭代修正候选干预方案；Orchestrator 审核建议，并通过在线热更新或基于检查点的恢复来实施获批改动。

Result: 在 Qwen3 与 LongCat-Flash-Lite 上的实验表明，AutoRL 优于静态 RL 配方和通用智能体；研究记录显示现有智能体能够胜任无人值守的 RL 监督与动态调整。

Conclusion: AutoRL 证明过程级、研究者式工作流的自动化 RL 训练可行；未来 RSI 的难点在于发展对高资源消耗验证具有价值的基础方法。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) is one of the most promising paths toward an AI singularity for superintelligence. Achieving this vision requires AI systems to undertake the full training pipeline for iterative improvement. Reinforcement learning (RL) plays an essential role in this cycle to improve model capabilities and generalization, yet automating RL remains difficult due to unstable training dynamics and the need for sustained supervision. We introduce AutoRL, a multi-agent system for automating RL training, as a cornerstone for future RSI. Different from conducting research by end-to-end complete experiments, AutoRL monitors the evolving training state and integrates process-level supervision and research into an ongoing RL training, following the practical workflow of an RL researcher. Specifically, AutoRL consists of three RL-specific agentic roles. Perceptors track training dynamics, rollouts, evaluation, and efficiency, triggering deliberation when they detect anomalies, degradation, or stagnation. Three complementary Proposers diagnose problems and refine candidate interventions through shared evidence and checks. The Orchestrator reviews their recommendation and applies approved changes through online hot updates or checkpoint-based resumption. Experiments on Qwen3 and LongCat-Flash-Lite demonstrate the superiority of AutoRL over static RL recipes and a general agent. The research records show that existing agents can handle unattended RL supervision and dynamic adjustment. The difficulty for future RSI lies in developing fundamental methods that are valuable for resource-consuming validation.

</details>


### [149] [Co-Sight Harness: Externalized Control Assets and Deterministic Enforcement for Long-Horizon Business Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17696)
*Luyao Wang,Xijun Zhu,Zhaohui Hao,Xiwen Zhang,Xingyu Wen,Weishan Li,Yuyang Liao,Yanqin Gao,Yongsheng Du,Lingjun Huang,Shaowei Zhao,Gang Li,Qifan Yu,Baoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种不改变大模型骨干、将工作流定义/状态/领域知识外置为版本化控制资产的方法，配合状态机与可机器校验的验收门禁，并在三个超200步的生产级工作流上验证可靠性与效率大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程业务工作流存在两类失败来源：随着活动链变长而累积的误差，以及所需知识与用户请求词汇不一致导致的知识鸿沟。作者希望在不动模型的前提下提升工作流可靠性。

Method: 保持骨干模型固定，把工作流定义、状态和领域知识迁移为模型外部的版本化控制资产；执行时由状态机与机器可检查的验收门禁强制执行这些资产；由独立审计器对执行轨迹评分；资产仅在通过回归检查与人工审批后才更新；并通过受控消融分离两类失败来源。

Result: 在三个超过200步的已部署工作流上评估。Domain A的120例端到端集合中，无人值守完成率从50%升至100%，检索召回从56%升至91.67%，端到端时延从440分钟降至159分钟，场景分析召回从45%升至93%。另两个由未参与资产编写的团队接管，分别用4天和7天完成。

Conclusion: 通过将控制逻辑与知识外置并非侵入式地约束固定模型，可显著提升长程工作流的可靠性与效率，且资产具备可迁移性，便于其他团队快速接管。

Abstract: Long-horizon business workflows fail in two ways: errors accumulate as a chain of activities lengthens, and the knowledge an activity needs is written in a different vocabulary from the user's request. We study whether such workflows can be made reliable without changing the language model. Our method keeps the backbone fixed and moves the workflow definition, state and domain knowledge into versioned control assets outside the model. A state machine and machine-checkable acceptance gates enforce these assets during execution; an independent auditor grades the resulting trace. The assets are updated only after a regression check and human approval .We evaluate the method on three deployed workflows exceeding 200 steps. On Domain A, a 120-case end-to-end evaluation set shows unattended completion rising from 50% to 100%, retrieval recall from 56% to 91.67%, and end-to-end latency dropping from 440 to 159 minutes, with scenario-analysis recall rising from 45% to 93%. Two other domains were taken over by teams that did not write the assets, one in four days and one in seven days. A controlled ablation separates the two failure sources. The code is available at https://github.com/ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight/tree/master/Co-Sight%20Harness.

</details>


### [150] [ARCHER: From Rationale Imitation to Executable Reasoning in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18494)
*Dhruv Dixit,Khushi Patel,Congcong Xu,Min Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ARCHER 两阶段框架，通过可执行动作轨迹监督与非对称分层强化学习，训练小型语言模型产出可执行、可审计的数学推理轨迹，在 5 个 10B 以下骨干上平均提升 5.51 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型适合本地、低成本、隐私敏感部署，但在需要分解、精确计算、工具调用与验证的数学推理任务上仍不可靠。现有链式思维监督只是模仿解释性文本，缺乏工具落地与可验证性。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段将自由式 CoT 理由编译为带类型的动作轨迹（推理步骤、计算器调用、确定性运行时观测、验证动作、最终答案），把监督目标从文本模仿转为工具落地的推理策略学习；第二阶段用非对称分层强化学习精化策略，采用分组且条件门控的轨迹奖励，覆盖答案正确性、工具落地、过程规范与轨迹对齐。

Result: 在 5 个标准 10B 以下骨干上，宏观平均精度较最强工具增强基线提升 5.51 个百分点，其中 MATH-500 提升 8.95，AIME-2024 与 AIME-2025 分别提升 5.33 与 5.34；在另外两个推理专用 7B 骨干上的评测表明，经过推理强化学习或蒸馏后，可执行落地仍带来增益。

Conclusion: 可执行监督结合奖励引导的行为精化，是训练紧凑型数学推理器的通用且有效的训练范式。

Abstract: Small language models are attractive for local, low-cost, and privacy-sensitive deployment, but they remain unreliable on mathematical reasoning tasks that require decomposition, exact computation, tool use, and verification. We introduce \textbf{ARCHER}: \textbf{A}ction-trace \textbf{R}einforcement for \textbf{C}alculator-grounded \textbf{H}ierarchical \textbf{E}xecutable \textbf{R}easoning, a two-stage framework for training small language models (SLMs) to produce executable and auditable reasoning trajectories. In the first stage, ARCHER compiles free-form chain-of-thought rationales into typed action traces containing reasoning steps, calculator calls, deterministic runtime observations, verification actions, and final answers. This shifts the supervision target from imitating explanatory text to learning a tool-grounded reasoning policy. In the second stage, ARCHER refines this policy through asymmetric hierarchical reinforcement learning, using grouped and conditionally gated trajectory rewards for answer correctness, tool grounding, process discipline, and trace alignment. Across five standard sub-10B backbones, ARCHER improves over the strongest tool-augmented baseline by 5.51 percentage points on macro-average across benchmark accuracies, including gains of 8.95 points on MATH-500 and 5.33 and 5.34 points on AIME-2024 and AIME-2025. Evaluations on two additional reasoning-specialized 7B backbones further show that executable grounding remains beneficial after reasoning-focused reinforcement learning or distillation. These results demonstrate that executable supervision and reward-guided behavioral refinement provide a general and effective training paradigm for compact mathematical reasoners.

</details>


### [151] [Safety Side Effects of Preventative Persona Steering: An Empirical Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17620)
*Sabrina Tang,Nikolai Vo,Carson Murillo,Chira Sukumarnpiti,Sarah Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验 persona vector 的"预防性引导"(preventative steering)是否真的安全中性：在三个模型上对谄媚与幻觉向量做预防性引导，除特质表达、连贯性和 MMLU 外，额外测量三项安全基准。结果显示预防性引导仍会改变拒答行为，且改变幅度取决于特质，因此不能认定其安全中性，引导方法应在多个安全基准上评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chen et al. (2025) 提出 persona vector 可表示谄媚、幻觉等高层行为方向，并推荐"预防性引导"（微调时加入该向量、评估前移除），理由是它能保持 MMLU 准确率。但现有验证仅衡量能力（capability），未考察安全行为是否受影响，故需补足安全维度的评估。

Method: 在三个模型上，对谄媚向量和幻觉向量分别实施预防性引导；评估指标包括特质表达（trait expression）、连贯性（coherence）、MMLU，以及三项安全基准，用以捕捉拒答行为变化。

Result: 预防性引导依然会改变模型的拒答行为，且变化幅度随特质不同而不同；MMLU 保持不变并不代表安全性不受影响。

Conclusion: 不能据此认定预防性 persona 引导是安全中性的；对引导方法的评估应覆盖多个安全基准，而非仅依赖能力指标。

Abstract: High-level behaviors such as sycophancy and hallucination correspond to directions in a model's activation space (Chen et al., 2025). These directions, known as persona vectors, can suppress undesirable traits either at inference time, subtracting the vector during generation, or through preventative steering, adding the vector during fine-tuning, so the optimizer never needs to encode the trait in weights, then removing the vector at evaluation. Chen et al. (2025) recommend preventative steering because it preserves Massive Multitask Language Understanding (MMLU) accuracy, but validations of this recommendation have measured capability alone. We primarily evaluate results on preventative steering of sycophancy and hallucination vectors on three models, measuring three safety benchmarks alongside trait expression, coherence, and MMLU. Our findings show that preventative steering still changes refusal behavior, but by an amount that depends on the trait and does not establish that preventative persona steering is safety-neutral, and steering methods should be evaluated on several safety benchmarks.

</details>


### [152] [Toward Interference-Agnostic RF Source Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17950)
*Maxence Albert,Raphaël Duroselle,Thibaud Ehret,Olivier Boeffard,Jean-François Bonastre*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文表明：专用射频干扰分离器在遇到未训练过的干扰类型时失效，甚至比不处理更差；而用同一套参数在全部“信号-干扰”对上训练的单一统一分离器，在测试时无需知道干扰类型，性能仍接近专用模型，且能泛化到训练集从未出现的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有射频同频干扰分离器多为“专用型”：每对信号与干扰类型各训练一个模型，并依赖对干扰类型的标签。然而在强干扰场景下接收机本身无法辨识干扰类型，标签不可得；论文旨在验证这种依赖的代价（误配时的性能崩塌），并给出无需类型标签的统一分离方案。

Method: 在全部信号-干扰对上联合训练单一分离器，测试时不提供任何干扰类型提示；采用两种无公共组件的架构交叉验证：ICASSP 2024 RF Challenge 的卷积 WaveNet，以及从音乐源分离迁移而来的 band-split transformer；以专用模型为基线，在训练覆盖的干扰对与训练集未出现的干扰上分别评测。

Result: 专用分离器用于非训练干扰时通常无法恢复信号，多数情形下使信号比不做处理更差。统一分离器参数规模与单个专用模型相当，在几乎每个干扰对上比特错误率（BER）都接近其替代的专用模型集合（一对更优、一对略差）；在训练集从未出现的干扰上，其表现与最佳专用模型相当。

Conclusion: 依赖干扰类型标签的专用分离范式并不稳健；“一模型通吃全部干扰对”的盲统一分离器可在同等模型规模下保持接近专用的性能，并省去部署时选择模型的决策负担，具备更好的泛化与实用性。

Abstract: Separating a radio-frequency signal of interest from a co-channel interference is mostly addressed with specialized separators, each trained for one pair of signal and interference types and therefore relying on a label that a heavily jammed receiver cannot identify. We show that this reliance is not benign: applied to an interference it was not trained on, such a separator usually fails to recover the signal, and in most of the cases we measure it leaves the signal worse than no processing at all. We then train a single separator over every pair, giving it no indication at test time of which one it faces, on two architectures with no component in common: the convolutional WaveNet of the ICASSP 2024 RF Challenge, and a band-split transformer that we adapt from music source separation. Both behave the same way. At the size of one specialized model, the unified separator stays close to the whole models it replaces, keeping the bit error rate close on almost every pair, better on one and worse on another. On an interference absent from every training set, it does as well as the best specialized model, without anyone having to choose which one to deploy.

</details>


### [153] [Adversarially Attacking Symbolic Vocabulary Vulnerabilities In LLM Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17910)
*Stephen Obadinma,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于LLM的攻击模型Symbolic Swapper，自动生成符号词汇被混淆的规划领域，用以揭示LLM智能体在联合符号解释与规划上的脆弱性；攻击可使基座模型乃至鲁棒规划框架的性能下降超过70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自主智能体日益需要完成自动化规划任务，但其规划能力不稳健：当任务定义中的符号词汇（动作、谓词、对象名称）不再提供常识线索时，LLM表现远不如符号规划器。近期推理模型在词汇混淆任务上有改善，因此需要系统检验其在更强混淆下的真实纯规划能力还剩多少。

Method: 将问题形式化为对抗攻击：设计 LLM 驱动的攻击模型 Symbolic Swapper，自动生成采用非标准符号词汇的混淆规划领域，系统性地破坏强推理模型的规划能力；并在基座模型与鲁棒规划框架上评估攻击效果，分析能力崩解的影响因素。

Result: 攻击可使基座模型及鲁棒规划框架的规划成功率下降超过70%；被成功混淆的模型虽仍表现出一定的领域理解能力，但推理质量显著退化，呈现出"能感知领域却无法在其上规划"的落差。

Conclusion: LLM 的规划能力在符号层面存在关键短板，现有鲁棒规划框架不足以保证可靠性；领域感知与在该领域下实际规划之间存在明显鸿沟，需针对符号解释与规划联合能力进行改进。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents, their abilities in navigating automated planning tasks have become essential. However, despite their strengths, LLMs' planning ability is not robust, particularly when they are not allowed to exploit commonsense cues in the symbolic vocabulary (e.g., names of actions, predicates, and objects) used to define planning tasks. This makes LLMs unreliable compared to symbolic planners. With recent reasoning models showing improved planning performance on tasks with obfuscated symbolic vocabularies, we utilize an adversarial attack framework to reveal to what extent the crucial weaknesses of LLM agents at joint symbolic interpretation and planning remain and whether they can reason properly when presented with severe cases. As such, our main contribution is devising an LLM-based attack model, which we call Symbolic Swapper, to automatically generate obfuscated planning domains with non-standard symbolic vocabularies that systematically break strong reasoning models' ability to plan. In doing so, we reveal how much LLMs' planning abilities can be further degraded under more advanced obfuscation schemes, allowing us to ascertain their success when having to rely on their pure planning ability. We find that attacks can decrease the planning performance of base models and even robust planning frameworks by over 70%. We further analyze the factors behind how LLM planning abilities break down and find that successful obfuscation causes models to appear to significantly degrade in reasoning quality despite them showing some inherent ability to understand the domain, revealing a gap in the perception of a task domain and being able to plan under it.

</details>


### [154] [Large Language Model-Empowered Intelligent Agent for Adaptive Fast-Charging Control of Lithium-Ion Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18140)
*Joey Chan,Haoyu Pan,Zhen Chen,ErShun Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个LLM赋能的智能体用于锂离子电池自适应快充闭环控制，通过PyBaMM构建跨5种化学体系、11组参数的大规模仿真语料，采用SFT+GRPO两阶段训练，在匹配能量吞吐要求下实现比传统协议与优化方法更长的循环寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快充是多目标控制问题（缩短充电时间、保持高能量吞吐、抑制加速老化与安全相关副反应）。传统协议易实现但适应性差；基于模型的优化和常规数据驱动控制依赖精确模型、昂贵的迭代搜索或任务专用策略，难以适应电池化学、工况与老化状态的变化。

Method: 构建LLM智能体闭环框架：智能体解析结构化电池状态、生成可执行充电动作、观测电池响应并持续调整决策。用PyBaMM在5种电池化学体系、11组参数集上构建大规模仿真语料，将状态-动作-响应轨迹转为结构化过程数据用于训练。两阶段策略：监督微调（SFT）从仿真示教中建立电池控制语义与可行决策先验；分组相对策略优化（GRPO）使智能体对齐涵盖能量吞吐、退化缓解与运行约束的多目标控制目标。

Result: 闭环仿真显示，在匹配能量吞吐需求的条件下，该智能体在异构电池配置上均获得比传统充电基线和基于优化的策略更长的循环寿命。

Conclusion: 该工作为将LLM智能体作为电池管理中的序贯决策者提供了基于仿真的概念验证，是迈向语言模型赋能物理能源系统控制的一步。

Abstract: Fast charging of lithium-ion batteries is a multi-objective control problem that requires reducing charging time and sustaining high energy throughput while suppressing accelerated degradation and safety-critical electrochemical reactions. Conventional protocols are easy to implement but have limited ability to adapt to variations in battery chemistry, operating conditions, and aging state. Model-based optimization and conventional data-driven control often require accurate models, costly iterative search, or task-specific policies. We develop a large language model (LLM)-empowered intelligent agent for adaptive battery fast-charging control. In this closed-loop framework, the agent interprets structured battery states, generates executable charging actions, observes the resulting battery response, and continuously adjusts subsequent decisions. We construct a large-scale simulation corpus with PyBaMM across five battery chemistries and 11 parameter sets and convert the resulting state-action-response trajectories into structured process data for agent training. Our two-stage strategy combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). SFT establishes battery-control semantics and feasible decision priors from simulation-derived demonstrations. GRPO aligns the agent with a multi-objective control objective covering energy throughput, degradation mitigation, and operational constraints. Closed-loop simulations show that, under matched energy-throughput requirements, the agent achieves longer cycling life than conventional charging baselines and optimization-based strategies across heterogeneous battery configurations. This study provides a simulation-grounded proof of concept for using LLM agents as sequential decision makers in battery management and takes a step toward language-model-enabled control of physical energy systems.

</details>


### [155] [Optimizing Drilling Trajectories in Multi-Material Structures Using Quantum Annealers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18121)
*Eneko Osaba,Antonio Rubio-Mateos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种量子混合优化方法，将多材料结构钻孔路径规划建模为带约束的路径问题，用D-Wave Leap CQM混合求解器求解，10–55孔基准实例验证其可处理多物理约束并生成节能钻孔序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钻孔轨迹规划直接影响制造能耗，而多材料结构存在材料属性异质、材料相关钻孔成本与位移能耗、刀具磨损惩罚等复杂因素，同时需满足开路径路由与同材料孔必须成组以避免频繁换刀等现实工业约束，传统方法难以统一处理。

Method: 将钻孔过程表述为约束路由问题：每孔必须访问，目标最小化总能耗，纳入材料相关钻孔成本、位移能耗与刀具磨损惩罚；约束包括开路径（非回路）路由和同材料孔强制分组；采用D-Wave Leap约束二次模型（CQM）混合求解器求解。

Result: 在10至55个孔、不同材料分布与成本矩阵的基准实例上进行评测，结果表明量子混合优化能够处理复杂多物理约束，并在真实制造条件下生成节能的钻孔序列。

Conclusion: 量子混合优化可用于多材料结构钻孔路径规划的实际工业场景，具备在复杂约束下生成低能耗加工序列的能力；摘要未给出与经典基线的定量能耗对比，后续需补充可扩展性与相对性能评估。

Abstract: In this work we present a quantum-hybrid optimization approach for planning drilling trajectories on multi-material structures, with the objective of minimizing total energy consumption. The proposed formulation models the drilling process as a constrained routing problem in which each hole must be visited while accounting for heterogeneous material properties, material-dependent drilling costs and displacement energy, as well as tool-wear penalties. Our method incorporates realistic industrial constraints, including open-path routing and mandatory grouping of holes belonging to the same material to avoid frequent tool-bit changes. The modeled problem is solved using D-Wave's Leap Constrained Quadratic Model Hybrid Solver. To evaluate performance, we benchmark instances ranging from 10 to 55 holes with varying material distributions and cost matrices. The results highlight the capability of quantum-hybrid optimization to handle complex multi-physics constraints and to generate energy-efficient drilling sequences under realistic manufacturing conditions.

</details>


### [156] [LACCO: Local Accumulation with Computation--Communication Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17991)
*Daniele Kopyshevskiy,Benjamin Th{é}rien,Nicolas Pinto,Edouard Oyallon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出两阶段算法 LACCO，将全局通信与本地计算重叠，同时保持外层更新无陈旧性，在低带宽下加速语言模型预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低带宽网络下训练语言模型需降低同步开销。DiLoCo 等本地优化方法仅每若干本地步通信一次，但外层梯度平均与外层更新仍在关键路径上造成停顿；已有的计算-通信重叠方法依赖延迟更新或副本本地更新，会导致收敛退化或副本漂移。

Method: LACCO（Local Accumulation with Computation-Communication Overlap）为两阶段算法：将全局通信与本地计算重叠，并对所有副本应用全局聚合的更新，且不在外层更新中引入陈旧性（staleness）。作者对光滑非凸目标给出收敛性保证。

Result: 在 FineWeb 语言模型预训练实验中，LACCO 一致优于 Eager 与 Delayed DiLoCo 等通信重叠基线；在模型规模扩展至 0.5B 参数、不同通信间隔及最多 16 个 worker 的设置下收益稳健，仅带来可忽略或完全可被重叠的计算/内存开销。

Conclusion: LACCO 在消除外层更新陈旧性的同时实现计算-通信重叠，兼具收敛保证与实证优势，适用于低带宽多副本的分布式语言模型训练。

Abstract: Training language models across low-bandwidth networks requires reducing synchronization overhead. Local optimization methods such as DiLoCo communicate only after several local steps, but outer gradient averaging and the outer update remain on the critical path. Existing computation-communication overlap methods avoid this stall through delayed or replica-local updates, which can degrade convergence or cause replicas to drift. We propose LACCO (Local Accumulation with Computation-Communication Overlap), a two-stage algorithm that overlaps global communication with local computation while applying globally aggregated updates shared by every replica, without introducing staleness in the outer update. We establish convergence guarantees for smooth non-convex objectives. Experiments on language-model pre-training with FineWeb show that LACCO consistently outperforms communication-overlapped baselines such as Eager and Delayed DiLoCo. These gains remain robust as model size scales up to 0.5B parameters and across a range of communication intervals and numbers of replicas up to 16 workers, while incurring only negligible-or fully overlappable-computational or memory overhead, which demonstrates the strength of LACCO.

</details>


### [157] [AscendGYM: A Cost-Aware Kernel Optimization Benchmark for Real-World Workloads on Ascend NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17621)
*AI Training Platform Team,Bingwei Zhang,Jing Li,Amir Abdullin,Sihang Chen,Xin Chen,Liangjun Feng,Yingmeng Gao,Tao Guo,Junjie Huang,Dongfang Li,Zhanglian Lin,Jiajun Liu,Zekun Liu,Zhengxuan Lu,Xiaodong Luo,Jian Meng,Ivan Nosov,Linyuan Qiu,Weijian Sun,Wanrui Yan,Binfan Zheng,Zhicheng Zhou,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang,Haizhou Li,Zhiquan Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AscendGYM 基准，用于评估智能体在真实负载配置下优化 Ascend NPU 内核的能力，并提出 STS 指标联合衡量加速比与 token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Ascend 内核基准主要聚焦内核生成，较少关注智能体在真实工作负载输入配置下优化既有实现的能力。

Method: 构建含 48 个内核、分三档难度的初始套件；每个内核配套取自真实训练/推理任务的数据类型与张量形状测试用例；支持模型评估与模型-框架（model-harness）评估两种模式，提供贴近实际流水线调用方式的完整执行环境，模型评估模式内置含搜索编排与经验积累的 harness；提出 Speedup-Token Score（STS）。

Result: AscendGYM 上的性能提升可直接映射到对应真实工作负载；STS 在可接受预算下联合考量加速比与 token 成本，可评估模型或 harness 的实际内核优化能力。

Conclusion: AscendGYM 为 Ascend NPU 内核优化提供了面向真实负载的标准化评测基准，并通过 STS 指标兼顾效果与成本，代码已开源。

Abstract: Kernel optimization is essential for efficient large language model (LLM) training and inference on Ascend NPUs. Existing Ascend kernel benchmarks primarily focus on kernel generation, with less emphasis on the ability of agents to optimize existing implementations under input configurations drawn from real workloads. To address this requirement, we introduce AscendGYM, a benchmark whose initial suite comprises 48 kernels across three difficulty levels. Each kernel is paired with test cases whose data types and tensor shapes are collected from real-world training and inference tasks. Consequently, performance improvements on AscendGYM map directly to the corresponding workloads. In terms of usability, two evaluation modes are supported in AscendGYM: model evaluation and model-harness evaluation. The kernel optimization capabilities of both trained models and custom agents are allowed to be measured. It provides a complete execution environment that closely reproduces how Ascend kernels are invoked in practical pipelines. For model evaluation, it additionally includes a built-in harness equipped with search orchestration and experience accumulation. Finally, we propose a new metric, the Speedup-Token Score (STS), which quantifies performance gains relative to token cost. By jointly considering speedup and cost, STS enables the assessment of practical kernel optimization capability of a model or harness under an acceptable budget. AscendGYM is available at https://github.com/SLAI-AITP/AscendGYM.

</details>


### [158] [An Operationalization of Opaque Serial Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17495)
*Nathan Sheffield,Alek Westover,Lukas Finnveden,Alexa Pan,Julian Stastny,Ryan Greenblatt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"NLS depth"（自然语言扎根的序列深度）这一可度量指标，用于在训练前评估模型架构中无法被CoT监控的隐性串行计算量，并发现其与层数强相关、随年份增长缓慢，而全带宽Transformer等潜在推理架构可能使其大幅增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT是目前监督AI模型的重要工具，但某些架构变化会显著削弱CoT的可监控性；需要一种能在训练前就判定、且不受常规后训练（提示、蒸馏、RL）影响的架构层面代理指标。

Method: 以"不透明串行深度"（最长不经过可解释瓶颈的计算路径）为概念基础，定义一个具体标准：自然语言扎根（NL-rooted）节点——由预训练先验初始化以产生人类可理解输出、输出空间未超出该先验、且未被修改为解释为其他数据类型的节点；由此构造NLS depth，并据此分析现有与拟议架构，给出Gemma3-1B的深度上界实例。

Result: 在开放权重模型中，NLS depth与层数强相关（存在若干例外），近年间增长缓慢；标准Transformer继续扩展下预计仍缓慢增长，而全带宽Transformer等潜在推理架构可使其大幅提升。

Conclusion: NLS depth可在训练前由架构、训练配方与部署配置裁定，建议AI开发者将其作为CoT可监控性直接测量的补充指标进行追踪与报告。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) is currently a valuable tool for overseeing AI models, but some architectural shifts could significantly reduce CoT monitorability. We operationalize a measure that serves as a proxy for the amount of unverbalized serial cognition a model can perform, as a specific instantiation of the notion of opaque serial depth: roughly, the length of the longest path through a model's computation that does not pass through an interpretable bottleneck. Our main contribution is a particular standard for what counts as such a bottleneck. We define natural-language-rooted (NL-rooted) nodes as nodes that were initialized from a pre-training prior to produce human-understandable outputs, whose output space has not been expanded beyond that prior, and whose outputs the model has not been modified to interpret as a different type of data. By construction, the resulting measure, NLS depth, can be adjudicated before training from an architecture, training recipe, and deployment configuration alone, and is insensitive to ordinary post-training such as prompting, distillation, and RL. We find that, across open-weight models, NLS depth correlates strongly with layer count, with some exceptions, and has grown slowly over recent years. It would likely grow slowly under continued scaling of standard transformers, while latent-reasoning architectures such as full-bandwidth transformers could increase it by a large amount. We work through how NLS depth applies to a range of existing and proposed architectures, discuss alternative operationalizations, and give a worked example bounding the depth of Gemma3-1B. We argue that AI developers should track and report the NLS depth of their models as a complement to direct measurements of CoT monitorability.

</details>


### [159] [Aligning AI Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17820)
*Siddarth Srinivasan,Morgan Jane Matthews,Jascha Sohl-Dickstein,Erik Jones*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究单体LLM智能体的对齐训练无法迁移到多智能体团队的机制，并提出保留对齐迁移的干预手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作显示单智能体对齐训练在多智能体设定下迁移失败，但其机制不明，且缺乏可靠补救措施，作者希望揭示失效机理并找到能恢复对齐行为的干预方案。

Method: 在两个软件工程任务（脓毒症分诊、新闻推荐）与十个咨询提案生成任务上对比单智能体与团队表现；测试多种自然干预（智能体互评、群聊协作等）；分析责任扩散机制；验证指定安全负责人智能体与协调者发布安全规范文档两类结构性干预；并开源多智能体编排与实验框架magelab。

Result: (1) 单智能体—团队安全差距与模型能力无清晰趋势，Mythos Preview在一个任务上差距最大而在另一任务上最小；(2) 互评、群聊等自然干预无法可靠恢复单智能体对齐行为；(3) 责任扩散显著解释该安全差距，团队中的智能体更少主动检查系统级安全失效、更倾向忽视、合理化或推诿责任；(4) 设置安全负责人智能体负责系统级安全、以及由协调者发布安全规范文档，可有效恢复单智能体对齐水平。

Conclusion: 多智能体对齐失效主要源于责任扩散而非模型能力不足；通过明确的安全角色与制度化安全规范可恢复对齐迁移，为实践者设计对齐的智能体团队提供可操作指导，并发布magelab框架支持后续研究。

Abstract: Previous work has shown that alignment training on individual LLM agents can fail to transfer to multi-agent settings. We investigate the underlying mechanisms for this failure, and explore interventions to preserve alignment transfer to multi-agent LLM teams. We focus on two software-engineering tasks (sepsis triage, a news recommender) and ten consulting-proposal generation tasks. We present four findings: (1) The single-vs-team safety gap does not show a clear trend with more capable models. We find that Anthropic's Mythos Preview shows the largest gap on one of our tasks but the smallest on another; (2) Many natural interventions, such as having agents critique each other's work or coordinate in a group chat, do not reliably bring agent teams back to single-agent alignment behavior; (3) diffusion of responsibility substantially accounts for the safety gap: agents in teams are less likely to proactively check for system-level safety failures and more likely to ignore, rationalize, or deflect responsibility for issues when they arise; (4) having a designated safety lead agent ensure system-level safety, and having a coordinator agent publish a safety specifications document are effective at restoring single-agent alignment. We also release magelab, a multi-agent LLM orchestration and experimentation framework. Our findings can help practitioners design agent teams that successfully preserve the behaviors expected of aligned individual agents.

</details>


### [160] [Vision-as-a-Tool: Visual Harness for Multimodal Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17797)
*Haolin Yang,Wenshan Wu,Jin Jiang,Yu Cheng,Yan Xia,Ting Song,Yangyu Huang,Zhiliang Peng,Wenhui Wang,Hangbo Bao,Yuzhe Zhang,Wen-Da Wei,Tengchao Lv,Li Dong,Lei Cui,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Vision-as-a-Tool (VaaT) 范式，将多模态大模型封装为可调用的外部视觉工具，使纯文本与多模态编码智能体均能离线/按需完成视觉推理；并实现统一框架 VHarness，在 Image-to-HTML、Image-to-SVG、Image-to-3D 任务上一致提升视觉保真度与几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体编码依赖稳健的视觉推理来解析观测与评估生成产物，但现有框架基于 Thinking-with-Images 范式，将视觉能力与智能体内在感知强耦合：性能天花板受限于自身感知能力；纯文本智能体缺乏原生视觉，无专用工具时无法进行视觉推理。

Method: 提出 VaaT 范式：区别于以往仅对图像做操作、仍由智能体自身视觉读取的视觉工具用法，VaaT 将通用多模态大模型重新定义为可调用的外部效用，可完全卸载视觉推理或按需增强原生感知。据此实现 VHarness 统一框架，由五种原子动作驱动。

Result: 在 Image-to-HTML、Image-to-SVG、Image-to-3D 三类生成任务上全面评测，VHarness 在纯文本编码智能体与多模态编码智能体上均一致提升视觉保真度与几何精度。

Conclusion: 将视觉推理模块化为可调用效用，可为未来智能体编码系统提供灵活、可扩展的基础；解耦视觉能力与智能体本体是突破感知瓶颈的有效路径。

Abstract: Multimodal agentic coding requires robust visual reasoning to analyze observations and evaluate generated artifacts. Current frameworks largely rely on a Thinking-with-Images paradigm, where agents depend on their intrinsic vision to execute visually grounded tasks. However, this architectural coupling introduces a fundamental bottleneck. Agent performance remains strictly bounded by internal perceptual limitations. Furthermore, text-only agents lack native perception. Without specialized tools, they cannot perform visual reasoning. To overcome these constraints, we introduce the Vision-as-a-Tool (VaaT) paradigm. Prior visual tool-use merely manipulates images for an agent's intrinsic vision to read. In contrast, VaaT reframes general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs) as callable external utilities. As a result, text-only agents can completely offload visual reasoning during agentic execution. Meanwhile, vision-capable agents can dynamically augment their native perception on demand. We instantiate this paradigm in VHarness, a unified framework driven by five atomic actions. We comprehensively evaluate VHarness across Image-to-HTML, Image-to-SVG, and Image-to-3D generation tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that VHarness consistently improves visual fidelity and geometric accuracy across both text-only and multimodal coding agents. These findings highlight that modularizing visual reasoning into callable utilities provides a flexible and scalable foundation for future agentic coding systems.

</details>


### [161] [Aligned, Conflicting, or Unspecified? A Diagnostic Benchmark for Multimodal Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17680)
*Xintong Wang,Haihan Nan,Ruidong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出首个按"文本—历史趋势关系"（一致/冲突/未指明）组织的真实世界诊断基准ACU-Bench，覆盖天气、网络流量与金融三类时序，共1,762个文本—窗口对；在10个预训练模型零样本评测中考察文本选择与组合（RQ1）及误差诊断与尺度化修正控制（RQ2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIoT系统越来越多地将传感时间序列与专家报告、新闻等外部文本结合，但模型如何可靠利用与历史一致或冲突的文本尚不清楚，缺乏按二者关系系统诊断的基准与误差机制分析。

Method: 构建按文本方向与历史趋势关系标注为Aligned/Conflicting/Unspecified的真实基准（Weather、Wikipedia、Finance）；对10个模型做零样本评测。RQ1在配对的Weather窗口上比较两类文本（直接预测温度变化 vs. 描述相关天气状况），并在Conflicting下仅将前者的幅度与时序信息注入后者进行组合；RQ2通过误差分解定位误差来源，并提出免训练规则限制过大的修正幅度。

Result: Aligned下直接预测型文本在多数模型上误差最低；Conflicting下其使多数模型误差高于无文本基线；组合方式相较单一文本在6个模型上降低MASE、7个模型上提升DA。误差分解将误差上升归因于修正幅度过大，限幅规则在存在该权衡的4个模型上优于有文本与无文本预测，MASE降低4.49%–21.98%，DA提升2.99%–9.71%。

Conclusion: 文本与历史的关系是决定多模态时序预测增益或损害的关键因素：冲突文本易引发过度修正，需谨慎选择与组合文本，并以幅度约束的免训练规则控制修正，从而稳定提升精度与方向准确性。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence of Things (AIoT) systems increasingly combine sensed time series with external text such as expert reports and news, yet how reliably models use text that agrees or conflicts with the sensed history remains unclear. We introduce the first real-world diagnostic benchmark organized by text-history relationship: 1,762 text-window pairs across sensor telemetry (Weather), web-service traffic (Wikipedia), and market data streams (Finance), each labeled Aligned, Conflicting, or Unspecified by whether the text's direction agrees with the historical trend, opposes it, or is not stated. Across ten pretrained models evaluated zero-shot, we investigate text selection and combination under text-history relationships (RQ1) and error diagnosis and horizon-scaled revision control (RQ2). For RQ1, we compare two types of text on paired Weather windows: one directly forecasts temperature changes, while the other describes related weather conditions, such as winds and fronts. Under Aligned, the first type yields the lowest error for most models. Under Conflicting, it increases most models' error above the no-text baseline; we therefore add only its stated magnitude and timing to the second type. Compared with either type alone, this combination lowers mean absolute scaled error (MASE) for six models and raises directional accuracy (DA) for seven. For RQ2, an error decomposition attributes the error increase to oversized revisions, and a training-free rule that limits them outperforms forecasts both with and without text for the four models with this trade-off, lowering MASE by 4.49%-21.98% and raising DA by 2.99%-9.71%. Our dataset is available at https://github.com/Shelton1202/ACU-Bench.

</details>


### [162] [FlexCast: Flexible Context Learning for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17862)
*Zuyang Shen,Luqi Gong,Fanda Fan,Bingsong Wang,Xiang Ao,Yahui Wei,Minghao Liu,Chunjie Luo,Jianfeng Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FlexCast框架，将时间序列基础模型中自回归预测解耦为"变量-上下文条件"和"预测单元分解+自回归展开"两个因素，用变长掩码预测统一训练与推理，证明灵活训练比顺序推理更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自回归下一token预测的时间序列基础模型（TSFM）混淆了多个因素：灵活的变量-上下文条件、局部预测目标、从左到右的推理过程。强自回归性能无法揭示泛化能力究竟来自哪一因素，因此需要解耦分析。

Method: 将预测重构为时间序列patch上的变长掩码预测：预训练时动态采样可见上下文与掩码目标patch，使模型接触多样化的上下文-目标配置且无需顺序解码；推理时同一预训练模型通过统一的mask-and-predict接口支持一步预测、基于置信度的迭代精化以及next-token式生成，从而在相同架构、数据和目标下可控比较不同推理调度。

Result: 在BLAST及多个零样本基准上，FlexCast精度与强TSFM基线相当或更优，同时显著降低推理延迟。消融实验表明上下文多样性和连续目标掩码是性能驱动力，而一旦学到灵活的上下文-目标关系，从左到右解码并不能带来一致增益。

Conclusion: 自回归式TSFM的关键收益在于灵活训练而非顺序推理；FlexCast以统一掩码预测框架实现了对上下文条件与推理调度的解耦分析，并提供更高效的推理路径。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) based on autoregressive (AR) next-token prediction entangle two factors: variable-context conditioning and prediction-unit factorization with autoregressive rollout. Strong AR performance therefore does not reveal whether generalization stems from the flexible training distribution, the local prediction target, the left-to-right inference process, or their combination. We propose FlexCast, a flexible context-target learning framework that disentangles these factors. FlexCast reformulates forecasting as variable-length masked prediction over time series patches: during pretraining, it dynamically samples visible context and masked target patches, exposing the model to diverse context-target configurations without sequential decoding. At inference time, the same pretrained model supports one-step forecasting, confidence-based iterative refinement, and next-token-style generation through a unified mask-and-predict interface, enabling controlled comparison of inference schedules under identical architecture, data, and objective. Evaluated on BLAST and multiple zero-shot benchmarks, FlexCast achieves accuracy competitive with or better than strong TSFM baselines while substantially reducing inference latency. Ablations confirm that context diversity and contiguous target masking drive performance, while left-to-right decoding does not yield consistent gains once flexible context-target relationships have been learned, suggesting that the key benefit of AR-style TSFMs lies in flexible training rather than sequential inference. The code is available at https://github.com/BenchCouncil/FlexCast.

</details>


### [163] [Typing Matters: Muscle, Language and Typing Behaviour in Live Keystroke Decoding from sEMG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18464)
*Abhilash Durgam,Nyle Siddiqui,Mubarak Shah,Qiushi Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于状态空间模型(SSM)的全因果、零前瞻sEMG打字解码器，融合打字解码器与自回归语言模型，并用"重分词"方法将WikiText改写为含错字/退格/换行的打字语料训练，在emg2qwerty跨用户基准上取得25.13%字符错误率(SOTA，相对提升5.4%，参数少6.7倍)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有sEMG打字研究被简化为"信号→字符"的离线推断问题并仅报告CER，忽略了真实打字受肌肉、语言和打字行为(错字、修正、退格、换行)共同约束，且需在手部持续运动时实时逐键预测。现有系统有两点不足：(1)固定窗口处理sEMG，窗口间不传递信息，丢弃会话累积的上下文；(2)缺乏对驱动打字的语言结构的理解，每个键仅凭肌肉信号推断。

Method: 1) 构建全因果、零前瞻的sEMG打字解码器，基于状态空间模型(SSM)，单一模型无需窗口切分即可解码整个会话，并以已输入内容为条件；2) 将传统打字解码器与高效自回归语言模型融合，使模型同时具备sEMG理解与语言理解；3) 提出重分词(retokenization)方法，将普通散文改写为类人打字过程(含击键、错字、退格、换行)，把WikiText转换为大规模打字语料以支持逐字符(而非整词)训练。

Result: 在emg2qwerty跨用户基准上，模型在从未见过任何sEMG→字符打字转写的情况下，取得25.13%的字符错误率(SOTA)，相对此前最佳提升5.4%，参数量减少6.7倍，且严格因果运行、不使用未来上下文。

Conclusion: 将sEMG打字重构为受语言与打字行为约束的在线因果解码问题是关键；SSM+自回归语言模型的融合与打字语料重分词可实现无需未来上下文的高效SOTA解码，并显著降低参数量。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) from the wrists can recover typed text without a keyboard. It is usually posed as a signal-inference problem: characters are read out of the muscle signal and character error rate is reported offline. We argue that this framing removes most of the problem. The decoder is constrained simultaneously by the muscles, by the language being typed, and by typing behaviour such as typos, corrections, backspaces and line breaks, and a usable system must predict keys live while the hand keeps moving. Existing systems fall short on two counts: (1) they process sEMG in fixed windows and carry nothing from one window to the next, discarding context accumulated over a session, and (2) they lack any understanding of the linguistic structure that drives typing, so every keystroke is inferred from the muscle signal alone. We propose a fully causal, zero-lookahead sEMG typing decoder built on a state space model (SSM), in which a single model decodes a whole session without windowed partitions, conditioned on what has already been typed. We achieve this by fusing a traditional typing decoder with an efficient autoregressive language model, instilling both sEMG and linguistic understanding in one model. To train such a model for per-character typing rather than whole-word text, we introduce a retokenization method that rewrites ordinary prose as human-like typing, with keystrokes, typos, backspaces and line breaks included, converting WikiText into a large typing corpus where none existed. Our model, having never seen a single sEMG-to-character transcript in typing, achieves a state-of-the-art character error rate of 25.13% on emg2qwerty's cross-user benchmark, a 5.4% relative improvement over the previous best with 6.7 times fewer parameters, while running strictly causally with no future context.

</details>


### [164] [Two-Clustering Regime of Token Dynamics in Causal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18088)
*Trinh T. Nguyen,Duy-Tung Pham,Hoang-Son Do,Tan Minh Nguyen,Thieu N. Vo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文部分解决了Karagodin等（NeurIPS 2024）关于因果注意力掩码聚类的猜想，并将其推广到时变参数情形：当值变换最大特征值λ_max>0时注意力动力学收敛到与λ_max特征向量对齐的双簇结构，而λ_max≤0时该现象一般不成立；核心贡献是揭示严格的高维→二维降维现象，并提出"流内不稳定性"分析框架以处理非单调、非自治动力学的稳态行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人工作仅刻画了经典的单簇收敛情形，而实践中更常见的双簇（聚类）机制在理论上缺乏刻画，Karagodin等的猜想未被证明；同时真实注意力动力学具有非单调、非自治（时变）特征，现有分析工具不足。

Method: 证明：在λ_max>0时严格导出双簇收敛，并给出λ_max≤0时反例说明一般不成立；识别并证明降维现象——高维嵌入空间中token轨迹坍缩到由少数主方向支配的二维结构；据此提出受物理系统失稳现象启发的"流内不稳定性"（instability within the flow）分析框架，用于分析非单调、非自治注意力动力学的稳态行为；并通过仿真与Group Query Attention（GQA）层实验验证。

Result: 确认猜想在λ_max>0下成立、在λ_max≤0下一般不成立；刻画出的双簇regime在实践中最常见，且比经典单簇情形困难得多；降维与流内不稳定性框架有效刻画了非单调非自治设定下token的稳态轨迹，仿真与GQA实验与理论一致。

Conclusion: 将因果注意力聚类的谱刻画从单簇推广到双簇并延伸至时变参数regime，提供了降维+流内不稳定性的通用理论工具箱，为理解实际Transformer注意力动力学（尤其双簇聚类机制）奠定严谨基础。

Abstract: We partially resolve Conjecture 1 of [Karagodin et al., Clustering in Causal Attention Masking (NeurIPS 2024)] and extend it to time-dependent parameter regimes. The conjecture predicts that causal attention dynamics converge to two clusters aligned with the eigenvectors associated with the largest eigenvalue $λ_{\max}$ of the value transformation. We prove that this behavior holds when $λ_{\max}>0$ and is generally false when $λ_{\max}\le0$. The resulting two-cluster regime is common in practice and substantially more challenging than the classical single-cluster setting studied previously. A central contribution of our work is the identification of a rigorous dimension reduction phenomenon: despite evolving in a high-dimensional embedding space, token trajectories collapse onto a two-dimensional structure governed by a small number of dominant directions. Building on this insight, we introduce a new analytical framework--instability within the flow--inspired by instability phenomena in physical systems. This framework enables the analysis of steady-regime behavior in non-monotone, non-autonomous attention dynamics. Our theoretical findings are illustrated through simulations and experimental studies using Group Query Attention (GQA) layers.

</details>


### [165] [Understanding the limits of snapshot-based fault diagnosis in distribution networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17998)
*Ba Tu Phung,Huy Hoang Le,Kim-Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分离了基于快照的配电网故障诊断中误差来源（分类器/特征/量测误差/故障电阻），在三个IEEE测试馈线和含分布式电源的公开数据集上验证：全相量快照在无噪声下100%正确，剩余误差主要由高阻故障的量测误差引起；三个电压传感器在1%精度等级下可达90–96%，配微PMU时达96–99%；提出判别-裕度布点法，仿真即可在1秒内选出传感器并媲美12种布点方法中的最优者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆变器型电源削弱配电网故障电流，快照式诊断（仅用少数母线单时刻电压相量分类故障）是低成本的一级筛查手段；但既有报道的准确率建立在不同的噪声假设上，无法说明误差究竟来自分类器、特征、量测误差还是故障电阻，导致该类方法可信度不明。

Method: 在三个IEEE测试馈线及一个含分布式发电的公开数据集上，采用4种特征集与13种分类器进行交叉组合，以总矢量误差（TVE）等级刻画量测误差；在无噪声条件下用全相量快照作上界分析，逐层剥离误差来源；并提出基于判别-裕度的布点（discriminability-margin placement）方法，仅依赖仿真进行传感器选址，与12种布点方法对比。

Result: 分类器并非瓶颈：无噪声时全相量快照对所有故障均正确分类；剩余误差源于高阻故障下的量测误差。三只电压传感器在1%精度等级下可识别90–96%的故障，使用配电微PMU（micro-PMU）时提升至96–99%。判别-裕度布点法在1秒内完成选点，性能达到12种布点方法中的最优水平。

Conclusion: 研究明确了快照式配电网故障诊断的适用边界与可信条件：其精度上限由量测误差与高阻故障共同决定，而非分类器能力；少量电压传感器配合合理的判别-裕度布点即可满足一级筛查需求，为实际部署提供了依据。

Abstract: Inverter-based resources weaken fault currents in distribution networks, and snapshot-based diagnosis, which classifies a fault from voltage phasors at a few buses at one instant, offers a cheap first-stage screen. Its published accuracies, however, rest on different noise assumptions and do not show whether the error comes from the classifier, the features, the measurement error, or the fault resistance. This paper separates these sources on three IEEE test feeders and a public data set with distributed generation, using four feature sets, thirteen classifiers, and measurement error defined by total vector error classes. The classifier is not the limiting factor, because the full phasor snapshot classifies every fault correctly without noise, and the remaining error comes from measurement error on high-impedance faults. Three voltage sensors classify 90-96% of faults at the 1% accuracy class and 96-99% with distribution micro phasor measurement units (micro-PMUs). A discriminability-margin placement selects them from simulations alone in under one second and matches the best of twelve placement methods. The results show when snapshot-based diagnosis can be trusted.

</details>


### [166] [OmniScore-Bench: Evaluating Vision-Language Models on Musical Score Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17870)
*Congren Dai,Zhancheng Guo,Danni Zhao,Wenchu Wang,Xiaobing Li,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 OmniScore-Bench，一个包含 3000 道题、覆盖 6 大方面 24 种子任务类型的乐谱 OMR 能力评测基准，用于细粒度诊断视觉语言模型在乐谱识别、结构恢复与音乐语义理解上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 OMR 资源只提供检测与转录指标，单一转录分数无法揭示 VLM 乐谱阅读器的能力优势与瓶颈；记谱符号识别、结构恢复与音乐含义解读需分开评估。

Method: 构建 3000 题、6 方面、24 子任务类型的基准；每题明确可见范围、参考标注与答案 schema，从而支持从符号定位到音乐一致性、证据关联问答的自动评测。评测 12 个 VLM，并做受控分析：去除乐谱图像以测视觉依赖、考察辅助已知条件与额外页上下文的角色、区分答案格式违规与音乐性错误。

Result: 12 个 VLM 呈现与操作类型相关的差异化能力画像；在片段、单页与多页任务中转录领先者各不相同。去除图像后，音高与反复结构解读暴露出明显视觉依赖，同时量化了仅凭公开上下文可保留的性能；格式/内容分析可将答案格式违规与音乐错误分离。

Conclusion: OmniScore-Bench 可诊断 VLM 哪些乐谱阅读操作可靠、哪些亟需改进，为细粒度 OMR 能力评估提供可复用框架。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can answer questions about musical scores, but their ability to recognise notation, recover its structure and interpret its musical meaning requires separate evaluation. Existing optical music recognition (OMR) resources establish detection and transcription measurements; a transcription score alone leaves the strengths and bottlenecks of a VLM score reader unresolved. We introduce OmniScore-Bench, an evaluation of OMR capabilities in VLMs through 3,000 questions spanning six aspects and 24 task subtypes. Each question specifies a visible scope, reference annotations and an answer schema, enabling automatic evaluation from symbol localisation to musical consistency and evidence-linked question answering. Our assessment of twelve VLMs reveals operation-specific capability profiles and different transcription leaders across excerpt, single-page and multipage tasks. Controlled analyses examine the roles of score images, auxiliary givens and additional page context. On the tested subset, image removal exposes visual dependence in pitch and repeat interpretation while measuring how much performance remains with public context alone. Separate format and content analyses distinguish answer-format violations from musical errors. OmniScore-Bench supports diagnosing which score-reading operations VLMs perform reliably and which require improvement.

</details>


### [167] [Can Agents Keep Up with Time? An Empirical Analysis of Temporal Adaptation After a Conversational Hiatus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17806)
*Hongguan Su,Ming Wang,Shun Zhang,Yifan Wang,Rong Yin,Zhiming Ma,Mengzheng Yang,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将“长时间会话中断后，智能体是否能感知当前时间并据此修正对已过期日程记录的建议”定义为时间状态感知与使用（temporal state perception and use），设计 session-rekindling 任务进行评测，发现模型能获取时间却常不加以使用，并据此提出基准与设计指引。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期（long-horizon）智能体的评测集中于事实保持与显式状态更新，未考察智能体在对话中断期间是否觉察时间流逝并相应调整建议；而现实场景中用户长期离线后返回，旧日程已过期且结果未记录，该能力缺失会导致错误建议。

Method: 设计 session-rekindling 任务：固定前期对话内容，仅改变后续追问的日期以及时间信息到达智能体的方式；在 5 种模型路由与 5 种时间信息条件下进行实验，考察智能体是否查询时钟、是否在推理中识别日期已过、是否据此修正建议。

Result: 智能体普遍能获取当前时间，但常常无法加以利用；它们可靠地查询时钟、甚至在推理中指出日程日期已过，却仍建议用户为已过期事件做准备。提供显式时间上下文可降低该错误，而直接给出计算好的日期关系则完全消除该错误。

Conclusion: 跟上时间流逝的关键在于将感知到的时间转化为更新后的计划。基于上述发现，作者构建了 session-rekindling 基准，为长期智能体的时间感知设计提供指导，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly serve as persistent personal assistants across modern web platforms, managing schedules, applications, and multi-step plans over weeks or months. Users often return to an ongoing conversation after a long conversational hiatus, while earlier bookings and preparation notes stay on record. During this hiatus, scheduled dates may pass even though the retained records remain unchanged and event outcomes go unrecorded. Existing evaluations of long-horizon agents focus on fact retention and explicit state updates. Whether agents perceive the passage of time and adapt their advice accordingly therefore remains unknown. We frame this ability as temporal state perception and use, which means perceiving the current time and applying it when interpreting dated records. To study it, we design session-rekindling tasks that keep the earlier conversation fixed and vary only the follow-up date and the way temporal information reaches the agent. Experiments across five model routes and five temporal-information conditions show that agents can obtain the current time but often fail to use it. They reliably query the clock and even note in their reasoning that the scheduled date has passed, yet many still tell users to prepare for the expired event. Supplying explicit temporal context reduces this error, and providing the computed date relation removes it entirely. Keeping up with time therefore depends on turning perceived time into updated plans. Based on these findings, we design a session-rekindling benchmark that provides temporal-awareness guidance for designing long-horizon agents. The code can be found at https://github.com/Adoreu2/session-rekindling-benchmark.

</details>


### [168] [MASTARS: Multi-Agent Sequential Trajectory Augmentation with Return-Conditioned Subgoals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17793)
*Jiwon Jeon,Myungsik Cho,Woojun Kim,Seongmin Kim,Woohyeon Byeon,Seungyul Han,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MASTARS：基于扩散模型的多智能体轨迹增广框架，通过"逐智能体顺序生成 + inpainting 跨智能体一致性约束"解决多样性-协调性权衡，生成多样、协调且全局一致的轨迹，在MPE、SMAC、SMAC-v2上显著提升多种离线MARL方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线MARL性能受限于固定离线数据集质量，需轨迹增广。但多智能体轨迹增广存在多样性-协调性权衡：各智能体独立生成多样性好但缺乏协调；联合生成协调但多样性差，仅复现训练分布。

Method: 提出扩散式框架MASTARS，对多智能体轨迹按智能体顺序（sequential）生成，并借助扩散模型的inpainting机制在生成过程中施加跨智能体一致的协调约束。

Result: 生成的轨迹兼具多样性、协调性与全局一致性；在MPE、SMAC、SMAC-v2等基准上，对多种离线MARL方法均带来显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 以顺序生成加inpainting约束的方式可有效突破多智能体轨迹增广中的多样性-协调性权衡，是一种通用的离线MARL数据增强方案。

Abstract: The performance of offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) is limited by the quality of the fixed offline dataset, motivating trajectory augmentation. However, augmenting multi-agent trajectories presents a fundamental challenge of the diversity-coordination trade-off: independent generation improves diversity but lacks coordination, while joint generation induces coordination but lacks diversity, reproducing the training distribution. To address this challenge in multi-agent data augmentation, we introduce MASTARS, a novel diffusion-based framework that generates multi-agent trajectories sequentially across agents while enforcing cross-agent consistent coordination via the method of inpainting. MASTARS produces trajectories that are diverse, coordinated and globally coherent. Experiments on various benchmarks such as MPE, SMAC, and SMAC-v2 demonstrate significant performance gains across multiple offline MARL methods.

</details>


### [169] [Knowledge After Collapse: Context and Wording Shape Factual Accessibility in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17763)
*Sujato Dutta,Prithviraj Patil,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Knowledge Answer Divergence (KAD) 协议，在模型首次采纳指定错误答案的精确对话历史处冻结并分支独立探针，从而区分"初始误采纳"与"后续持续、恢复与弃答"行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估只用单一"被说服率/易感性"得分衡量模型接受错误纠正的程度，无法刻画误采纳之后答案如何随对话推进而演化，也无法系统比较历史管理与干预手段的效果。

Method: KAD 将对话在首次显式采纳指定错误目标的时刻冻结，并从同一精确历史分支出相互独立的后续探针，实现匹配对照下的采纳后行为比较与历史管理干预评估。实验覆盖 8 个本地检查点条件与 6 个托管端点，并辅以经审计的 300 题重跑；另设置中性历史对照、位置匹配的线索放置对照，以及 12 个独立撰写的施压模板做措辞敏感性检验。

Result: 在 corrected Llama 3.2 Instruct 3B 对照中，中性历史分支得 42/46 正确回答，而完整历史下仅 16/46；在 Qwen 3.5 4B 中，匹配的线索放置对照使误采纳率变化 27.3 个百分点；12 个压力模板显示结果对措辞高度敏感。

Conclusion: KAD 能把事实可靠性评估扩展到初始易感性之外，支持对错误答案持续性、恢复条件与对话干预进行系统、可匹配的比较，并揭示结果对历史构成与措辞的强敏感性。

Abstract: When a language model accepts a false correction in a factual discussion, its next answer can depend on how the conversation continues. We introduce Knowledge Answer Divergence (KAD), an event-conditioned evaluation protocol that distinguishes initial false-answer adoption from subsequent persistence, recovery, and abstention. KAD freezes the conversation at the first explicit adoption of a designated false target and branches independent probes from that exact history. This enables matched comparisons of post-adoption behavior and history-management interventions that a single susceptibility score cannot capture. We evaluate eight local checkpoint conditions and six hosted endpoints, alongside an audited 300-question rerun. In corrected Llama 3.2 Instruct 3B controls, a neutral-history branch yields 42/46 correct responses, compared with 16/46 under full history. A matched cue-placement control changes false adoption by 27.3 percentage points in Qwen 3.5 4B. Twelve independently authored pressure templates demonstrate substantial sensitivity to wording. Together, these findings demonstrate the utility of KAD for evaluating factual reliability beyond initial susceptibility, enabling systematic comparisons of false-answer persistence, recovery conditions and conversational interventions.

</details>


### [170] [A Spectral Framework for Universal Proxy Regularization in Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17728)
*Shangge Liu,Danyue Qian,Qi Fan,Yinghuan Shi,Wenbin Li,Lei Wang,Yang Gao,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“通用代理正则化谱框架”，用谱覆盖度α与泄漏度β刻画代理分布与待合并任务分布的关系，推导出统一的参数干扰上界，并比较三种代理正则化实现（J/F/O）在非线性微调下的偏差层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法为缓解参数干扰，常需借助其他待合并任务的数据约束微调，违背了模型合并“任务独立、数据隐私”的核心前提。已有工作初步显示任务无关的通用代理分布可替代这些数据，但缺乏何时、为何有效的理论解释。

Method: 提出谱框架，通过谱覆盖度α与泄漏度β刻画代理分布与其他任务分布的关系，并据此推导统一的参数干扰上界；在训练目标中加入代理正则项，研究三种实现：J（基于Jacobian-向量积的精确评估）、F（基于Fisher信息矩阵的冻结曲率近似）、O（直接约束模型在代理上功能偏移的输出漂移形式）；在NTK线性化下证明三者一致，并进一步刻画标准非线性微调下各自偏离线性参考界的层级（J为零、F与代理无关、O随代理质量缩放）。

Result: 证明更小的(α, β)可通过更好的谱对齐可证地降低参数干扰；三种实现在NTK线性化下重合，在非线性微调下形成J=0、F代理无关、O随代理质量缩放的三级层级；在CLIP视觉-语言模型上的实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 通用、任务无关的代理分布在满足谱覆盖/泄漏条件时可替代其他任务数据实现有效正则化；输出漂移形式(O)在非线性微调下最受益于高质量代理，为隐私友好的模型合并提供理论与实践依据。

Abstract: Model merging constructs multi-task models without expensive joint training but often suffers from parameter interference. Some solutions mitigate this by constraining finetuning using data from the other tasks to be merged, violating the core independence and privacy assumption of model merging. Recent work offers preliminary empirical evidence that a universal, task-agnostic proxy distribution can substitute for such data, but leaves open when and why it suffices. To this end, we propose a \textit{Spectral Framework for Universal Proxy Regularization}, which characterizes the relationship between a proxy distribution and the distribution of other tasks to be merged through spectral coverage ($α$) and leakage ($β$). Based on the framework, we derive a unified parameter interference bound: smaller $(α, β)$ provably yields lower parameter interference through better spectral alignment. To exploit such a proxy, we augment the training objective with a proxy regularizer. Evaluating this regularizer during fine-tuning requires a concrete mechanism. We study three instantiations, including (J) an exact evaluation via Jacobian-vector products, (F) a frozen curvature approximation using the Fisher information matrix, and (O) an output-drift formulation that constrains the model's functional deviation on the proxy directly. We prove that all three coincide under the NTK linearization, where existing analyses of such regularizers stop, and go further to characterize standard non-linear fine-tuning, bounding how far each instantiation departs from its linearized reference and obtain a three-way hierarchy, zero for (J), proxy-independent for (F), and proxy-quality-scaling for (O). Empirical validation on the commonly used vision-language model CLIP confirms these theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [171] [Federated Soft Poly-Level Learning Over a Distributed Network with Guaranteed Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17722)
*Yang Jiao,Kai Yang,Jianwei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出联邦多层级学习框架FPLL，用新型L-cut松弛求解四层及以上多层级优化问题，并给出迭代复杂度与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多层级优化研究多集中于双层与三层问题，涉及四层及以上的"多层级学习"(PLL)问题鲜有探索；且现有PLL研究多为非联邦设定，需将数据集中至中心服务器，存在隐私风险。

Method: 构建面向分布式网络的联邦多层级学习(FPLL)框架，处理带软约束的PLL问题；核心是提出一种新型非线性割——L-cut，用于对PLL问题逐层松弛；联邦算法引入可调参数，以在计算复杂度与性能效率之间灵活权衡。

Result: 通过非渐近分析推导出所提FPLL达到ε-帕累托稳定点的迭代复杂度；大量实验表明该方法具有更优的有效性。

Conclusion: FPLL将多层级优化拓展至联邦设定，兼顾隐私保护与可调效率，为四层及以上层级优化问题提供了理论可证的求解方案。

Abstract: Multi-level learning/optimization, e.g., bilevel optimization, has been recognized for its versatile applicability across a broad array of domains, spanning from signal processing to machine learning. However, most existing works focus on bilevel and trilevel optimization. In contrast, multi-level optimization problems involving four or more hierarchical levels, which we term poly-level learning (PLL) problems, remain largely unexplored.In addition, in practical PLL, data may be distributed across multiple nodes over a distributed network. Nevertheless, extant studies on PLL predominantly focus on the non-federated setting, necessitating the collection of data to central servers and thereby posing potential privacy risks. To this end, we propose a Federated Poly-Level Learning (FPLL) framework over a distributed network to tackle the PLL problems with soft constraints in a federated manner. FPLL hinges on a novel non-linear cut, i.e., $L$-cut, for the level-wise relaxation to the PLL problems. Additionally, the proposed federated algorithm is designed with adjustable parameters that allow for a flexible trade-off between its computational complexity and performance efficiency, enabling users to tune it according to specific application requirements. We also carry out non-asymptotic analysis to derive the iteration complexity for the proposed FPLL to achieve the $ε$-Pareto stationary point. Extensive experiments have been undertaken to elucidate the superior efficacy of the proposed method.

</details>


### [172] [Cross-Linguistic Acoustic Biomarkers of Cognitive Impairment Across Interactive and Single-Speaker Speech Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17710)
*Kenyu Ikeuchi,Tomoaki Ohtsuki,Michelle Cohn,Alyssa Weakley,Yui Ishihara,Joseph Hoang,Taishiro Kishimoto,Sarah Tomaszewski Farias,Jet M. J. Vonk,Chen-Nee Chuah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 跨语言、跨任务的声学分析表明，步态/时序类声学特征跨任务语言稳健，语速特征强依赖任务，音高特征呈语言特异性，提示不宜盲目合并异质语音数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言痴呆筛查需可扩展、公平的方案，自动语音分析颇具潜力；但现有研究多依赖单一任务（如图片描述）、黑箱模型，忽视任务与语言的作用。

Method: 整合会话式数据集与结构化语音任务，覆盖西班牙语、汉语、英语、日语四种语言；采用统计、语言学家/临床医生引导及基于机器学习模型的特征选择，得到紧凑声学特征集并保持分类性能，从而跨数据集与任务系统比较与认知障碍相关的特征。

Result: 声学特征并未在任务与语言间一致泛化：时序特征在所有数据集上稳健，反映时间紊乱；语速强任务依赖，仅在内容与节奏外部锚定的单说话人任务中有信息量，内容高度个性化时可靠性下降；音高特征呈语言特异模式，声调语言（如汉语）偏好 chroma 类特征，非声调语言偏好 F0 类特征。

Conclusion: 应警惕对异质语音数据的无差别合并，强调面向多语言痴呆检测的可解释、情境感知的自动语音分析方法。

Abstract: Multilingual dementia detection is essential for scalable and equitable screening. In recent years, automated speech analysis has emerged as a promising approach for supporting dementia detection by enabling non-invasive and scalable assessment of cognitive function. However, existing studies that use automated speech analysis often rely on one type of task, such as picture description, and use black-box models. These studies often neglect the role of task and language. To address these gaps, we performed a cross-linguistic and cross-task acoustic analysis that integrates a conversational dataset with structured speech tasks across four languages (Spanish, Mandarin, English, Japanese). We applied statistical, linguist-/clinician-guided, and machine learning model-based feature selection to derive compact acoustic feature sets while maintaining classification performance. This enabled systematic comparison of cognitive impairment-relevant features across datasets and tasks. Our results revealed that acoustic features did not generalize uniformly across tasks and languages. Temporal features remained robust across all datasets, consistently capturing timing disruption regardless of language or task. In contrast, speech rate showed strong task dependence -- emerging as informative mainly in single-speaker tasks with externally anchored content and pacing, but becoming less reliable when speech content was highly individualized. Pitch-related features further exhibited language-specific patterns, favoring chroma-related features in tonal languages (e.g., Mandarin) and F0-related features in non-tonal languages. These findings caution against indiscriminate pooling of heterogeneous speech data and underscore the need for interpretable, context-aware approaches to multilingual dementia detection using automated speech analysis.

</details>


### [173] [Learn then Bet: Learning Interpretable Rules for Reliable Agent Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17662)
*You-Lin Chen,Xinyi Lisa Chen,Pedro Herrero-Vidal,Xiaojing Yu,Bingqing Song,Bin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LeBet统计学习框架，学习可解释的OR-of-AND运行时监控器并通过固定投注统计量累积证据，在有限类内可交换性与留一稳定性下给出有限样本的有效性与检测力保证，在SWE-bench Lite上将token消耗降低34.46%并把输出精度从18.33%提升至21.59%（假阳性率13.64%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体常运行数百步且缺乏监督，实践中依赖轮次上限、关键词过滤等启发式监控器来决定何时停止、升级或介入，但这些启发式会误停本可成功的运行或放任失败运行继续，缺乏统计保证。

Method: LeBet（Learn then Bet）：从有效且高检测力介入的充分条件推导学习目标；学习可解释的OR-of-AND规则监控器，并以固定投注统计量累积证据、达到预设阈值即介入；在有限类内可交换性与留一稳定性假设下，用同一批轨迹完成学习与校准（不要求i.i.d.）推导训练条件下的有限样本假阳性控制与检测力保证；针对学习中的组合优化难题，采用利用布尔结构提出规则编辑的随机局部搜索来提升经验可分性。

Result: 在SWE-bench Lite上用Ministral 3 8B，学到的监控器节省34.46%的输入+输出token，输出精度由18.33%升至21.59%，经验假阳性率13.64%。

Conclusion: 该工作示范了如何在可解释规则集上形式化学习问题并给出统计条件，基于部分历史建立训练条件下的可靠介入保证，优于缺乏保证的启发式监控器。

Abstract: Autonomous agents now run for hundreds of steps with little or no human supervision. In practice, practitioners use runtime monitors such as turn limits and keyword filters to decide when a run should be stopped, upgraded, or assisted. However, these heuristics may stop runs that would otherwise succeed or allow failing runs to continue. To address this limitation, we introduce LeBet (Learn then Bet), a statistical learning framework that derives its learning objective from sufficient conditions for valid and powerful intervention.
